    var YEAR_TIERS = {
        '百年': {min:100, max:990, step:10, marks:['100','300','500','700','990'], format:function(v){
            if(v%100===0)return (v/100)+'百年';
            return v+'年';
        }},
        '千年': {min:1000, max:9900, step:100, marks:['1千','3千','5千','7千','9900'], format:function(v){
            if(v%1000===0)return (v/1000)+'千年';
            var qian=Math.floor(v/1000);
            var bai=Math.floor((v%1000)/100);
            var res=qian+'千';
            if(bai>0)res+=bai+'百';
            return res+'年';
        }},
        '万年': {min:10000, max:1000000, step:1000, marks:['1万','10万','50万','100万'], format:function(v){
            if(v>=1000000)return (v/10000)+'万年';
            if(v>=10000){
                var wan=Math.floor(v/10000);
                var qian2=Math.floor((v%10000)/1000);
                var res=wan+'万';
                if(qian2>0)res+=qian2+'千';
                return res+'年';
            }
            return v+'年';
        }}
    };

    function fuzzyMatchBone(text) { text = text.trim().replace(/[：:]/g, ''); for (var key in BONE_ALIAS) { for (var i = 0; i < BONE_ALIAS[key].length; i++) { if (text.indexOf(BONE_ALIAS[key][i]) >= 0) return key; } } return null; }

    function getWorldbookName(){try{var cfg=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(API_KEY)||'{}');return cfg.worldbookName||'斗罗大陆3.0';}catch(e){return '斗罗大陆3.0';}}
    function getApi(name){try{if(typeof window[name]==='function')return window[name];}catch(e){}return null;}
    function escHtml(s){var d=document.createElement('div');d.textContent=String(s);return d.innerHTML;}
    function fmt(v){return v%1===0?v.toString():v.toFixed(1);}
    function toast(id,msg,type){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;el.textContent=msg;el.className='toast '+type;}
    function clearToasts(){document.querySelectorAll('.toast').forEach(function(t){t.className='toast'});}
    function addTag(text,targetId){var el=document.getElementById(targetId);var cur=el.value.trim();if(!cur){el.value=text;return;}var tags=cur.split(/[、,，]/);for(var i=0;i<tags.length;i++){if(tags[i].trim()===text)return;}el.value=cur+'、'+text;}
    function adjNum(id,d,min,max){var el=document.getElementById(id);if(el.readOnly)return;var v=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Math.round(((parseFloat(el.value)||0)+d)*100)/100));el.value=fmt(v);if(id==='f-level')onLevelChange();}
    function ringCount(){var lv=Math.floor(parseFloat(document.getElementById('f-level').value)||0);if(lv>=100)return 9;if(lv<11)return 0;return Math.min(Math.floor((lv-1)/10),9);}
    /* v3.1修改：rankName 100级以上细分 */
    function rankName(lv){if(lv<=10)return'魂士';if(lv<=20)return'魂师';if(lv<=30)return'大魂师';if(lv<=40)return'魂尊';if(lv<=50)return'魂宗';if(lv<=60)return'魂王';if(lv<=70)return'魂帝';if(lv<=80)return'魂圣';if(lv<=90)return'魂斗罗';if(lv<=94)return'封号斗罗';if(lv<=98)return'超级斗罗';if(lv===99)return'极限斗罗';if(lv>=160)return'至高神';if(lv>=140)return'神王';if(lv>=130)return'一级神';if(lv>=120)return'超神';if(lv>=110)return'真神';if(lv>=100)return'神官';return'神';}
    function isDoubleSkillRing(color){return color==='红'||color==='橙金';}
    function isQuadSkillRing(color){return color==='金';}
    function isSeventh(idx){return idx===6;}
    function isGodLevel(){return parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value||0)>=100;}
    /* v3.1新增：获取当前等级对应的神位等级名称 */
    function getGodLevelName(lv){for(var i=GOD_LEVELS.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(lv>=GOD_LEVELS[i].minLv)return GOD_LEVELS[i].name;}return '';}
    function parseYears(str){str=(str||'').trim().replace(/,/g,'');var numMatch=str.match(/(\d+)/);if(numMatch){var n=parseInt(numMatch[1]);if(str.indexOf('百万')>=0)return n*1000000;if(str.indexOf('万')>=0)return n*10000;if(str.indexOf('千')>=0)return n*1000;if(str.indexOf('百')>=0)return n*100;return n;}return 0;}

    function cleanAiOutput(text){
        if(!text)return '';
        text=text.replace(/^[\s\S]*?(好的|以下|根据|当然|没问题|Sure|Here)[^\n]*\n/i,'');
        text=text.replace(/\n(以上|希望|注意|如果|请注意)[^\n]*$/i,'');
        return text.trim();
    }
    function extractField(text, fieldName){
        var variants = [fieldName];
        var aliasMap = {
            '魂技壹':['魂技壹','魂技一','魂技1','技能壹','技能一'],
            '魂技贰':['魂技贰','魂技二','魂技2','技能贰','技能二'],

            '魂技肆':['魂技肆','魂技四','魂技4','技能肆','技能四'],
            '魂技名称':['魂技名称','魂技名','技能名称','名称'],
            '详情':['详情','效果','描述','说明'],
            '效果':['效果','详情','描述','说明'],
            '名称':['名称','魂骨名称','骨名'],
            '魂兽':['魂兽','来源魂兽','来源','魂兽名称'],
            '年限':['年限','修为年限','修为'],
            '融合技名称':['融合技名称','融合技'],
            '融合技效果':['融合技效果','效果'],
            '我方武魂':['我方武魂','我的武魂'],
            '对方武魂':['对方武魂','对方的武魂'],
            '武魂壹':['武魂壹','武魂一','武魂1'],
            '武魂贰':['武魂贰','武魂二','武魂2'],
            '神位名称':['神位名称','神位名'],
            '神装描述':['神装描述','神装'],
            '联邦币':['联邦币','FC'],
            '贡献点':['贡献点'],
            '银联币':['银联币','SLC'],
            '外观':['外观','外观描述'],
            '能力':['能力','能力描述','战斗能力'],
            '魂灵名称':['魂灵名称','魂灵名'],
            '魂灵类型':['魂灵类型','类型'],
            '魂灵年限':['魂灵年限','年限'],
            '来源种族':['来源种族','来源魂兽','种族'],
            '斗铠名称':['斗铠名称','名称'],
            '斗铠外观':['斗铠外观','外观'],
            '机甲名称':['机甲名称','名称'],
            '机甲等级':['机甲等级','等级'],
            '机甲描述':['机甲描述','描述'],
            '神位类型':['神位类型','类型'],
            '神位描述':['神位描述','描述'],
            '神技名称':['神技名称','神技名'],
            '神技效果':['神技效果','效果'],
            '神器名称':['神器名称','神器名'],
            '神器等级':['神器等级','等级'],
            '神器描述':['神器描述','描述']
        };
        if(aliasMap[fieldName])variants=aliasMap[fieldName];
        for(var i=0;i<variants.length;i++){
            var re=new RegExp(variants[i]+'[：:]\\s*(.+)');
            var m=text.match(re);
            if(m)return m[1].trim();
        }
        return '';
    }
    function splitSections(text){
        var sections=text.split(/---+/);
        if(sections.length>1)return sections.filter(function(s){return s.trim();});
        sections=text.split(/===+/);
        if(sections.length>1)return sections.filter(function(s){return s.trim();});
        sections=text.split(/\n\s*\n\s*\n/);
        if(sections.length>1)return sections.filter(function(s){return s.trim();});
        return [text];
    }

        el.parentNode.querySelectorAll('.ai-count-opt').forEach(function(o){o.classList.remove('active');});

    }

    function switchSubTab(btn, tabGroupId, paneId){
        document.querySelectorAll('#'+tabGroupId+' .sub-tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
        btn.classList.add('active');
        var panesContainer = document.getElementById(tabGroupId+'-panes');
        if(panesContainer){
            panesContainer.querySelectorAll('.sub-tab-pane').forEach(function(p){p.classList.remove('active');});
        }
        var target = document.getElementById(paneId);
        if(target) target.classList.add('active');
    }

    function switchInvTab(btn, paneId){
        document.querySelectorAll('#invTabs .inv-tab').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
        btn.classList.add('active');
        document.querySelectorAll('.inv-tab-pane').forEach(function(p){p.classList.remove('active');});
        var target = document.getElementById(paneId);
        if(target) target.classList.add('active');
    }

    function updateModeIndicator(){
        var area = document.getElementById('modeIndicatorArea');
        if(!area) return;
        if(createMode === 'npc'){
            area.innerHTML = '<div class="mode-indicator npc-mode">👤 NPC创建模式</div>';
        } else if(createMode === 'world'){
            area.innerHTML = '<div class="mode-indicator world-mode">🌍 世界观创建模式</div>';
        } else {
            area.innerHTML = '<div class="mode-indicator user-mode">⚡ 主角创建模式</div>';
        }
    }

    /* ===== 自定义标签输入函数 ===== */
    function addCustomTagToField(inputEl, fieldId){
        var text = inputEl.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        addTag(text, fieldId);
        inputEl.value = '';
    }

    function addCustomSingleTag(inputEl, groupId){
        if(typeof inputEl === 'string') inputEl = document.getElementById(inputEl);
        var text = inputEl.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var container = document.getElementById(groupId);
        if(!container) return;
        var exists = false;
        container.querySelectorAll('.tag-chip').forEach(function(t){
            if(t.textContent.trim() === text) exists = true;
        });
        if(exists){
            container.querySelectorAll('.tag-chip').forEach(function(t){
                t.classList.toggle('active', t.textContent.trim() === text);
            });
        } else {
            container.querySelectorAll('.tag-chip').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
            var chip = document.createElement('span');
            chip.className = 'tag-chip active';
            chip.textContent = text;
            chip.onclick = function(){toggleSingleTag(this, groupId);};
            container.appendChild(chip);
        }
        inputEl.value = '';
    }

    function addCustomMultiTag(inputEl, groupId){
        if(typeof inputEl === 'string') inputEl = document.getElementById(inputEl);
        var text = inputEl.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var container = document.getElementById(groupId);
        if(!container) return;
        var exists = false;
        container.querySelectorAll('.tag-chip').forEach(function(t){
            if(t.textContent.trim() === text){ exists = true; t.classList.add('active'); }
        });
        if(!exists){
            var chip = document.createElement('span');
            chip.className = 'tag-chip active';
            chip.textContent = text;
            chip.onclick = function(){this.classList.toggle('active');};
            container.appendChild(chip);
        }
        inputEl.value = '';
    }

    function addCustomAttrTag(inputEl, containerId){
        if(typeof inputEl === 'string') inputEl = document.getElementById(inputEl);
        var text = inputEl.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var container = document.getElementById(containerId);
        if(!container) return;
        var exists = false;
        container.querySelectorAll('.attr-tag').forEach(function(t){
            if(t.getAttribute('data-attr') === text){ exists = true; t.classList.add('active'); }
        });
        if(!exists){
            var chip = document.createElement('span');
            chip.className = 'attr-tag active';
            chip.setAttribute('data-attr', text);
            chip.textContent = text;
            chip.onclick = function(){this.classList.toggle('active');};
            container.appendChild(chip);
        }
        inputEl.value = '';
    }

    function addCustomPrefMultiTag(inputEl, groupId){
        if(typeof inputEl === 'string') inputEl = document.getElementById(inputEl);
        var text = inputEl.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var container = document.getElementById(groupId);
        if(!container) return;
        var exists = false;
        container.querySelectorAll('.pref-chip').forEach(function(t){
            if(t.textContent.trim() === text){ exists = true; t.classList.add('active'); }
        });
        if(!exists){
            var chip = document.createElement('span');
            chip.className = 'pref-chip active';
            chip.textContent = text;
            chip.onclick = function(){this.classList.toggle('active');};
            container.appendChild(chip);
        }
        inputEl.value = '';
    }

    function setCustomAffiliation(){
        var input = document.getElementById('customAffiliationInput');
        var text = input.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var select = document.getElementById('f-affiliation-faction');
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = '[自定义]' + text;
        opt.textContent = '★ ' + text;
        opt.selected = true;
        var customGroup = document.getElementById('affiliationCustomGroup');
        if(customGroup) customGroup.appendChild(opt);
        else select.appendChild(opt);
        input.value = '';
    }

    function setCustomStation(){
        var input = document.getElementById('customStationInput');
        var text = input.value.trim();
        if(!text) return;
        var select = document.getElementById('f-station-location');
        var opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = '[自定义]' + text;
        opt.textContent = '★ ' + text;
        opt.selected = true;
        var customGroup = document.getElementById('stationCustomGroup');
        if(customGroup) customGroup.appendChild(opt);
        else select.appendChild(opt);
        input.value = '';
    }

    /* ===== v3.1：神位系统函数（嵌入awaken面板） ===== */

    /* 更新神位等级显示（根据魂力等级自动映射） */
    function updateGodFoldContainer(){
        var container = document.getElementById('godFoldContainer');
        if(!container) return;
        var lv = parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value) || 0;
        var show = (lv >= 100) && needsSoulSteps();
        container.classList.toggle('show', show);
        if(show){
            var godLevelName = getGodLevelName(lv);
            var autoLevelEl = document.getElementById('godAutoLevel');
            if(autoLevelEl) autoLevelEl.textContent = godLevelName;
            var displayEl = document.getElementById('godAutoLevelDisplay');
            if(displayEl) displayEl.textContent = godLevelName;
            var rangeEl = document.getElementById('godAutoLevelRange');
            if(rangeEl){
                var gl = GOD_LEVELS.find(function(g){return lv>=g.minLv&&lv<=g.maxLv;});
                rangeEl.textContent = gl ? (gl.minLv + '~' + gl.maxLv + '级') : '';
            }
        }
    }

    function addGodPosition(){
        godPositionCounter++;
        var uid = 'gp_' + godPositionCounter;
        var container = document.getElementById('godPositionList');
        var h = '<div class="godpos-card" data-gp-uid="' + uid + '">';

        h += '<div class="godpos-hd"><span class="godpos-idx">' + godPositionCounter + '</span><span class="godpos-label">神位 ' + godPositionCounter + '</span></div>';

        h += '<div id="godSkills_' + uid + '"></div>';
        h += '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:6px">';
        h += '<button class="custom-skill-add-btn" onclick="addGodSkill(\'' + uid + '\')">+ 添加神技</button>';
        h += '<button class="ai-btn" onclick="aiGenGodSkills(\'' + uid + '\',this)" style="padding:4px 10px;font-size:10px"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" style="width:10px;height:10px"><path d="M12 3l1.5 5.5L19 10l-5.5 1.5L12 17l-1.5-5.5L5 10l5.5-1.5L12 3z"/></svg>AI生成</button>';
        h += '<div class="ai-count-select" id="aiCountGS_' + uid + '"><span class="ai-count-opt active" data-n="1" onclick="setAiCount(this)">1</span><span class="ai-count-opt" data-n="2" onclick="setAiCount(this)">2</span><span class="ai-count-opt" data-n="3" onclick="setAiCount(this)">3</span></div>';

    function removeGodPosition(btn){
        btn.closest('.godpos-card').remove();
    }

    }

    /* ===== 斗铠函数（v3.1简化） ===== */
    function selectDoukaiLevel(el, key){
        document.querySelectorAll('#doukaiLevelTags .tag-chip').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});
        el.classList.add('active');
        var nameArea = document.getElementById('doukaiNameArea');
        var hint = document.getElementById('doukaiNameHint');
        if(key === 'none'){
            if(nameArea) nameArea.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
            if(nameArea) nameArea.style.display = '';
            var lv = parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value) || 0;
            var limit = DOUKAI_LIMITS[key];
            if(limit && lv < limit.minLevel){
                if(hint) hint.textContent = '⚠ 需要' + limit.minLevel + '级以上才能装备' + limit.name + '，当前' + lv + '级';
                if(hint) hint.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
            } else {
                if(hint) hint.textContent = '名称字数应为' + (limit?limit.wordCount:'?') + '字';
                if(hint) hint.style.color = 'var(--text-3)';
            }
        }
    }

    /* ===== 机甲函数（v3.1重设计：等级标签美化+描述+批量） ===== */
    function addMechaItem(){
        mechaCounter++;
        var uid = 'mecha_' + mechaCounter;
        var container = document.getElementById('mechaList');
        var h = '<div class="mecha-item" data-mecha-uid="' + uid + '">';
        h += '<input type="checkbox" class="item-check" onchange="updateDeleteBar(\'mechaList\')">';
        h += '<div class="uni-row-2">';
        h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">机甲名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-mecha="name" placeholder="如：黑龙号"></div>';
        h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">等级</div><div class="mecha-level-tags" id="' + uid + '_level">';
        MECHA_LEVELS.forEach(function(ml){
            h += '<span class="mecha-level-chip" onclick="toggleSingleMechaLevel(this,\'' + uid + '_level\')">' + ml + '</span>';
        });
        h += '</div></div>';
        h += '</div>';
        h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">描述（外观+特点）</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-mecha="desc" placeholder="机甲的外观和特点描述..." style="min-height:40px"></textarea></div></div>';
        h += '</div>';
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);
        updateDeleteBar('mechaList');
    }

    function toggleSingleMechaLevel(el, groupId){
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + groupId + ' .mecha-level-chip').forEach(function(c){c.classList.remove('active');});
        el.classList.add('active');
    }

    /* ===== 副职业函数 ===== */
    function buildVocLevelTags(){
        ['vocForgeTags','vocDesignTags','vocMakeTags','vocRepairTags'].forEach(function(containerId){
            var container = document.getElementById(containerId);
            if(!container) return;
            var h = '<span class="voc-level-chip" onclick="selectVocLevel(this,\'' + containerId + '\',0)">无</span>';
            for(var i = 1; i <= 9; i++){
                h += '<span class="voc-level-chip" onclick="selectVocLevel(this,\'' + containerId + '\',' + i + ')">' + i + '</span>';
            }
            container.innerHTML = h;
        });
    }

    function selectVocLevel(el, groupId, level){
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + groupId + ' .voc-level-chip').forEach(function(c){c.classList.remove('active');});
        el.classList.add('active');
    }

    function getVocLevel(groupId){
        var active = document.querySelector('#' + groupId + ' .voc-level-chip.active');
        if(!active) return 0;
        var text = active.textContent.trim();
        return text === '无' ? 0 : parseInt(text) || 0;
    }

    /* ===== 精神力面板函数 ===== */
    function buildSpiritLevelTags(){
        var container = document.getElementById('spiritLevelTags');
        if(!container) return;
        var h = '';
        SPIRIT_LEVELS.forEach(function(sl){
            h += '<span class="spirit-level-chip' + (selectedSpiritLevel === sl.key ? ' active' : '') + '" data-slkey="' + sl.key + '" onclick="selectSpiritLevel(this,\'' + sl.key + '\')">' + sl.name + '<span style="font-size:9px;color:inherit;opacity:.6;margin-left:3px">(' + sl.min + '-' + (sl.max >= 999999 ? '∞' : sl.max) + ')</span></span>';
        });
        container.innerHTML = h;
        updateSpiritValRange();
    }

    function selectSpiritLevel(el, key){
        document.querySelectorAll('#spiritLevelTags .spirit-level-chip').forEach(function(c){c.classList.remove('active');});
        el.classList.add('active');
        selectedSpiritLevel = key;
        var sl = SPIRIT_LEVELS.find(function(s){return s.key === key;});
        if(sl){
            var valEl = document.getElementById('f-spirit-val');
            var curVal = parseInt(valEl.value) || 0;
            if(curVal < sl.min || curVal > sl.max) valEl.value = sl.min;
        }
        updateSpiritValRange();
        updateSpiritCapacityInfo();
    }

    function updateSpiritValRange(){
        var rangeEl = document.getElementById('spiritValRange');
        if(!rangeEl) return;
        var sl = SPIRIT_LEVELS.find(function(s){return s.key === selectedSpiritLevel;});
        if(sl){
            rangeEl.textContent = '范围：' + sl.min + ' - ' + (sl.max >= 999999 ? '∞' : sl.max);
        } else {
            rangeEl.textContent = '';
        }
    }

    function updateSpiritCapacityInfo(){
        var el = document.getElementById('spiritCapacityInfo');
        if(!el) return;
        var sl = SPIRIT_LEVELS.find(function(s){return s.key === selectedSpiritLevel;});
        if(sl){
            el.textContent = sl.name + '：' + sl.capacity;
        } else {
            el.textContent = '请先选择精神力境界';
        }
    }

    /* ===== 年限→颜色映射 ===== */
    function yearsToColor(yearsStr){
        var y = parseYears(yearsStr);
        if(y >= 1000000) return '金';
        if(y >= 200000) return '橙金';
        if(y >= 100000) return '红';
        if(y >= 10000) return '黑';
        if(y >= 1000) return '紫';
        if(y >= 100) return '黄';
        if(y >= 1) return '白';
        return '无';
    }

    function getRingBorderColor(c){var m={'白':'#c0c0c8','黄':'#e6a800','紫':'#af52de','黑':'#2c2c2e','红':'#ff3b30','橙金':'#e8820c','金':'#daa520'};return m[c]||'var(--text-3)';}
    function getRingGlowColor(c){var m={'白':'rgba(192,192,200,.3)','黄':'rgba(230,168,0,.35)','紫':'rgba(175,82,222,.4)','黑':'rgba(0,0,0,.25)','红':'rgba(255,59,48,.45)','橙金':'rgba(232,130,12,.5)','金':'rgba(218,165,32,.5)'};return m[c]||'transparent';}

    /* ===== 步骤流 ===== */
    function needsSoulSteps(){if(selectedRace==='人类')return true;if(selectedRace==='魂兽'&&beastFormState==='彻底化形')return true;return false;}

    /* v3.1修改：移除独立godhood步骤，神位嵌入awaken */
    function getActiveSteps(){
        if(createMode === 'world'){
            return ['home','world-type','world-basic','world-detail','world-relations','world-preview','world-save'];
        }
        var steps=['home','era','basic','appear'];
        if(needsSoulSteps()){
            steps.push('awaken','soulinfo');
            if(selectedEra === '斗三'){
                steps.push('spirits');
            }
            steps.push('rings','bones');
        }
        if(selectedEra === '斗三' && needsSoulSteps()){
            steps.push('equip');
        }
        /* v3.1：不再有独立godhood步骤 */
        if(createMode === 'user'){
            steps.push('arrival','identity','inventory','preview','intro');
        } else {
            steps.push('identity','inventory','preview','npc-save');
        }
        return steps;
    }
    function refreshSteps(){activeSteps=getActiveSteps();}
    function curStepIndex(){return activeSteps.indexOf(curPaneId);}

    function onGenderChange(){buildGenderTags();buildNsfwBodyFields();updateHumanFormTags();buildNsfwBeastHumanFields();buildOutfitTags();buildNsfwOutfitFields();buildInvOutfitSelectTags();buildNsfwPersonalityTags();buildSpeechTags();}
    function onBeastSexChange(){updateHumanFormTags();buildNsfwBeastHumanFields();buildBeastOutfitTags();buildNsfwBeastOutfitFields();buildBeastPersonalityContent();buildBeastNsfwPersonalityTags();}

    function buildGenderTags(){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var f=isFemaleGender(g);buildTagGroup('bodyTypeTags',f?BODY_TYPES_F:BODY_TYPES_M,true);buildTagGroup('hairStyleTags',f?HAIR_STYLES_F:HAIR_STYLES_M,true);buildTagGroup('faceShapeTags',f?FACE_SHAPE_F:FACE_SHAPE_M,true);buildTagGroup('faceAuraTags',f?FACE_AURA_F:FACE_AURA_M,false);}
    function buildTagGroup(containerId,items,isSingle){var c=document.getElementById(containerId);if(!c)return;var h='';items.forEach(function(item){if(isSingle){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+containerId+'\')">'+item+'</span>';}else{h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">'+item+'</span>';}});c.innerHTML=h;}
    function buildTagGroup2(containerId, items) {var c = document.getElementById(containerId);if (!c) return;var h = '';items.forEach(function(item) {h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">' + item + '</span>';});c.innerHTML = h;}

    function buildOutfitTagsInput(containerId, inputId, items, isSingle){var c=document.getElementById(containerId);if(!c)return;var h='';items.forEach(function(item){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="fillOutfitInput(this,\''+inputId+'\','+(isSingle?'true':'false')+')">'+item+'</span>';});c.innerHTML=h;}
    function fillOutfitInput(el, inputId, isSingle){var input=document.getElementById(inputId);if(!input)return;var text=el.textContent.trim();if(isSingle){input.value=text;}else{var cur=input.value.trim();if(!cur){input.value=text;return;}var tags=cur.split(/[、,，]/);for(var i=0;i<tags.length;i++){if(tags[i].trim()===text)return;}input.value=cur+'、'+text;}}
    function buildOutfitTags(){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var f=isFemaleGender(g);buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitTopTags','f-outfit-top',f?OUTFIT_TOP_F:OUTFIT_TOP_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitBottomTags','f-outfit-bottom',f?OUTFIT_BOTTOM_F:OUTFIT_BOTTOM_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitShoesTags','f-outfit-shoes',f?OUTFIT_SHOES_F:OUTFIT_SHOES_M,true);var socksSection=document.getElementById('outfitSocksSection');if(socksSection){socksSection.style.display=f?'':'none';if(f) buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitSocksTags','f-outfit-socks',OUTFIT_SOCKS_F,true);}buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitAccessoryTags','f-outfit-accessory',f?OUTFIT_ACC_F:OUTFIT_ACC_M,false);buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitColorTags','f-outfit-color',f?OUTFIT_COLORS_F:OUTFIT_COLORS_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('outfitStyleTags','f-outfit-style',f?OUTFIT_STYLES_F:OUTFIT_STYLES_M,true);}
    function buildBeastOutfitTags(){var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{}).value||'雄';var f=isFemaleBeastSex(sex);buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitTopTags','f-beast-outfit-top',f?OUTFIT_TOP_F:OUTFIT_TOP_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitBottomTags','f-beast-outfit-bottom',f?OUTFIT_BOTTOM_F:OUTFIT_BOTTOM_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitShoesTags','f-beast-outfit-shoes',f?OUTFIT_SHOES_F:OUTFIT_SHOES_M,true);var socksSection=document.getElementById('beastOutfitSocksSection');if(socksSection){socksSection.style.display=f?'':'none';if(f) buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitSocksTags','f-beast-outfit-socks',OUTFIT_SOCKS_F,true);}buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitAccessoryTags','f-beast-outfit-accessory',f?OUTFIT_ACC_F:OUTFIT_ACC_M,false);buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitColorTags','f-beast-outfit-color',f?OUTFIT_COLORS_F:OUTFIT_COLORS_M,true);buildOutfitTagsInput('beastOutfitStyleTags','f-beast-outfit-style',f?OUTFIT_STYLES_F:OUTFIT_STYLES_M,true);}
    function buildNsfwOutfitFields(){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var fields=isFemaleGender(g)?NSFW_OUTFIT_F:NSFW_OUTFIT_M;var h='';fields.forEach(function(f){h+='<div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">'+f.label+'</label>';if(f.type==='single'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="nsfw-outfit-'+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'nsfw-outfit-'+f.key+'\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}else if(f.type==='multi'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="nsfw-outfit-'+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}h+='</div>';});var container=document.getElementById('nsfwOutfitFields');if(container) container.innerHTML=h;}
    function buildNsfwBeastOutfitFields(){var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{}).value||'雄';var fields=isFemaleBeastSex(sex)?NSFW_OUTFIT_F:NSFW_OUTFIT_M;var h='';fields.forEach(function(f){h+='<div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">'+f.label+'</label>';if(f.type==='single'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="nsfw-beast-outfit-'+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'nsfw-beast-outfit-'+f.key+'\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}else if(f.type==='multi'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="nsfw-beast-outfit-'+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}h+='</div>';});var container=document.getElementById('nsfwBeastOutfitFields');if(container) container.innerHTML=h;}
    function buildInvOutfitSelectTags(){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var tags=isFemaleGender(g)?INV_OUTFIT_TAGS_F:INV_OUTFIT_TAGS_M;var container=document.getElementById('invOutfitSelectTags');if(!container)return;var h='';tags.forEach(function(t){h+='<span class="inv-tag-select" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'selected\')">'+t+'</span>';});container.innerHTML=h;}

    function buildNsfwPersonalityTags() {var g = document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var f = isFemaleGender(g);var pTags = f ? NSFW_PERSONALITY_F : NSFW_PERSONALITY_M;var hTags = f ? NSFW_HOBBY_F : NSFW_HOBBY_M;buildTagGroup2('nsfwPersonalityTags', pTags);buildTagGroup2('nsfwHobbyTags', hTags);}
    function buildBeastNsfwPersonalityTags() {var sex = (document.getElementById('f-beast-sex') || {}).value || '雄';var showHuman = (beastFormState === '彻底化形' || beastFormState === '幻化人形');var pContainer = document.getElementById('nsfwBeastPersonalityTags');var hContainer = document.getElementById('nsfwBeastHobbyTags');if (!pContainer || !hContainer) return;if (showHuman) {var f = isFemaleBeastSex(sex);var pTags = (f ? NSFW_PERSONALITY_F : NSFW_PERSONALITY_M).concat(NSFW_BEAST_RESIDUAL);var hTags2 = f ? NSFW_HOBBY_F : NSFW_HOBBY_M;buildTagGroup2('nsfwBeastPersonalityTags', pTags);buildTagGroup2('nsfwBeastHobbyTags', hTags2);} else {var pTags2 = isFemaleBeastSex(sex) ? NSFW_BEAST_PERSONALITY_FEMALE : NSFW_BEAST_PERSONALITY_MALE;var hTags3 = isFemaleBeastSex(sex) ? NSFW_BEAST_HOBBY_FEMALE : NSFW_BEAST_HOBBY_MALE;buildTagGroup2('nsfwBeastPersonalityTags', pTags2);buildTagGroup2('nsfwBeastHobbyTags', hTags3);}}
    function collectNsfwPersonality() {var result = {};result.tags = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwPersonalityTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.hobbies = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwHobbyTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.extra = (document.getElementById('f-nsfw-personality-extra') || {}).value || '';return result;}
    function collectBeastNsfwPersonality() {var result = {};result.tags = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwBeastPersonalityTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.hobbies = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwBeastHobbyTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.extra = (document.getElementById('f-nsfw-beast-personality-extra') || {}).value || '';return result;}
    function restoreNsfwPersonality(data) {if (!data) return;if (data.tags) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwPersonalityTags'), data.tags);if (data.hobbies) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwHobbyTags'), data.hobbies);if (data.extra) (document.getElementById('f-nsfw-personality-extra') || {}).value = data.extra;}
    function restoreBeastNsfwPersonality(data) {if (!data) return;if (data.tags) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwBeastPersonalityTags'), data.tags);if (data.hobbies) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfwBeastHobbyTags'), data.hobbies);if (data.extra) (document.getElementById('f-nsfw-beast-personality-extra') || {}).value = data.extra;}

    function buildSpeechTags() {var g = document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var f = isFemaleGender(g);buildTagGroup2('speechToneTags', f ? SPEECH_TONE_F : SPEECH_TONE_M);buildTagGroup2('speechHabitTags', f ? SPEECH_HABIT_F : SPEECH_HABIT_M);}
    function addSpeechSample() {var container = document.getElementById('speechSampleList');var h = '<div class="custom-skill-item">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.custom-skill-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">场景</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ss="scene" placeholder="如：被挑衅时、初次见面时"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">台词</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-ss="line" placeholder="角色会说的原话"></textarea></div></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);}
    function collectSpeechData() {var result = {};result.tone = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('speechToneTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.habit = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('speechHabitTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.catchphrase = (document.getElementById('f-catchphrase') || {}).value || '';result.addressHabit = (document.getElementById('f-address-habit') || {}).value || '';result.samples = [];document.querySelectorAll('#speechSampleList .custom-skill-item').forEach(function(item) {var scene = (item.querySelector('[data-ss="scene"]') || {}).value || '';var line = (item.querySelector('[data-ss="line"]') || {}).value || '';if (scene.trim() || line.trim()) result.samples.push({ scene: scene.trim(), line: line.trim() });});return result;}
    function restoreSpeechData(data) {if (!data) return;if (data.tone) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('speechToneTags'), data.tone);if (data.habit) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('speechHabitTags'), data.habit);if (data.catchphrase) (document.getElementById('f-catchphrase') || {}).value = data.catchphrase;if (data.addressHabit) (document.getElementById('f-address-habit') || {}).value = data.addressHabit;if (data.samples && data.samples.length > 0) {var container = document.getElementById('speechSampleList');if (container) container.innerHTML = '';data.samples.forEach(function(s) {addSpeechSample();var lastItem = container.querySelector('.custom-skill-item:last-child');if (lastItem) {var scEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ss="scene"]'); if (scEl) scEl.value = s.scene || '';var lnEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ss="line"]'); if (lnEl) lnEl.value = s.line || '';}});}}
    function addBeastSpeechSample() {var container = document.getElementById('beastSpeechSampleList');if (!container) return;var h = '<div class="custom-skill-item">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.custom-skill-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">场景</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-bss="scene" placeholder="如：被人类挑衅时"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">台词</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-bss="line" placeholder="角色会说的原话"></textarea></div></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);}
    function collectBeastSpeechData() {var result = {};result.tone = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('beastSpeechToneTags') || document.createElement('div'));result.catchphrase = (document.getElementById('f-beast-catchphrase') || {}).value || '';result.samples = [];document.querySelectorAll('#beastSpeechSampleList .custom-skill-item').forEach(function(item) {var scene = (item.querySelector('[data-bss="scene"]') || {}).value || '';var line = (item.querySelector('[data-bss="line"]') || {}).value || '';if (scene.trim() || line.trim()) result.samples.push({ scene: scene.trim(), line: line.trim() });});return result;}
    function restoreBeastSpeechData(data) {if (!data) return;if (data.tone) {var toneContainer = document.getElementById('beastSpeechToneTags');if (toneContainer) setActiveMultiTags(toneContainer, data.tone);}if (data.catchphrase) {var cpEl = document.getElementById('f-beast-catchphrase');if (cpEl) cpEl.value = data.catchphrase;}if (data.samples && data.samples.length > 0) {var container = document.getElementById('beastSpeechSampleList');if (container) {container.innerHTML = '';data.samples.forEach(function(s) {addBeastSpeechSample();var lastItem = container.querySelector('.custom-skill-item:last-child');if (lastItem) {var scEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-bss="scene"]'); if (scEl) scEl.value = s.scene || '';var lnEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-bss="line"]'); if (lnEl) lnEl.value = s.line || '';}});}}}

    function buildNsfwFieldHtml(f,prefix){var h='<div class="form-group"><label class="form-label">'+f.label+'</label>';if(f.type==='range'){h+='<div class="form-row"><input class="form-input sm" type="number" id="'+prefix+f.key+'" placeholder="'+f.min+'~'+f.max+'" min="'+f.min+'" max="'+f.max+'"><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-3);align-self:center">'+(f.unit||'')+'</span></div>';}else if(f.type==='single'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="'+prefix+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+prefix+f.key+'\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}else if(f.type==='multi'){h+='<div class="tag-group" id="'+prefix+f.key+'">';f.opts.forEach(function(o){h+='<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">'+o+'</span>';});h+='</div>';}h+='</div>';return h;}

    function buildNsfwBodyFields(){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var h='';if(g==='扶她'){h+='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nsfw);margin-bottom:8px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:1px solid hsla(350,75%,55%,.2)">♀ 女性身体数据</div>';NSFW_FIELDS_F.forEach(function(f){ h+=buildNsfwFieldHtml(f,'nsfw-'); });h+='<div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--nsfw);margin:12px 0 8px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:1px solid hsla(350,75%,55%,.2)">♂ 男性器官数据</div>';NSFW_FIELDS_M.forEach(function(f){ h+=buildNsfwFieldHtml(f,'nsfw-m-'); });}else{var fields=isFemaleGender(g)?NSFW_FIELDS_F:NSFW_FIELDS_M;fields.forEach(function(f){ h+=buildNsfwFieldHtml(f,'nsfw-'); });}document.getElementById('nsfwBodyFields').innerHTML=h;}

    function updateHumanFormTags(){var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{value:'雄'}).value;var f=isFemaleBeastSex(sex);buildTagGroup('humanBodyTypeTags',f?BODY_TYPES_F:BODY_TYPES_M,true);buildTagGroup('humanHairStyleTags',f?HAIR_STYLES_F:HAIR_STYLES_M,true);buildTagGroup('humanFaceShapeTags',f?FACE_SHAPE_F:FACE_SHAPE_M,true);buildTagGroup('humanFaceAuraTags',f?FACE_AURA_F:FACE_AURA_M,false);buildTagGroup('humanHairColorTags',HAIR_COLORS,true);buildTagGroup('humanEyeColorTags',EYE_COLORS,true);}
    function buildNsfwBeastHumanFields(){var container=document.getElementById('nsfwBeastHumanFields');if(!container)return;var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{}).value||'雄';var fields=isFemaleBeastSex(sex)?NSFW_FIELDS_F:NSFW_FIELDS_M;var h='';fields.forEach(function(f){h+=buildNsfwFieldHtml(f,'nsfw-bh-');});container.innerHTML=h;}
    function collectNsfwBeastHuman(){var result={};var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{}).value||'雄';var fields=isFemaleBeastSex(sex)?NSFW_FIELDS_F:NSFW_FIELDS_M;fields.forEach(function(f){if(f.type==='range'){var el=document.getElementById('nsfw-bh-'+f.key);result[f.key]=el?el.value:'';}else if(f.type==='single'){result[f.key]=getActiveSingleTag('nsfw-bh-'+f.key);}else if(f.type==='multi'){result[f.key]=getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-bh-'+f.key)||document.createElement('div'));}});return result;}
    function restoreNsfwBeastHuman(data){if(!data)return;var sex=(document.getElementById('f-beast-sex')||{}).value||'雄';var fields=isFemaleBeastSex(sex)?NSFW_FIELDS_F:NSFW_FIELDS_M;fields.forEach(function(f){if(!data[f.key])return;if(f.type==='range'){var el=document.getElementById('nsfw-bh-'+f.key);if(el)el.value=data[f.key];}else if(f.type==='single'){setActiveSingleTag('nsfw-bh-'+f.key,data[f.key]);}else if(f.type==='multi'){setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-bh-'+f.key),data[f.key]);}});}

    /* ===== 外貌/服饰数据收集恢复 ===== */
    function collectOutfitData(){var of={};of.top=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-top')||{}).value||'';of.bottom=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-bottom')||{}).value||'';of.shoes=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-shoes')||{}).value||'';of.socks=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-socks')||{}).value||'';of.accessories=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-accessory')||{}).value||'';of.mainColor=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-color')||{}).value||'';of.style=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-style')||{}).value||'';of.extra=(document.getElementById('f-outfit-extra')||{}).value||'';of.nsfw={};var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var fields=isFemaleGender(g)?NSFW_OUTFIT_F:NSFW_OUTFIT_M;fields.forEach(function(f){if(f.type==='single'){of.nsfw[f.key]=getActiveSingleTag('nsfw-outfit-'+f.key);}else if(f.type==='multi'){of.nsfw[f.key]=getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-outfit-'+f.key)||document.createElement('div'));}});return of;}
    function restoreOutfitData(of){if(!of)return;if(of.top)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-top')||{}).value=of.top;if(of.bottom)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-bottom')||{}).value=of.bottom;if(of.shoes)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-shoes')||{}).value=of.shoes;if(of.socks)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-socks')||{}).value=of.socks;if(of.accessories){var accEl=document.getElementById('f-outfit-accessory');if(accEl){if(Array.isArray(of.accessories))accEl.value=of.accessories.join('、');else accEl.value=of.accessories;}}if(of.mainColor)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-color')||{}).value=of.mainColor;if(of.style)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-style')||{}).value=of.style;if(of.extra)(document.getElementById('f-outfit-extra')||{}).value=of.extra;if(of.nsfw){var g=document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var fields=isFemaleGender(g)?NSFW_OUTFIT_F:NSFW_OUTFIT_M;fields.forEach(function(f){if(!of.nsfw[f.key])return;if(f.type==='single')setActiveSingleTag('nsfw-outfit-'+f.key,of.nsfw[f.key]);else if(f.type==='multi')setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-outfit-'+f.key),of.nsfw[f.key]);});}}

    function collectAppearanceData() {var ap = {};ap.height = (document.getElementById('f-height')||{}).value || '';ap.weight = (document.getElementById('f-weight')||{}).value || '';ap.bodyType = getActiveSingleTag('bodyTypeTags');ap.skinColor = getActiveSingleTag('skinColorTags');ap.hairColor = getActiveSingleTag('hairColorTags');ap.hairStyle = getActiveSingleTag('hairStyleTags');ap.eyeColor = getActiveSingleTag('eyeColorTags');ap.faceShape = getActiveSingleTag('faceShapeTags');ap.faceAura = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('faceAuraTags') || document.createElement('div'));ap.specialMarks = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('specialMarkTags') || document.createElement('div'));ap.extra = (document.getElementById('f-appear-extra')||{}).value || '';ap.nsfw = {};var g = document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var fields = isFemaleGender(g) ? NSFW_FIELDS_F : NSFW_FIELDS_M;fields.forEach(function(f) {if (f.type === 'range') { var el = document.getElementById('nsfw-' + f.key); ap.nsfw[f.key] = el ? el.value : ''; }else if (f.type === 'single') { ap.nsfw[f.key] = getActiveSingleTag('nsfw-' + f.key); }else if (f.type === 'multi') { ap.nsfw[f.key] = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-' + f.key) || document.createElement('div')); }});if (g === '扶她') {ap.nsfwMale = {};NSFW_FIELDS_M.forEach(function(f) {if (f.type === 'range') { var el = document.getElementById('nsfw-m-' + f.key); ap.nsfwMale[f.key] = el ? el.value : ''; }else if (f.type === 'single') { ap.nsfwMale[f.key] = getActiveSingleTag('nsfw-m-' + f.key); }else if (f.type === 'multi') { ap.nsfwMale[f.key] = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-m-' + f.key) || document.createElement('div')); }});}return ap;}
    function restoreAppearanceData(ap) {if (!ap) return;if (ap.height) { var el = document.getElementById('f-height'); if(el) el.value = ap.height; }if (ap.weight) { var el2 = document.getElementById('f-weight'); if(el2) el2.value = ap.weight; }if (ap.bodyType) setActiveSingleTag('bodyTypeTags', ap.bodyType);if (ap.skinColor) setActiveSingleTag('skinColorTags', ap.skinColor);if (ap.hairColor) setActiveSingleTag('hairColorTags', ap.hairColor);if (ap.hairStyle) setActiveSingleTag('hairStyleTags', ap.hairStyle);if (ap.eyeColor) setActiveSingleTag('eyeColorTags', ap.eyeColor);if (ap.faceShape) setActiveSingleTag('faceShapeTags', ap.faceShape);if (ap.faceAura && ap.faceAura.length) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('faceAuraTags'), ap.faceAura);if (ap.specialMarks && ap.specialMarks.length) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('specialMarkTags'), ap.specialMarks);if (ap.extra) { var el3 = document.getElementById('f-appear-extra'); if(el3) el3.value = ap.extra; }if (ap.nsfw) {var g = document.getElementById('f-gender').value;var fields = isFemaleGender(g) ? NSFW_FIELDS_F : NSFW_FIELDS_M;fields.forEach(function(f) {if (!ap.nsfw[f.key]) return;if (f.type === 'range') { var el = document.getElementById('nsfw-' + f.key); if (el) el.value = ap.nsfw[f.key]; }else if (f.type === 'single') { setActiveSingleTag('nsfw-' + f.key, ap.nsfw[f.key]); }else if (f.type === 'multi') { setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-' + f.key), ap.nsfw[f.key]); }});}if (ap.nsfwMale && document.getElementById('f-gender').value === '扶她') {NSFW_FIELDS_M.forEach(function(f) {if (!ap.nsfwMale[f.key]) return;if (f.type === 'range') { var el = document.getElementById('nsfw-m-' + f.key); if (el) el.value = ap.nsfwMale[f.key]; }else if (f.type === 'single') { setActiveSingleTag('nsfw-m-' + f.key, ap.nsfwMale[f.key]); }else if (f.type === 'multi') { setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('nsfw-m-' + f.key), ap.nsfwMale[f.key]); }});}}

    function buildAttrTags(containerId){var h='';ALL_ATTRS.forEach(function(a){h+='<span class="attr-tag" data-attr="'+a+'" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">'+a+'</span>';});document.getElementById(containerId).innerHTML=h;}
    function getSelectedAttrs(containerId){var attrs=[];document.querySelectorAll('#'+containerId+' .attr-tag.active').forEach(function(t){attrs.push(t.getAttribute('data-attr'));});return attrs;}
    function setSelectedAttrs(containerId,attrList){document.querySelectorAll('#'+containerId+' .attr-tag').forEach(function(t){t.classList.toggle('active',attrList.indexOf(t.getAttribute('data-attr'))>=0);});}
    function getSoulCategory(soulNum){var el=document.getElementById('f-soul'+soulNum+'-cat');return el?el.value:'';}

    function getPrefFor(num){var p={};var nameEl=document.getElementById('pref-name-'+num);if(nameEl&&nameEl.value.trim())p.customName=nameEl.value.trim();var catEl=document.querySelector('#pref-cat-'+num+' .pref-chip.active');if(catEl)p.category=catEl.textContent.trim();var qualEl=document.querySelector('#pref-qual-'+num+' .pref-chip.active');if(qualEl)p.quality=qualEl.textContent.trim();var sysEl=document.querySelector('#pref-sys-'+num+' .pref-chip.active');if(sysEl)p.system=sysEl.textContent.trim();var attrEls=document.querySelectorAll('#pref-attr-'+num+' .pref-chip.active');if(attrEls.length>0){p.attrs=[];attrEls.forEach(function(a){p.attrs.push(a.textContent.trim());});}return p;}
    function buildPrefPrompt(p){var lines=[];if(p.customName)lines.push('【铁律】武魂名称必须是：'+p.customName+'，禁止更改！');if(p.category)lines.push('【铁律】分类必须是：'+p.category+'，禁止更改！');if(p.quality)lines.push('【铁律】品质必须是：'+p.quality+'，禁止更改！');if(p.system)lines.push('【铁律】体系必须是：'+p.system+'，禁止更改！');if(p.attrs&&p.attrs.length>0)lines.push('【铁律】属性必须包含以下全部：'+p.attrs.join('、')+'。一个都不能少！');return lines.length>0?'\n===== 用户偏好限定 =====\n'+lines.join('\n')+'\n以上限定为铁律，严禁违反！':'';}

    /* ===== 删除工具栏（v3.1增加godArtifactList和mechaList支持） ===== */
    function updateDeleteBar(groupId){
        var listMap = {
            'invOutfitList':'invOutfitDelBar','invSoulDevList':'invSoulDevDelBar',
            'invSpareBoneList':'invSpareBoneDelBar','invHerbsList':'invHerbsDelBar',
            'invFoodList':'invFoodDelBar','invOthersList':'invOthersDelBar',
            'godArtifactList':'godArtifactDelBar','mechaList':'mechaDelBar'
        };
        var barId = listMap[groupId];
        if(!barId) return;
        var bar = document.getElementById(barId);
        if(!bar) return;
        var checks = document.querySelectorAll('#'+groupId+' .item-check');
        var checkedCount = 0;
        checks.forEach(function(c){if(c.checked) checkedCount++;});
        if(checks.length === 0){
            bar.innerHTML = '';
            return;
        }
        bar.innerHTML = '<div class="del-selected-bar">' +
            '<button class="del-selected-btn" onclick="deleteSelected(\''+groupId+'\')" '+(checkedCount===0?'disabled':'')+'>'+SVG_TRASH+' 删除选中 ('+checkedCount+')</button>' +
            '<span class="del-selected-count">共 '+checks.length+' 项</span>' +
            '<button class="del-select-all-btn" onclick="toggleSelectAll(\''+groupId+'\')">全选</button>' +
            '</div>';
    }
    function deleteSelected(groupId){
        var itemClass = '.inv-item';
        if(groupId === 'godArtifactList') itemClass = '.god-artifact-item';
        else if(groupId === 'mechaList') itemClass = '.mecha-item';
        var checks = document.querySelectorAll('#'+groupId+' .item-check:checked');
        if(checks.length === 0) return;
        if(!confirm('确定删除选中的 '+checks.length+' 项？')) return;
        checks.forEach(function(c){
            var item = c.closest(itemClass);
            if(item) item.remove();
        });
        updateDeleteBar(groupId);
    }
    function toggleSelectAll(groupId){
        var checks = document.querySelectorAll('#'+groupId+' .item-check');
        var allChecked = true;
        checks.forEach(function(c){if(!c.checked) allChecked = false;});
        checks.forEach(function(c){c.checked = !allChecked;});
        updateDeleteBar(groupId);
    }

    /* ===== 基调标签收集/恢复 ===== */
    function collectToneTags(containerId){
        var tags = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + containerId + ' .tone-chip.active').forEach(function(t){
            tags.push(t.textContent.trim());
        });
        return tags;
    }
    function restoreToneTags(containerId, data){
        if(!data || !data.length) return;
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + containerId + ' .tone-chip').forEach(function(t){
            t.classList.toggle('active', data.indexOf(t.textContent.trim()) >= 0);
        });
    }

    function collectSpiritPower(){
        return {
            level: selectedSpiritLevel,
            value: parseInt((document.getElementById('f-spirit-val') || {}).value) || 0
        };
    }
    function restoreSpiritPower(data){
        if(!data) return;
        selectedSpiritLevel = data.level || '';
        buildSpiritLevelTags();
        if(data.value)(document.getElementById('f-spirit-val') || {}).value = data.value;
        updateSpiritValRange();
        updateSpiritCapacityInfo();
    }

    /* ===== v3.1：魂灵系统函数（按武魂分组+勾选魂环位置+外观+能力） ===== */

    function getOccupiedRingPositions(soulKey){
        var occupied = {};
        var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulKey.replace('soul',''));
        if(!container) return occupied;
        container.querySelectorAll('.soul-spirit-card').forEach(function(card){
            card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check.active').forEach(function(check){
                var pos = parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos'));
                if(!isNaN(pos)) occupied[pos] = card.getAttribute('data-ss-uid');
            });
        });
        return occupied;
    }

    /* v3.1修改：魂灵类型改为人造魂灵/魂灵，增加来源种族+外观+能力字段 */
    function addSoulSpirit(soulNum){
        soulSpiritCounter++;
        var uid = 'ss_' + soulSpiritCounter;
        var soulKey = 'soul' + soulNum;
        var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
        if(!container) return;
        var rc = ringCount();
        var occupied = getOccupiedRingPositions(soulKey);

        var h = '<div class="soul-spirit-card" data-ss-uid="' + uid + '" data-soul-key="' + soulKey + '">';
        h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除此魂灵" onclick="removeSoulSpirit(this)">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';
        h += '<div class="soul-spirit-hd"><span class="soul-spirit-idx">' + soulSpiritCounter + '</span><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ss="name" placeholder="魂灵名称" style="flex:1"></div>';
        h += '<div class="uni-row-2">';
        /* v3.1修改：只有两种类型 */
        h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">魂灵类型</div><div class="tag-group" id="' + uid + '_type">';
        SOUL_SPIRIT_TYPES.forEach(function(t){
            h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'' + uid + '_type\')">' + t + '</span>';
        });
        h += '</div></div>';
        h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">魂灵年限</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ss="years" placeholder="如：5000年" onchange="onSpiritYearsChange(this)"></div>';
        h += '</div>';
        /* v3.1新增：来源种族 */
        h += '<div class="uni-row-2">';
        h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">来源种族</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ss="species" placeholder="如：冰碧帝皇蝎"></div>';
        h += '<div></div>';
        h += '</div>';
        /* 属性 */
        h += '<div class="wu-field" style="margin-top:8px"><div class="wu-field-label">属性（多选）</div><div class="tag-group" id="' + uid + '_attrs">';
        ALL_ATTRS.forEach(function(a){
            h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">' + a + '</span>';
        });
        h += '</div><div class="custom-tag-input-wrap"><input class="custom-tag-input" type="text" placeholder="+ 自定义属性" onkeydown="if(event.key===\'Enter\'){addCustomMultiTag(this,\'' + uid + '_attrs\');event.preventDefault()}"><button class="custom-tag-add" onclick="addCustomMultiTag(this.previousElementSibling,\'' + uid + '_attrs\')">添加</button></div></div>';
        /* v3.1新增：外观 */
        h += '<div class="wu-field" style="margin-top:8px"><div class="wu-field-label">外观描述</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-ss="appearance" placeholder="魂灵的外观形态（30-60字）" style="min-height:44px"></textarea></div>';
        /* v3.1新增：能力 */
        h += '<div class="wu-field" style="margin-top:6px"><div class="wu-field-label">能力描述</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-ss="ability" placeholder="魂灵自带的能力和战斗特点（30-80字）" style="min-height:44px"></textarea></div>';

        /* 魂环位置勾选区域 */
        h += '<div class="ring-assign-area">';
        h += '<div class="ring-assign-title">提供的魂环位置（勾选，已被其他魂灵占据的不可选）</div>';
        h += '<div class="ring-assign-row" id="' + uid + '_assign">';
        for(var i = 0; i < rc; i++){
            var isTaken = occupied[i] && occupied[i] !== uid;
            h += '<span class="ring-assign-check' + (isTaken ? ' taken' : '') + '" data-ring-pos="' + i + '" onclick="toggleRingAssign(this,\'' + uid + '\',\'' + soulKey + '\')">';
            h += '第' + CN[i+1] + '环';
            h += '</span>';
        }
        h += '</div></div>';

        h += '</div>';
        container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);
        updateRingStatusSummary();
    }

    function removeSoulSpirit(btn){
        btn.closest('.soul-spirit-card').remove();
        updateRingStatusSummary();
        rebuildAllSpiritAssignUI();
    }

    function toggleRingAssign(el, ssUid, soulKey){
        if(el.classList.contains('taken')) return;
        el.classList.toggle('active');
        updateRingStatusSummary();
        rebuildAllSpiritAssignUI();
    }

    function onSpiritYearsChange(inputEl){
        updateRingStatusSummary();
    }

    function rebuildAllSpiritAssignUI(){
        ['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(soulKey){
            var soulNum = soulKey.replace('soul','');
            var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
            if(!container) return;
            var cards = container.querySelectorAll('.soul-spirit-card');
            var allAssigned = {};
            cards.forEach(function(card){
                var cUid = card.getAttribute('data-ss-uid');
                card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check.active').forEach(function(check){
                    var pos = parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos'));
                    if(!isNaN(pos)){
                        if(!allAssigned[pos]) allAssigned[pos] = cUid;
                    }
                });
            });
            cards.forEach(function(card){
                var cUid = card.getAttribute('data-ss-uid');
                card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check').forEach(function(check){
                    var pos = parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos'));
                    var isMyActive = check.classList.contains('active');
                    if(allAssigned[pos] && allAssigned[pos] !== cUid && !isMyActive){
                        check.classList.add('taken');
                    } else {
                        check.classList.remove('taken');
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    }

    function updateRingStatusSummary(){
        var summaryEl = document.getElementById('ringStatusContent');
        if(!summaryEl) return;
        var rc = ringCount();
        if(rc === 0){
            summaryEl.innerHTML = '当前等级不足11级，无需配置魂环';
            return;
        }

        var h = '';
        ['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(soulKey){
            var soulNum = parseInt(soulKey.replace('soul',''));
            var soulNameEl = document.getElementById('f-soul' + soulNum + '-name');
            var soulName = soulNameEl ? soulNameEl.value.trim() : '';
            if(!soulName && soulNum > 1) return;
            if(!soulName) soulName = '第' + CN[soulNum] + '武魂';

            h += '<div style="font-weight:700;margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:4px">' + escHtml(soulName) + '：</div>';
            var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
            var occupied = {};
            if(container){
                container.querySelectorAll('.soul-spirit-card').forEach(function(card){
                    var ssName = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="name"]') || {}).value || '未命名魂灵';
                    var ssYears = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="years"]') || {}).value || '';
                    var color = yearsToColor(ssYears);
                    card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check.active').forEach(function(check){
                        var pos = parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos'));
                        if(!isNaN(pos)){
                            occupied[pos] = { name: ssName, color: color, years: ssYears };
                        }
                    });
                });
            }

            for(var i = 0; i < rc; i++){
                var occ = occupied[i];
                if(occ){
                    h += '<div class="ring-status-row"><span class="ring-status-dot" style="border:2px solid ' + getRingBorderColor(occ.color) + ';box-shadow:0 0 4px ' + getRingGlowColor(occ.color) + '"></span>第' + CN[i+1] + '环：<span class="ring-status-spirit"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" style="width:10px;height:10px;vertical-align:middle;color:var(--accent)"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="6"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/></svg> ' + escHtml(occ.name) + '（' + occ.color + '色·' + escHtml(occ.years) + '）</span></div>';
                } else {
                    h += '<div class="ring-status-row"><span class="ring-status-dot" style="border:2px dashed var(--text-3)"></span>第' + CN[i+1] + '环：<span class="ring-status-free">未分配（需在魂环面板手动配置）</span></div>';
                }
            }
        });

        summaryEl.innerHTML = h || '请先添加魂灵并勾选位置';
    }

    /* v3.1修改：构建锁定数据时跳过年限为空的魂灵（修复Bug5） */
    function buildSpiritRingAssign(){
        spiritRingAssign = { soul1:{}, soul2:{}, soul3:{} };
        ['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(soulKey){
            var soulNum = parseInt(soulKey.replace('soul',''));
            var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
            if(!container) return;
            container.querySelectorAll('.soul-spirit-card').forEach(function(card){
                var ssName = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="name"]') || {}).value || '';
                var ssYears = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="years"]') || {}).value || '';
                /* v3.1修复：年限为空时不锁定 */
                if(!ssYears || !ssYears.trim()) return;
                var color = yearsToColor(ssYears);
                if(color === '无') return;
                card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check.active').forEach(function(check){
                    var pos = parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos'));
                    if(!isNaN(pos)){
                        spiritRingAssign[soulKey][pos] = {
                            spiritName: ssName,
                            spiritYears: ssYears,
                            color: color
                        };
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    }

    /* v3.1修改：收集魂灵数据增加新字段 */
    function collectSoulSpirits(){
        var result = {};
        ['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(soulKey){
            var soulNum = parseInt(soulKey.replace('soul',''));
            var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
            if(!container) return;
            var spirits = [];
            container.querySelectorAll('.soul-spirit-card').forEach(function(card){
                var uid = card.getAttribute('data-ss-uid') || '';
                var name = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="name"]') || {}).value || '';
                if(!name.trim()) return;
                var type = getActiveSingleTag(uid + '_type');
                var years = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="years"]') || {}).value || '';
                var species = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="species"]') || {}).value || '';
                var attrs = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById(uid + '_attrs') || document.createElement('div'));
                var appearance = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="appearance"]') || {}).value || '';
                var ability = (card.querySelector('[data-ss="ability"]') || {}).value || '';
                var positions = [];
                card.querySelectorAll('.ring-assign-check.active').forEach(function(check){
                    positions.push(parseInt(check.getAttribute('data-ring-pos')));
                });
                spirits.push({
                    name: name.trim(),
                    type: type,
                    years: years.trim(),
                    species: species.trim(),
                    attrs: attrs,
                    appearance: appearance.trim(),
                    ability: ability.trim(),
                    ringPositions: positions
                });
            });
            if(spirits.length > 0) result[soulKey] = spirits;
        });
        return result;
    }

    function restoreSoulSpirits(data){
        if(!data) return;
        soulSpiritCounter = 0;
        Object.keys(data).forEach(function(soulKey){
            var soulNum = parseInt(soulKey.replace('soul',''));
            var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNum);
            if(!container) return;
            container.innerHTML = '';
            var spirits = data[soulKey];
            if(!spirits || !spirits.length) return;
            spirits.forEach(function(sp){
                addSoulSpirit(soulNum);
                var lastCard = container.querySelector('.soul-spirit-card:last-child');
                if(!lastCard) return;
                var uid = lastCard.getAttribute('data-ss-uid');
                var nameEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="name"]'); if(nameEl) nameEl.value = sp.name || '';
                if(sp.type) setActiveSingleTag(uid + '_type', sp.type);
                var yearsEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="years"]'); if(yearsEl) yearsEl.value = sp.years || '';
                var speciesEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="species"]'); if(speciesEl) speciesEl.value = sp.species || '';
                if(sp.attrs) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById(uid + '_attrs'), sp.attrs);
                var appEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="appearance"]'); if(appEl) appEl.value = sp.appearance || '';
                var abiEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="ability"]'); if(abiEl) abiEl.value = sp.ability || '';
                if(sp.ringPositions && sp.ringPositions.length > 0){
                    sp.ringPositions.forEach(function(pos){
                        var check = lastCard.querySelector('.ring-assign-check[data-ring-pos="' + pos + '"]');
                        if(check) check.classList.add('active');
                    });
                }
            });
        });
        rebuildAllSpiritAssignUI();
        updateRingStatusSummary();
    }

    /* ===== v3.1：神位数据收集/恢复（嵌入式结构） ===== */
    function collectGodData(){
        var result = {};
        result.positions = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('#godPositionList .godpos-card').forEach(function(card){
            var uid = card.getAttribute('data-gp-uid') || '';
            var name = (card.querySelector('[data-gp="name"]') || {}).value || '';
            var type = getActiveSingleTag(uid + '_type');
            var desc = (card.querySelector('[data-gp="desc"]') || {}).value || '';
            var armor = (card.querySelector('[data-gp="armor"]') || {}).value || '';
            /* 收集该神位的神技 */
            var skills = [];
            var skillContainer = document.getElementById('godSkills_' + uid);
            if(skillContainer){
                skillContainer.querySelectorAll('.god-skill-item').forEach(function(item){
                    var sname = (item.querySelector('[data-gs="name"]') || {}).value || '';
                    var sdesc = (item.querySelector('[data-gs="desc"]') || {}).value || '';
                    if(sname.trim() || sdesc.trim()) skills.push({ name:sname.trim(), desc:sdesc.trim() });
                });
            }
            result.positions.push({ uid:uid, name:name.trim(), type:type, desc:desc.trim(), armor:armor.trim(), skills:skills });
        });
        /* 神器 */
        result.artifacts = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('#godArtifactList .god-artifact-item').forEach(function(item){
            var name = (item.querySelector('[data-ga="name"]') || {}).value || '';
            if(!name.trim()) return;
            var levelGroupId = '';
            item.querySelectorAll('.tag-group').forEach(function(g){ if(g.id && g.id.indexOf('gaLevel_')===0) levelGroupId = g.id; });
            var level = levelGroupId ? getActiveSingleTag(levelGroupId) : '';
            var godpos = (item.querySelector('[data-ga="godpos"]') || {}).value || '';
            var desc = (item.querySelector('[data-ga="desc"]') || {}).value || '';
            result.artifacts.push({ name:name.trim(), level:level, godpos:godpos.trim(), desc:desc.trim() });
        });
        result.laws = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('godLawTags') || document.createElement('div'));
        return result;
    }

    function restoreGodData(data){
        if(!data) return;
        var container = document.getElementById('godPositionList');
        if(container) container.innerHTML = '';
        godPositionCounter = 0;
        if(data.positions && data.positions.length > 0){
            data.positions.forEach(function(pos){
                addGodPosition();
                var lastCard = container.querySelector('.godpos-card:last-child');
                if(!lastCard) return;
                var uid = lastCard.getAttribute('data-gp-uid');
                var nameEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="name"]');
                if(nameEl) nameEl.value = pos.name || '';
                if(pos.type) setActiveSingleTag(uid + '_type', pos.type);
                var descEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="desc"]');
                if(descEl) descEl.value = pos.desc || '';
                var armorEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="armor"]');
                if(armorEl) armorEl.value = pos.armor || '';
                /* 恢复神技 */
                if(pos.skills && pos.skills.length > 0){
                    pos.skills.forEach(function(sk){
                        addGodSkill(uid);
                        var skillContainer2 = document.getElementById('godSkills_' + uid);
                        var lastSkill = skillContainer2.querySelector('.god-skill-item:last-child');
                        if(lastSkill){
                            var snEl = lastSkill.querySelector('[data-gs="name"]');
                            if(snEl) snEl.value = sk.name || '';
                            var sdEl = lastSkill.querySelector('[data-gs="desc"]');
                            if(sdEl) sdEl.value = sk.desc || '';
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }
        /* 恢复神器 */
        godArtifactCounter = 0;
        var artContainer = document.getElementById('godArtifactList');
        if(artContainer) artContainer.innerHTML = '';
        if(data.artifacts && data.artifacts.length > 0){
            data.artifacts.forEach(function(art){
                addGodArtifact();
                var lastItem = artContainer.querySelector('.god-artifact-item:last-child');
                if(!lastItem) return;
                var nEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ga="name"]');
                if(nEl) nEl.value = art.name || '';
                if(art.level){
                    lastItem.querySelectorAll('.tag-group').forEach(function(g){
                        if(g.id && g.id.indexOf('gaLevel_')===0){
                            setActiveSingleTag(g.id, art.level);
                        }
                    });
                }
                var gpEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ga="godpos"]');
                if(gpEl) gpEl.value = art.godpos || '';
                var dEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ga="desc"]');
                if(dEl) dEl.value = art.desc || '';
            });
        }
        if(data.laws) setActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('godLawTags'), data.laws);
    }

    /* ===== v3.1：斗铠数据收集/恢复（增加外观描述） ===== */
    function collectDoukaiData(){
        var activeChip = document.querySelector('#doukaiLevelTags .tag-chip.active');
        var level = 'none';
        if(activeChip){
            var text = activeChip.textContent.trim();
            if(text === '无') level = 'none';
            else if(text === '一字') level = 'one';
            else if(text === '二字') level = 'two';
            else if(text === '三字') level = 'three';
            else if(text === '四字') level = 'four';
        }
        return {
            level: level,
            name: (document.getElementById('f-doukai-name') || {}).value || '',
            desc: (document.getElementById('f-doukai-desc') || {}).value || ''
        };
    }

    function restoreDoukaiData(data){
        if(!data) return;
        var levelMap = {'none':'无','one':'一字','two':'二字','three':'三字','four':'四字'};
        var levelText = levelMap[data.level] || '无';
        document.querySelectorAll('#doukaiLevelTags .tag-chip').forEach(function(t){
            t.classList.toggle('active', t.textContent.trim() === levelText);
        });
        if(data.level && data.level !== 'none'){
            var nameArea = document.getElementById('doukaiNameArea');
            if(nameArea) nameArea.style.display = '';
        }
        if(data.name)(document.getElementById('f-doukai-name') || {}).value = data.name;
        if(data.desc)(document.getElementById('f-doukai-desc') || {}).value = data.desc;
    }

    /* ===== v3.1：机甲数据收集/恢复（美化标签等级+描述） ===== */
    function collectMechaData(){
        var mechas = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('#mechaList .mecha-item').forEach(function(item){
            var name = (item.querySelector('[data-mecha="name"]') || {}).value || '';
            var uid = item.getAttribute('data-mecha-uid') || '';
            var levelGroupId = uid + '_level';
            var levelEl = document.querySelector('#' + levelGroupId + ' .mecha-level-chip.active');
            var level = levelEl ? levelEl.textContent.trim() : '';
            var desc = (item.querySelector('[data-mecha="desc"]') || {}).value || '';
            if(name.trim()) mechas.push({ name:name.trim(), level:level, desc:desc.trim() });
        });
        return mechas;
    }

    function restoreMechaData(data){
        if(!data || !data.length) return;
        var container = document.getElementById('mechaList');
        if(container) container.innerHTML = '';
        mechaCounter = 0;
        data.forEach(function(m){
            addMechaItem();
            var lastItem = container.querySelector('.mecha-item:last-child');
            if(lastItem){
                var nEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-mecha="name"]');
                if(nEl) nEl.value = m.name || '';
                var uid = lastItem.getAttribute('data-mecha-uid') || '';
                if(m.level){
                    document.querySelectorAll('#' + uid + '_level .mecha-level-chip').forEach(function(c){
                        c.classList.toggle('active', c.textContent.trim() === m.level);
                    });
                }
                var dEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-mecha="desc"]');
                if(dEl) dEl.value = m.desc || '';
            }
        });
    }

    /* ===== 副职业数据收集/恢复 ===== */
    function collectVocationData(){
        return {
            forge: getVocLevel('vocForgeTags'),
            design: getVocLevel('vocDesignTags'),
            make: getVocLevel('vocMakeTags'),
            repair: getVocLevel('vocRepairTags')
        };
    }

    function restoreVocationData(data){
        if(!data) return;
        function setVoc(groupId, level){
            document.querySelectorAll('#' + groupId + ' .voc-level-chip').forEach(function(c){
                var chipVal = c.textContent.trim() === '无' ? 0 : parseInt(c.textContent.trim()) || 0;
                c.classList.toggle('active', chipVal === level);
            });
        }
        setVoc('vocForgeTags', data.forge || 0);
        setVoc('vocDesignTags', data.design || 0);
        setVoc('vocMakeTags', data.make || 0);
        setVoc('vocRepairTags', data.repair || 0);
    }

    /* ===== 构建神位法则标签 ===== */
    function buildGodLawTags(){
        var container = document.getElementById('godLawTags');
        if(!container) return;
        var h = '';
        GOD_LAWS.forEach(function(law){
            h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">' + law + '</span>';
        });
        container.innerHTML = h;
    }

小镇酒店冲突，赵无极一巴掌扇飞苍晖学院带队老师。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 星斗森林猎杀凤尾鸡冠蛇，奥斯卡获第三魂环飞行肠。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 蛇婆朝天香抢蛇，唐三凭玄天功魂力比拼胜孟依然。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 泰坦巨猿突袭营地击退赵无极，强行抓走小舞。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 唐三吃下飞行肠独自升空追入森林深处。"],"第八章":["【斗罗历2643年9月】 唐三越级单杀两千年人面魔蛛，紫色魂环浮现。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 龙公孟蜀出面施压，唐三亮出教皇令后暗器比试获胜。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 唐三越级吸收魂环，获外附魂骨八蛛矛与第三魂技。","【斗罗历2643年9月】 小舞携紫色第三魂环归队，众人撤出星斗大森林。"],"第九章":["【斗罗历2643年10月】 玉小刚抵达学院，唐三等人平安归来。","【斗罗历2643年10月】 马红俊在索托城被不乐打伤，小舞设局施展八段摔重创不乐。","【斗罗历2643年10月】 玉小刚按年龄排序正式命名史莱克七怪。","【斗罗历2643年10月】 玉小刚制定魔鬼负重跑训练，七人背竹筐跑四百公里。","【斗罗历2643年10月】 辅助系体力透支，七人互相分担负重搀扶完成训练。"],"第十章":["【斗罗历2643年11月】
