    /* ===== 年限滑动条系统 ===== */
    function selectYearTier(btn, tier){document.querySelectorAll('#beastYearsBtns .tag-chip').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});btn.classList.add('active');_curYearTier = tier;var area = document.getElementById('yearRangeArea');var slider = document.getElementById('yearRangeSlider');var config = YEAR_TIERS[tier];if(!config){area.classList.remove('open');return;}area.classList.add('open');slider.min = config.min;slider.max = config.max;slider.step = config.step;slider.value = config.min;var marksHtml = '';config.marks.forEach(function(m){marksHtml += '<span>'+m+'</span>';});document.getElementById('yearRangeMarks').innerHTML = marksHtml;onYearSlider();}
    function onYearSlider(){var slider = document.getElementById('yearRangeSlider');var config = YEAR_TIERS[_curYearTier];if(!config)return;var numVal = parseInt(slider.value);var label = config.format(numVal);document.getElementById('yearRangeVal').textContent = label;document.getElementById('yearRangeSub').textContent = numVal.toLocaleString() + '年';document.getElementById('f-beast-years').value = label;document.getElementById('f-beast-years-num').value = numVal;onBeastYearsChange(numVal);}
    function onBeastYearsChange(yearsNum){var area=document.getElementById('transformOptions');var hint=document.getElementById('transformHint');var btnsArea=document.getElementById('transformBtns');if(yearsNum>=100000){area.style.display='';var h='<button class="beast-form-btn'+(beastFormState==='不化形'?' active':'')+'\" data-form="不化形" onclick="setBeastForm(this)">不化形</button>';h+='<button class="beast-form-btn'+(beastFormState==='彻底化形'?' active':'')+'\" data-form="彻底化形" onclick="setBeastForm(this)">彻底化形</button>';if(yearsNum>=200000){h+='<button class="beast-form-btn'+(beastFormState==='幻化人形'?' active':'')+'\" data-form="幻化人形" onclick="setBeastForm(this)">幻化人形</button>';hint.innerHTML='<p><strong>彻底化形</strong>：融入人类社会，以自身为武魂走完整魂师体系（先天满魂力）</p><p><strong>幻化人形</strong>：可定制人形外貌，但不走武魂/魂环/魂骨流程</p>';}else{hint.innerHTML='<p><strong>彻底化形</strong>：融入人类社会，以自身为武魂走完整魂师体系（先天满魂力）</p><p>修为达到<strong>20万年</strong>可解锁幻化人形</p>';if(beastFormState==='幻化人形'){beastFormState='不化形';}}btnsArea.innerHTML=h;}else{area.style.display='none';if(beastFormState!=='不化形'){beastFormState='不化形';document.getElementById('f-innate').readOnly=false;}}updatePanelVisibility();buildBeastPersonalityContent();refreshSteps();}

    /* ===== 时代/种族/化形 ===== */
    function setEra(btn){document.querySelectorAll('.era-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active');});btn.classList.add('active');selectedEra=btn.getAttribute('data-era');updatePanelVisibility();refreshSteps();}
    function setRace(btn){document.querySelectorAll('.race-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active');});btn.classList.add('active');selectedRace=btn.getAttribute('data-race');updatePanelVisibility();buildBeastPersonalityContent();refreshSteps();maxStepReached=Math.min(maxStepReached,activeSteps.length-1);}
    function setBeastForm(btn){document.querySelectorAll('.beast-form-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active');});btn.classList.add('active');beastFormState=btn.getAttribute('data-form');var innateEl=document.getElementById('f-innate');if(beastFormState==='彻底化形'){innateEl.value='10';innateEl.readOnly=true;}else{innateEl.readOnly=false;}updatePanelVisibility();buildBeastPersonalityContent();refreshSteps();maxStepReached=Math.min(maxStepReached,activeSteps.length-1);}
    /* v3.1修改：调用updateGodFoldContainer */
    function onLevelChange(){updateTitleArea();var sgArea=document.getElementById('sgLevelArea');if(sgArea&&sgArea.style.display!=='none')buildSgLevelTags();updateSoulCorePanel();updateGodFoldContainer();}
    function updateTitleArea(){var area=document.getElementById('titleArea');if(!area)return;var lv=parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value)||0;var showTitle=(lv>=91&&lv<100)&&(selectedRace==='人类'||(selectedRace==='魂兽'&&beastFormState==='彻底化形'));area.classList.toggle('show',showTitle);}
    function buildSgLevelTags(){var container=document.getElementById('sgLevelTags');if(!container)return;var soulLevel=parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value)||0;var cap=Math.min(Math.floor(soulLevel/10)+2,10);var h='<span class="tag-chip'+(sgLevel===0?' active':'')+'\" onclick="setSgLevel(this,0)">无</span>';for(var i=1;i<=10;i++){var locked=(i>cap);h+='<span class="tag-chip'+(sgLevel===i?' active':'')+(locked?' sg-locked':'')+'\" '+(locked?'':'onclick="setSgLevel(this,'+i+')"')+' title="'+(locked?'需'+SG_LEVEL_DATA[i].soul+'魂力':'')+'\">'+i+'级'+(locked?' 🔒':'')+'</span>';}container.innerHTML=h;}
    function setSgLevel(btn,lv){sgLevel=lv;document.querySelectorAll('#sgLevelTags .tag-chip').forEach(function(t){t.classList.remove('active');});btn.classList.add('active');}

    function updatePanelVisibility(){var isHuman=(selectedRace==='人类');var isBeast=(selectedRace==='魂兽');var showHumanForm=(beastFormState==='彻底化形'||beastFormState==='幻化人形');var humanFields=document.getElementById('humanBasicFields');var beastPanel=document.getElementById('beastInfoPanel');if(humanFields)humanFields.style.display=isHuman?'':'none';if(beastPanel)beastPanel.classList.toggle('show',isBeast);var humanAppear=document.getElementById('humanAppearFields');var beastAppear=document.getElementById('beastAppearFields');if(humanAppear)humanAppear.style.display=isHuman?'':'none';if(beastAppear)beastAppear.style.display=isBeast?'':'none';var batHumanTab=document.getElementById('batHumanTab');var batOutfitTab=document.getElementById('batOutfitTab');if(batHumanTab)batHumanTab.style.display=(isBeast&&showHumanForm)?'':'none';if(batOutfitTab)batOutfitTab.style.display=(isBeast&&showHumanForm)?'':'none';var sgArea=document.getElementById('sgLevelArea');if(sgArea){var canSg=(selectedEra==='斗二')&&(isHuman||(isBeast&&beastFormState==='彻底化形'));sgArea.style.display=canSg?'':'none';if(canSg)buildSgLevelTags();}updateTitleArea();}

    /* ===== 魂核系统 ===== */
    function updateSoulCorePanel(){var panel=document.getElementById('soulCorePanel');if(!panel)return;var show=(selectedEra==='斗二'||selectedEra==='斗三')&&needsSoulSteps();panel.classList.toggle('show',show);if(!show)return;var lv=parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value)||0;var maxCores=0;if(lv>=99)maxCores=3;else if(lv>=90)maxCores=2;else if(lv>=70)maxCores=1;buildSoulCoreCountTags(maxCores);buildSoulCoreItems();}
    function buildSoulCoreCountTags(maxCores){var container=document.getElementById('soulCoreCountTags');if(!container)return;if(soulCoreCount>maxCores)soulCoreCount=maxCores;var h='';for(var i=0;i<=3;i++){var locked=(i>maxCores);h+='<span class="tag-chip'+(soulCoreCount===i?' active':'')+(locked?' sg-locked':'')+'\" '+(locked?'':'onclick="setSoulCoreCount('+i+')"')+'>'+i+'个'+(locked?' 🔒':'')+'</span>';}container.innerHTML=h;}
    function setSoulCoreCount(n){if(n===3){if(!confirm('极少数天才才能凝聚第三魂核，确定选择？'))return;}soulCoreCount=n;updateSoulCorePanel();}
    function buildSoulCoreItems(){var container=document.getElementById('soulCoreItems');if(!container)return;if(soulCoreCount===0){container.innerHTML='';document.getElementById('soulCoreMethodArea').style.display='none';return;}var allAttrs=getSelectedAttrs('f-soul1-attrs').concat(getSelectedAttrs('f-soul2-attrs'));if(document.getElementById('f-soul3-attrs'))allAttrs=allAttrs.concat(getSelectedAttrs('f-soul3-attrs'));var uniqueAttrs=[];allAttrs.forEach(function(a){if(uniqueAttrs.indexOf(a)<0)uniqueAttrs.push(a);});var positions=['上丹田','中丹田','下丹田'];var existingData=collectSoulCores().cores||[];var h='';for(var i=0;i<soulCoreCount;i++){var ed=existingData[i]||{};h+='<div class="soul-core-item" data-core-idx="'+i+'">';h+='<div class="wu-field-label" style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">魂核 '+(i+1)+'</div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2">';h+='<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">属性</div><div class="tag-group" id="scAttr'+i+'">';if(uniqueAttrs.length>0){uniqueAttrs.forEach(function(a){h+='<span class="tag-chip'+(ed.attr===a?' active':'')+'\" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'scAttr'+i+'\')">'+a+'</span>';});}else{h+='<span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-3)">请先在武魂信息中选择属性</span>';}h+='</div></div>';h+='<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">位置</div><div class="tag-group" id="scPos'+i+'">';positions.forEach(function(p){h+='<span class="tag-chip'+(ed.position===p?' active':'')+'\" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'scPos'+i+'\')">'+p+'</span>';});h+='</div></div>';h+='</div></div>';}container.innerHTML=h;document.getElementById('soulCoreMethodArea').style.display=(soulCoreCount>=2)?'':'none';}
    function collectSoulCores(){var result={count:soulCoreCount,method:'',cores:[]};if(soulCoreCount>=2)result.method=getActiveSingleTag('soulCoreMethodTags')||'同位共振';for(var i=0;i<soulCoreCount;i++){var attr=getActiveSingleTag('scAttr'+i);var pos=getActiveSingleTag('scPos'+i);result.cores.push({attr:attr,position:pos});}return result;}
    function restoreSoulCores(data){if(!data)return;soulCoreCount=data.count||0;updateSoulCorePanel();if(data.method)setActiveSingleTag('soulCoreMethodTags',data.method);if(data.cores){data.cores.forEach(function(c,i){if(c.attr)setActiveSingleTag('scAttr'+i,c.attr);if(c.position)setActiveSingleTag('scPos'+i,c.position);});}}

    /* ===== 天赋魂技 ===== */
    function addBeastTalent(){var container=document.getElementById('beastTalentList');var h='<div class="beast-talent-item">';h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.beast-talent-item\').remove()">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" data-bt="name" placeholder="天赋魂技名称"><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" data-bt="desc" placeholder="天赋魂技效果描述（30-60字）"></textarea></div>';h+='</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);}
    function collectBeastTalents(){var talents=[];document.querySelectorAll('#beastTalentList .beast-talent-item').forEach(function(item){var name=(item.querySelector('[data-bt="name"]')||{}).value||'';var desc=(item.querySelector('[data-bt="desc"]')||{}).value||'';if(name.trim())talents.push({name:name.trim(),desc:desc.trim()});});return talents;}
    function restoreBeastTalents(talents){var container=document.getElementById('beastTalentList');if(!container||!talents||!talents.length)return;container.innerHTML='';talents.forEach(function(t){var h='<div class="beast-talent-item">';h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.beast-talent-item\').remove()">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" data-bt="name" placeholder="天赋魂技名称" value="'+escHtml(t.name||'')+'"><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" data-bt="desc" placeholder="天赋魂技效果描述">'+escHtml(t.desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);});}

    /* ===== 自创魂技 ===== */
    function addCustomSkill(soulNum){var container=document.getElementById('customSkills'+soulNum);var count=container.querySelectorAll('.custom-skill-item').length;var h='<div class="custom-skill-item" data-cs-idx="'+count+'">';h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.custom-skill-item\').remove()">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" placeholder="魂技名称" data-cs-field="name"><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" placeholder="魂技效果（30-60字）" data-cs-field="desc"></textarea></div>';h+='</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);}
    function collectCustomSkills(soulNum){var container=document.getElementById('customSkills'+soulNum);if(!container)return[];var skills=[];container.querySelectorAll('.custom-skill-item').forEach(function(item){var name=(item.querySelector('[data-cs-field="name"]')||{}).value||'';var desc=(item.querySelector('[data-cs-field="desc"]')||{}).value||'';if(name.trim()||desc.trim())skills.push({name:name.trim(),desc:desc.trim()});});return skills;}
    function restoreCustomSkills(soulNum,skills){var container=document.getElementById('customSkills'+soulNum);if(!container||!skills||!skills.length)return;container.innerHTML='';skills.forEach(function(sk,idx){var h='<div class="custom-skill-item" data-cs-idx="'+idx+'">';h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.custom-skill-item\').remove()">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" placeholder="魂技名称" data-cs-field="name" value="'+escHtml(sk.name||'')+'"><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" placeholder="魂技效果" data-cs-field="desc">'+escHtml(sk.desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);});}

    /* ===== 功法/冥想法 ===== */
    function addGongfa(){var container=document.getElementById('gongfaList');var uid='gf_'+Date.now()+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).substr(2,4);var h='<div class="gongfa-item">';h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.gongfa-item\').remove()">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2">';h+='<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">种类</div><div class="tag-group" id="'+uid+'_0"><span class="tag-chip active" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_0\')" data-gf="type">功法</span><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_0\')" data-gf="type">冥想法</span></div></div>';h+='<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">品级</div><div class="tag-group" id="'+uid+'_1"><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_1\')" data-gf="grade">普通</span><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_1\')" data-gf="grade">高级</span><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_1\')" data-gf="grade">顶级</span><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_1\')" data-gf="grade">绝世</span><span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\''+uid+'_1\')" data-gf="grade">神级</span></div></div>';h+='</div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-gf="name" placeholder="如：玄天功、紫极魔瞳冥想法"></div><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">详情（来历、特点等）</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-gf="detail" placeholder="功法的来历、修炼方式、特殊效果等"></textarea></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">作用效果</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-gf="effect" placeholder="修炼此功法/冥想法带来的具体效果"></textarea></div></div>';h+='</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',h);}
    function collectGongfa(){var list=[];document.querySelectorAll('#gongfaList .gongfa-item').forEach(function(item){var name=(item.querySelector('[data-gf="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim())return;var typeGroup=item.querySelectorAll('[data-gf="type"]');var gradeGroup=item.querySelectorAll('[data-gf="grade"]');var typeVal='功法';typeGroup.forEach(function(t){if(t.classList.contains('active'))typeVal=t.textContent.trim();});var gradeVal='';gradeGroup.forEach(function(t){if(t.classList.contains('active'))gradeVal=t.textContent.trim();});var detail=(item.querySelector('[data-gf="detail"]')||{}).value||'';var effect=(item.querySelector('[data-gf="effect"]')||{}).value||'';list.push({type:typeVal,grade:gradeVal,name:name.trim(),detail:detail.trim(),effect:effect.trim()});});return list;}
    function restoreGongfa(list){var container=document.getElementById('gongfaList');if(!container||!list||!list.length)return;container.innerHTML='';list.forEach(function(gf){addGongfa();var lastItem=container.querySelector('.gongfa-item:last-child');if(!lastItem)return;var nameEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-gf="name"]');if(nameEl)nameEl.value=gf.name||'';var detailEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-gf="detail"]');if(detailEl)detailEl.value=gf.detail||'';var effectEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-gf="effect"]');if(effectEl)effectEl.value=gf.effect||'';lastItem.querySelectorAll('[data-gf="type"]').forEach(function(t){t.classList.toggle('active',t.textContent.trim()===(gf.type||'功法'));});if(gf.grade)lastItem.querySelectorAll('[data-gf="grade"]').forEach(function(t){t.classList.toggle('active',t.textContent.trim()===gf.grade);});})}
    /* ========================================================================== */
    /* ===== 第9楼：魂环/魂骨/物品/导航/表单/API/AI/预览/导出/档案/动画/初始化 = */
    /* ========================================================================== */

    /* ===== 魂环系统 ===== */
    function saveRingData(){['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(key){var rc=ringData[key].length;for(var i=0;i<rc;i++){if(!ringDetailData[key])ringDetailData[key]={};if(!ringDetailData[key][i])ringDetailData[key][i]={};var beast=document.getElementById('rd-beast-'+key+'-'+i);var year=document.getElementById('rd-year-'+key+'-'+i);var sn1=document.getElementById('rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+i);var sd1=document.getElementById('rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn2=document.getElementById('rd-skill2name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd2=document.getElementById('rd-skill2desc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn3=document.getElementById('rd-skill3name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd3=document.getElementById('rd-skill3desc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn4=document.getElementById('rd-skill4name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd4=document.getElementById('rd-skill4desc-'+key+'-'+i);if(beast)ringDetailData[key][i].beast=beast.value;if(year)ringDetailData[key][i].year=year.value;if(sn1)ringDetailData[key][i].skillName=sn1.value;if(sd1)ringDetailData[key][i].skillDesc=sd1.value;if(sn2)ringDetailData[key][i].skill2Name=sn2.value;if(sd2)ringDetailData[key][i].skill2Desc=sd2.value;if(sn3)ringDetailData[key][i].skill3Name=sn3.value;if(sd3)ringDetailData[key][i].skill3Desc=sd3.value;if(sn4)ringDetailData[key][i].skill4Name=sn4.value;if(sd4)ringDetailData[key][i].skill4Desc=sd4.value;}});}
    function getNextAvailableIdx(key){var rd=ringData[key]||[];for(var i=0;i<rd.length;i++){if(rd[i]==='无')return i;}return -1;}

    function buildRings(skipSave){
        if(!skipSave)saveRingData();
        var rc=ringCount();
        var s1=document.getElementById('f-soul1-name').value.trim()||'第一武魂';
        var s2=document.getElementById('f-soul2-name').value.trim();
        var area=document.getElementById('ringArea');
        if(rc===0){area.innerHTML='<div class="ring-no-data">当前等级不足11级，无需配置魂环</div>';ringData.soul1=[];ringData.soul2=[];ringData.soul3=[];return;}
        if(selectedEra==='斗三') buildSpiritRingAssign();
        while(ringData.soul1.length<rc)ringData.soul1.push('无');ringData.soul1=ringData.soul1.slice(0,rc);
        applySpiriLockedColors('soul1');
        if(s2){while(ringData.soul2.length<rc)ringData.soul2.push('无');ringData.soul2=ringData.soul2.slice(0,rc);applySpiriLockedColors('soul2');}else{ringData.soul2=[];ringDetailData.soul2={};}
        var s3=document.getElementById('f-soul3-name')?document.getElementById('f-soul3-name').value.trim():'';
        if(s3&&selectedRace==='人类'){while(ringData.soul3.length<rc)ringData.soul3.push('无');ringData.soul3=ringData.soul3.slice(0,rc);applySpiriLockedColors('soul3');}else{ringData.soul3=[];ringDetailData.soul3={};}
        var h=buildRingWu(s1,'soul1',rc,1);if(s2)h+=buildRingWu(s2,'soul2',rc,2);if(s3&&selectedRace==='人类')h+=buildRingWu(s3,'soul3',rc,3);
        area.innerHTML=h;bindRingEvents();restoreRingDetails();checkBoneHint();updateRingLockState();
    }

    function applySpiriLockedColors(soulKey){
        var assign = spiritRingAssign[soulKey] || {};
        for(var idx in assign){
            var i = parseInt(idx);
            if(!isNaN(i) && i < ringData[soulKey].length){
                ringData[soulKey][i] = assign[i].color;
                if(!ringDetailData[soulKey])ringDetailData[soulKey]={};
                if(!ringDetailData[soulKey][i])ringDetailData[soulKey][i]={};
                ringDetailData[soulKey][i].beast = assign[i].spiritName;
                ringDetailData[soulKey][i].year = assign[i].spiritYears.replace(/年$/,'');
            }
        }
    }

    function updateRingLockState(){['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(key){var rd=ringData[key]||[];var nextIdx=getNextAvailableIdx(key);var assign = spiritRingAssign[key] || {};for(var i=0;i<rd.length;i++){var orb=document.querySelector('.ring-orb[data-key="'+key+'"][data-idx="'+i+'"]');if(!orb)continue;if(assign[i]){orb.classList.add('spirit-locked');orb.classList.remove('locked');continue;}if(rd[i]!=='无'){orb.classList.remove('locked');}else if(i===nextIdx){orb.classList.remove('locked');}else{orb.classList.add('locked');}}});}

    function buildRingWu(name,key,count,soulNum){var h='<div class="ring-wu"><div class="ring-wu-title"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/></svg>'+escHtml(name)+'（'+count+'环）</div>';h+='<div class="ring-row" id="ringrow-'+key+'">';for(var i=0;i<count;i++){var c=ringData[key][i]||'无';h+='<div class="ring-orb" data-color="'+c+'" data-key="'+key+'" data-idx="'+i+'" data-soul="'+soulNum+'"><span class="ring-idx">'+CN[i+1]+'</span><span class="ring-clr">'+c+'</span><div class="burst-ring"></div></div>';}h+='</div>';h+='<div class="color-picker-bar" id="picker-'+key+'"></div>';h+='<div class="ring-detail-grid" id="ringdetails-'+key+'">';for(var j=0;j<count;j++){if(ringData[key][j]!=='无'){h+=buildRingDetailItem(key,j,ringData[key][j],soulNum);}}h+='</div></div>';return h;}

    function buildRingDetailItem(key,idx,color,soulNum){var range=COLOR_YEAR_RANGE[color];var rangeHint=range?'（'+range.label+'）':'';var dd=ringDetailData[key]&&ringDetailData[key][idx]?ringDetailData[key][idx]:{};var isDbl=isDoubleSkillRing(color)&&!isSeventh(idx);var isQuad=isQuadSkillRing(color)&&!isSeventh(idx);var is7th=isSeventh(idx);var s7name=is7th?(isQiWuHun(soulNum)?'器魂真身':'武魂真身'):'';var isHigh=color==='红'||color==='橙金'||color==='金';var isLocked=spiritRingAssign[key]&&spiritRingAssign[key][idx];var h='<div class="ring-detail-item" id="rditem-'+key+'-'+idx+'">';if(!isLocked){h+='<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="清空此环魂技" onclick="clearSingleRingSkill(\''+key+'\','+idx+')">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div>';}h+='<div class="ring-detail-hd"><span class="ring-detail-dot" style="border:3px solid '+getRingBorderColor(color)+';box-shadow:0 0 6px '+getRingGlowColor(color)+'"></span><span class="ring-detail-label">第'+CN[idx+1]+'环（'+color+'）'+(isLocked?' · <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" style="width:10px;height:10px;vertical-align:middle;color:var(--accent)"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="6"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2"/></svg>魂灵锁定':'')+(is7th?' · 武魂真身':(isQuad?' · 四魂技':(isDbl?' · 双魂技':'')))+'</span></div>';if(isHigh&&!is7th){h+='<div class="ring-high-warn">⚠ 十万年以上魂兽必出魂骨，请在魂骨步骤补充</div>';}h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input'+(isLocked?' locked':'')+'\" type="text" id="rd-beast-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂兽名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.beast||'')+'"'+(isLocked?' readonly':'')+'><input class="ring-detail-input'+(isLocked?' locked':'')+'\" type="text" id="rd-year-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="年限'+rangeHint+'" value="'+escHtml(dd.year||'')+'"'+(isLocked?' readonly':'')+'></div>';if(is7th){h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input locked" type="text" id="rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+idx+'" value="'+s7name+'" readonly><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="武魂真身效果详情">'+escHtml(dd.skillDesc||'')+'</textarea></div>';}else if(isQuad){h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技壹名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skillName||'')+'"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skill2name-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技贰名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skill2Name||'')+'"></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技壹详情">'+escHtml(dd.skillDesc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skill2desc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技贰详情">'+escHtml(dd.skill2Desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skill3name-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技叁名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skill3Name||'')+'"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skill4name-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技肆名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skill4Name||'')+'"></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skill3desc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技叁详情">'+escHtml(dd.skill3Desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skill4desc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技肆详情">'+escHtml(dd.skill4Desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';}else if(isDbl){h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技壹名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skillName||'')+'"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skill2name-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技贰名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skill2Name||'')+'"></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技壹详情">'+escHtml(dd.skillDesc||'')+'</textarea></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skill2desc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技贰详情">'+escHtml(dd.skill2Desc||'')+'</textarea></div>';}else{h+='<div class="uni-row-2"><input class="ring-detail-input" type="text" id="rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技名称" value="'+escHtml(dd.skillName||'')+'"><div></div></div>';h+='<div class="uni-row-full"><textarea class="detail-ta" id="rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+idx+'" placeholder="魂技详情">'+escHtml(dd.skillDesc||'')+'</textarea></div>';}h+='</div>';return h;}

    function clearSingleRingSkill(key,idx){saveRingData();if(ringDetailData[key]&&ringDetailData[key][idx]){var is7=isSeventh(idx);var soulNum=key==='soul1'?1:(key==='soul2'?2:3);ringDetailData[key][idx]={};if(is7){ringDetailData[key][idx].skillName=isQiWuHun(soulNum)?'器魂真身':'武魂真身';}}buildRings(true);buildRingGenTags();}
    function checkBoneHint(){var hasHighRing=false;['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(key){ringData[key].forEach(function(c){if(c==='红'||c==='橙金'||c==='金')hasHighRing=true;});});var banner=document.getElementById('boneHintBanner');if(banner)banner.classList.toggle('show',hasHighRing);}
    function restoreRingDetails(){['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(key){var dd=ringDetailData[key]||{};for(var i in dd){var b=document.getElementById('rd-beast-'+key+'-'+i);var y=document.getElementById('rd-year-'+key+'-'+i);var sn1=document.getElementById('rd-skillname-'+key+'-'+i);var sd1=document.getElementById('rd-skilldesc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn2=document.getElementById('rd-skill2name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd2=document.getElementById('rd-skill2desc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn3=document.getElementById('rd-skill3name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd3=document.getElementById('rd-skill3desc-'+key+'-'+i);var sn4=document.getElementById('rd-skill4name-'+key+'-'+i);var sd4=document.getElementById('rd-skill4desc-'+key+'-'+i);if(b&&dd[i].beast)b.value=dd[i].beast;if(y&&dd[i].year)y.value=dd[i].year;if(sn1&&dd[i].skillName&&!sn1.readOnly)sn1.value=dd[i].skillName;if(sd1&&dd[i].skillDesc)sd1.value=dd[i].skillDesc;if(sn2&&dd[i].skill2Name)sn2.value=dd[i].skill2Name;if(sd2&&dd[i].skill2Desc)sd2.value=dd[i].skill2Desc;if(sn3&&dd[i].skill3Name)sn3.value=dd[i].skill3Name;if(sd3&&dd[i].skill3Desc)sd3.value=dd[i].skill3Desc;if(sn4&&dd[i].skill4Name)sn4.value=dd[i].skill4Name;if(sd4&&dd[i].skill4Desc)sd4.value=dd[i].skill4Desc;}});}

    function bindRingEvents(){document.querySelectorAll('.ring-orb').forEach(function(orb){orb.addEventListener('click',function(){if(this.classList.contains('locked')){var idx2=parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-idx'));alert('请先获取第'+CN[idx2]+'环！魂环必须按顺序获取。');return;}if(this.classList.contains('spirit-locked')){return;}openColorPicker(this);});});}

    function openColorPicker(orb){var key=orb.getAttribute('data-key');var idx=parseInt(orb.getAttribute('data-idx'));var soulNum=parseInt(orb.getAttribute('data-soul'));var curColor=orb.getAttribute('data-color');activeRingOrb={el:orb,key:key,idx:idx,soulNum:soulNum};document.querySelectorAll('.ring-orb').forEach(function(o){o.style.outline='none';});orb.style.outline='2.5px solid var(--accent)';orb.style.outlineOffset='3px';var picker=document.getElementById('picker-'+key);var colorsToUse=getColorsForEra();var h='';colorsToUse.forEach(function(c){h+='<div class="color-pick-orb'+(c===curColor?' active':'')+'\" data-pc="'+c+'">'+c+'</div>';});picker.innerHTML=h;picker.classList.add('open');picker.onclick=function(e){var el=e.target.closest('.color-pick-orb');if(!el)return;pickColor(el,el.getAttribute('data-pc'));};['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(k){if(k!==key){var p=document.getElementById('picker-'+k);if(p)p.classList.remove('open');}});}

    function pickColor(el,color){if(!activeRingOrb)return;var key=activeRingOrb.key;var idx=activeRingOrb.idx;var soulNum=activeRingOrb.soulNum;if(color==='无'){for(var ci=idx;ci<ringData[key].length;ci++){if(spiritRingAssign[key]&&spiritRingAssign[key][ci])continue;ringData[key][ci]='无';if(ringDetailData[key]&&ringDetailData[key][ci]){ringDetailData[key][ci]={};}}}else{ringData[key][idx]=color;}if(isSeventh(idx)&&color!=='无'){if(!ringDetailData[key])ringDetailData[key]={};if(!ringDetailData[key][idx])ringDetailData[key][idx]={};ringDetailData[key][idx].skillName=isQiWuHun(soulNum)?'器魂真身':'武魂真身';}activeRingOrb.el.setAttribute('data-color',color);activeRingOrb.el.querySelector('.ring-clr').textContent=color;activeRingOrb.el.style.outline='none';activeRingOrb.el.classList.remove('bursting');void activeRingOrb.el.offsetWidth;activeRingOrb.el.classList.add('bursting');setTimeout(function(){activeRingOrb.el.classList.remove('bursting');},600);var detailArea=document.getElementById('ringdetails-'+key);saveRingData();var rc2=ringData[key].length;var dh='';for(var j=0;j<rc2;j++){if(ringData[key][j]!=='无'){dh+=buildRingDetailItem(key,j,ringData[key][j],soulNum);}}detailArea.innerHTML=dh;restoreRingDetails();checkBoneHint();updateRingLockState();for(var ui=0;ui<rc2;ui++){var uOrb=document.querySelector('.ring-orb[data-key="'+key+'"][data-idx="'+ui+'"]');if(uOrb){uOrb.setAttribute('data-color',ringData[key][ui]);uOrb.querySelector('.ring-clr').textContent=ringData[key][ui];}}updateRingLockState();buildRingGenTags();setTimeout(function(){var picker2=document.getElementById('picker-'+key);if(picker2)picker2.classList.remove('open');activeRingOrb=null;},250);}

    function buildRingGenTags(){var area=document.getElementById('ringGenTags');if(!area)return;var h='';['soul1','soul2','soul3'].forEach(function(key){var rd=ringData[key]||[];if(rd.length===0)return;for(var i=0;i<rd.length;i++){if(rd[i]!=='无'){h+='<span class="ring-gen-tag" data-ring-key="'+key+'" data-ring-idx="'+i+'" onclick="this.classList.toggle(\'active\')">第'+CN[i+1]+'环·'+rd[i]+'</span>';}}});area.innerHTML=h||'<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-3)">请先在上方选择魂环颜色</span>';}
    function selectAllRingGenTags(){document.querySelectorAll('#ringGenTags .ring-gen-tag').forEach(function(t){t.classList.add('active');});}

    function collectInventory(){var inv={gold:parseInt(document.getElementById('inv-gold').value)||0,silver:parseInt(document.getElementById('inv-silver').value)||0,copper:parseInt(document.getElementById('inv-copper').value)||0,fc:parseInt((document.getElementById('inv-fc')||{value:0}).value)||0,contribution:parseInt((document.getElementById('inv-contribution')||{value:0}).value)||0,slc:parseInt((document.getElementById('inv-slc')||{value:0}).value)||0,outfits:[],soulDevices:[],spareBones:[],herbs:[],food:[],others:[]};function name=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim())return;var qty=parseInt((item.querySelector('[data-inv="qty"]')||{}).value)||1;var .inv-item').forEach(function(item){var name=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim())return;var level=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="sdlevel"]')||{}).value||'1';var appearance=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="appearance"]')||{}).value||'';var .inv-item').forEach(function(item){var name=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim())return;var partSel=item.querySelector('[data-inv="part"]');var yearSel=item.querySelector('[data-inv="year"]');var part=partSel?partSel.value:'';var year=yearSel?yearSel.value:'';var beast=(item.querySelector('[data-inv="beast"]')||{}).value||'';var inv;}

    function yearSel=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="year"]');if(yearSel&&sb.year)yearSel.value=sb.year;var s1n=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sk1name"]');if(s1n)s1n.value=sb.skill1Name||'';var s1d=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sk1desc"]');if(s1d)s1d.value=sb.skill1Desc||'';var s2n=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sk2name"]');if(s2n)s2n.value=sb.skill2Name||'';var     function parseAndAddInvItems(result, category){var secs=splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec){sec=sec.trim();if(!sec)return;if(category==='soulDevices'){addInvSoulDev();var lastItem=document.querySelector('#invSoulDevList .inv-item:last-child');if(lastItem){var n=extractField(sec,'名称');var lv=extractField(sec,'等级');var ap=extractField(sec,'外观');var ef=extractField(sec,'作用')||extractField(sec,'效果');var nEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]');if(nEl&&n)nEl.value=n;var lvEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sdlevel"]');if(lvEl&&lv)lvEl.value=parseInt(lv)||1;var apEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="appearance"]');if(apEl&&ap)apEl.value=ap;var efEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="effect"]');if(efEl&&ef)efEl.value=ef;}}else{var listMap={'outfits':'invOutfitList','herbs':'invHerbsList','food':'invFoodList','others':'invOthersList'};addInvItem(category);var container=document.getElementById(listMap[category]);var lastItem2=container.querySelector('.inv-item:last-child');if(lastItem2){var n2=extractField(sec,'名称');var qty=extractField(sec,'数量');var desc=extractField(sec,'描述');var nEl2=lastItem2.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]');if(nEl2&&n2)nEl2.value=n2;var qEl=lastItem2.querySelector('[data-inv="qty"]');if(qEl&&qty)qEl.value=parseInt(qty)||1;var dEl=lastItem2.querySelector('[data-inv="desc"]');if(dEl&&desc)dEl.value=desc;}}});}

    function parseAndAddSpareBone(sec){sec=sec.trim();if(!sec)return;addInvSpareBone();var lastItem=document.querySelector('#invSpareBoneList .inv-item:last-child');if(lastItem){var n=extractField(sec,'名称');var beast=extractField(sec,'来源魂兽')||extractField(sec,'魂兽');var part=extractField(sec,'部位');var year=extractField(sec,'年限');var sk1=extractField(sec,'魂技壹');var detailMatches=[];var detailRe=/详情[：:]\s*(.+)/g;var dm;while((dm=detailRe.exec(sec))!==null){detailMatches.push(dm[1].trim());}var nEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="name"]');if(nEl&&n)nEl.value=n;var bEl=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="beast"]');if(bEl&&beast)bEl.value=beast;var s1n=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sk1name"]');if(s1n&&sk1)s1n.value=sk1;var s1d=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="sk1desc"]');if(s1d&&detailMatches.length>=1)s1d.value=detailMatches[0];if(part){var partSel=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="part"]');if(partSel){for(var i=0;i<partSel.options.length;i++){if(partSel.options[i].value===part.trim()){partSel.selectedIndex=i;break;}}}}if(year){year=year.trim().replace(/,/g,'');var yearSel=lastItem.querySelector('[data-inv="year"]');if(yearSel){var yearNum=parseYears(year);var targetVal='';if(yearNum>=1000000)targetVal='百万年';else if(yearNum>=100000)targetVal='十万年';else if(yearNum>=10000)targetVal='万年';else if(yearNum>=1000)targetVal='千年';else if(yearNum>=100)targetVal='百年';if(targetVal){for(var k=0;k<yearSel.options.length;k++){if(yearSel.options[k].value===targetVal){yearSel.selectedIndex=k;break;}}}}}}}

data-ft="soul1" placeholder="NPC的第一个武魂名称"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full" data-ft="soul2row" style="display:none"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">NPC武魂②名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ft="soul2" placeholder="NPC的第二个武魂名称"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full" data-ft="soul3row" style="display:none"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">NPC武魂③名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-ft="soul3" placeholder="NPC的第三个武魂名称"></div></div>';h += '</div>';h += '<div style="margin-top:10px;display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">';h += '<button class="ai-btn" onclick="aiGenFusionForTarget(this)" style="padding:5px 12px;font-size:10px"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" style="width:11px;height:11px"><path d="M12 3l1.5 5.5L19 10l-5.5 1.5L12 17l-1.5-5.5L5 10l5.5-1.5L12 3z"/></svg>AI生成融合技</button>';h += '</div>';h += '<div data-ft="results" style="margin-top:10px"></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);}
    function onFusionSoulCountChange(selectEl) {var panel = selectEl.closest('.fusion-target-panel');var count = parseInt(selectEl.value) || 1;var s2row = panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul2row"]');var s3row = panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul3row"]');if (s2row) s2row.style.display = (count >= 2) ? '' : 'none';if (s3row) s3row.style.display = (count >= 3) ? '' : 'none';}
    function collectFusionSkills() {var targets = [];document.querySelectorAll('#fusionTargetList .fusion-target-panel').forEach(function(panel) {var name = (panel.querySelector('[data-ft="name"]') || {}).value || '';if (!name.trim()) return;var count = parseInt((panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soulCount"]') || {}).value) || 1;var npcSouls = [];for (var i = 1; i <= count; i++) {var sEl = panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul' + i + '"]');if (sEl && sEl.value.trim()) npcSouls.push(sEl.value.trim());}var fusions = [];panel.querySelectorAll('.fusion-result-item').forEach(function(item) {fusions.push({mySoul: (item.querySelector('[data-fres="mySoul"]') || {}).value || '',targetSoul: (item.querySelector('[data-fres="targetSoul"]') || {}).value || '',name: (item.querySelector('[data-fres="name"]') || {}).value || '',effect: (item.querySelector('[data-fres="effect"]') || {}).value || ''});});targets.push({targetName: name.trim(),npcSoulCount: count,npcSouls: npcSouls,fusions: fusions});});return targets;}
    function restoreFusionSkills(list) {var container = document.getElementById('fusionTargetList');if (!container || !list || !list.length) return;container.innerHTML = '';list.forEach(function(target) {addFusionTarget();var lastPanel = container.querySelector('.fusion-target-panel:last-child');if (!lastPanel) return;var nameEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="name"]');if (nameEl) nameEl.value = target.targetName || '';var countEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="soulCount"]');if (countEl) {countEl.value = target.npcSoulCount || 1;onFusionSoulCountChange(countEl);}if (target.npcSouls) {target.npcSouls.forEach(function(s, i) {var sEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul' + (i + 1) + '"]');if (sEl) sEl.value = s;});}if (target.fusions && target.fusions.length > 0) {var resultsArea = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="results"]');if (resultsArea) {target.fusions.forEach(function(f) {var rh = buildFusionResultItem(f.mySoul, f.targetSoul, f.name, f.effect);resultsArea.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', rh);});}}});}
    function buildFusionResultItem(mySoul, targetSoul, fusionName, fusionEffect) {var h = '<div class="fusion-result-item">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.fusion-result-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="fusion-pair-label">我方「' + escHtml(mySoul || '') + '」× 对方「' + escHtml(targetSoul || '') + '」</div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2">';h += '<input class="ring-detail-input" data-fres="mySoul" value="' + escHtml(mySoul || '') + '" style="display:none">';h += '<input class="ring-detail-input" data-fres="targetSoul" value="' + escHtml(targetSoul || '') + '" style="display:none">';h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">融合技名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-fres="name" value="' + escHtml(fusionName || '') + '" placeholder="2-6字"></div>';h += '<div></div>';h += '</div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">融合技效果</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-fres="effect" placeholder="30-80字">' + escHtml(fusionEffect || '') + '</textarea></div></div>';h += '</div>';return h;}
    function buildSelfFusionPanel() {var panel = document.getElementById('selfFusionPanel');var combosArea = document.getElementById('selfFusionCombos');if (!panel || !combosArea) return;var myWu = getMyWuList();if (myWu.length < 2) {panel.style.display = 'none';return;}panel.style.display = '';var combos = [];for (var i = 0; i < myWu.length; i++) {for (var j = i + 1; j < myWu.length; j++) {combos.push({ wu1: myWu[i], wu2: myWu[j], type: 'dual' });}}if (myWu.length >= 3) {combos.push({ wu1: myWu[0], wu2: myWu[1], wu3: myWu[2], type: 'triple' });}var existingData = collectSelfFusion();var h = '';combos.forEach(function(combo) {var comboKey = combo.type === 'triple' ? (combo.wu1 + '×' + combo.wu2 + '×' + combo.wu3) : (combo.wu1 + '×' + combo.wu2);var existing = null;existingData.forEach(function(e) { if (e.comboKey === comboKey) existing = e; });h += '<div class="self-fusion-combo" data-sf-combo="' + escHtml(comboKey) + '">';if (combo.type === 'triple') {h += '<div class="self-fusion-combo-label">「' + escHtml(combo.wu1) + '」×「' + escHtml(combo.wu2) + '」×「' + escHtml(combo.wu3) + '」（三武魂融合）</div>';} else {h += '<div class="self-fusion-combo-label">「' + escHtml(combo.wu1) + '」×「' + escHtml(combo.wu2) + '」</div>';}h += '<div class="uni-row-2">';h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">融合技名称</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-sf="name" value="' + escHtml(existing ? existing.name : '') + '" placeholder="2-6字"></div>';h += '<div></div>';h += '</div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-full"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">融合技效果</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-sf="effect" placeholder="30-80字">' + escHtml(existing ? existing.effect : '') + '</textarea></div></div>';h += '</div>';});combosArea.innerHTML = h;}
    function collectSelfFusion() {var result = [];document.querySelectorAll('#selfFusionCombos .self-fusion-combo').forEach(function(combo) {var comboKey = combo.getAttribute('data-sf-combo') || '';var name = (combo.querySelector('[data-sf="name"]') || {}).value || '';var effect = (combo.querySelector('[data-sf="effect"]') || {}).value || '';if (name.trim() || effect.trim()) {result.push({ comboKey: comboKey, name: name.trim(), effect: effect.trim() });}});return result;}
    function restoreSelfFusion(data) {if (!data || !data.length) return;buildSelfFusionPanel();data.forEach(function(item) {var combo = document.querySelector('.self-fusion-combo[data-sf-combo="' + item.comboKey + '"]');if (combo) {var nameEl = combo.querySelector('[data-sf="name"]');var effectEl = combo.querySelector('[data-sf="effect"]');if (nameEl) nameEl.value = item.name || '';if (effectEl) effectEl.value = item.effect || '';}});}

    /* ===== NPC关系管理系统（完整保留原版） ===== */
    function addNpcRelation() {var container = document.getElementById('npcRelationList');if (!container) return;var uid = 'nr_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 4);var h = '<div class="npc-rel-item" data-nr-uid="' + uid + '">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.npc-rel-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2" style="margin-bottom:8px">';h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">选择NPC</div><select class="wu-field-select" data-nr="npcName" onchange="onNpcRelSelect(this)"><option value="">手动输入...</option>';_loadedNpcList.forEach(function(npc) {if (npc.era === selectedEra) {h += '<option value="' + escHtml(npc.displayName) + '">' + escHtml(npc.displayName) + '</option>';}});h += '</select></div>';h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">或手动输入NPC名字</div><input class="ring-detail-input" data-nr="npcNameManual" placeholder="NPC名字"></div>';h += '</div>';h += '<div class="wu-field" style="margin-bottom:8px"><div class="wu-field-label">关系定位（单选）</div><div class="tag-group" id="' + uid + '_rel">';NPC_REL_TYPES.forEach(function(t) {h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'' + uid + '_rel\')">' + t + '</span>';});h += '</div></div>';h += '<div class="wu-field" style="margin-bottom:8px"><div class="wu-field-label">初始态度（单选）</div><div class="tag-group" id="' + uid + '_att">';NPC_ATTITUDES.forEach(function(a) {h += '<span class="tag-chip" onclick="toggleSingleTag(this,\'' + uid + '_att\')">' + a + '</span>';});h += '</div></div>';h += '<div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">关系描述</div><textarea class="detail-ta" data-nr="desc" placeholder="描述和这个NPC的具体关系..."></textarea></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);}
    function onNpcRelSelect(selectEl) {var item = selectEl.closest('.npc-rel-item');var manualInput = item.querySelector('[data-nr="npcNameManual"]');if (selectEl.value) {manualInput.value = selectEl.value;manualInput.readOnly = true;} else {manualInput.value = '';manualInput.readOnly = false;}}
    function collectNpcRelations() {var relations = [];document.querySelectorAll('#npcRelationList .npc-rel-item').forEach(function(item) {var uid = item.getAttribute('data-nr-uid') || '';var selectEl = item.querySelector('[data-nr="npcName"]');var manualEl = item.querySelector('[data-nr="npcNameManual"]');var npcName = (selectEl && selectEl.value) ? selectEl.value : ((manualEl || {}).value || '');if (!npcName.trim()) return;var relType = getActiveSingleTag(uid + '_rel');var attitude = getActiveSingleTag(uid + '_att');var desc = (item.querySelector('[data-nr="desc"]') || {}).value || '';relations.push({npcName: npcName.trim(),relationType: relType,attitude: attitude,description: desc.trim()});});return relations;}
    function restoreNpcRelations(data) {var container = document.getElementById('npcRelationList');if (!container || !data || !data.length) return;container.innerHTML = '';data.forEach(function(rel) {addNpcRelation();var lastItem = container.querySelector('.npc-rel-item:last-child');if (!lastItem) return;var uid = lastItem.getAttribute('data-nr-uid') || '';var manualEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-nr="npcNameManual"]');if (manualEl) manualEl.value = rel.npcName || '';if (rel.relationType) setActiveSingleTag(uid + '_rel', rel.relationType);if (rel.attitude) setActiveSingleTag(uid + '_att', rel.attitude);var descEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-nr="desc"]');if (descEl) descEl.value = rel.description || '';});}
    function openNpcPickerForFusion() {var filteredNpcs = _loadedNpcList.filter(function(n) {return n.era === selectedEra && n.data;});if (filteredNpcs.length === 0) {alert('当前时代没有已创建的NPC。请先在首页创建NPC角色。');return;}document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent = '选择已创建的NPC';var h = '';filteredNpcs.forEach(function(npc, idx) {var soulInfo = npc.soul1Name || '未知武魂';h += '<div class="picker-option" data-npc-pick-idx="' + idx + '">' + escHtml(npc.displayName) + '<span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-3);margin-left:8px">' + escHtml(soulInfo) + '</span></div>';});document.getElementById('pickerBody').innerHTML = h;document.querySelectorAll('#pickerBody .picker-option').forEach(function(opt) {opt.addEventListener('click', function() {var npcIdx = parseInt(this.getAttribute('data-npc-pick-idx'));var npc = filteredNpcs[npcIdx];if (!npc || !npc.data) return;addFusionTarget();var container = document.getElementById('fusionTargetList');var lastPanel = container.querySelector('.fusion-target-panel:last-child');if (lastPanel) {var nameEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="name"]');if (nameEl) nameEl.value = npc.displayName;var npcSouls = [];if (npc.data.soul1Name) npcSouls.push(npc.data.soul1Name);if (npc.data.soul2Name) npcSouls.push(npc.data.soul2Name);if (npc.data.soul3Name) npcSouls.push(npc.data.soul3Name);var countEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="soulCount"]');if (countEl) {countEl.value = Math.max(1, npcSouls.length);onFusionSoulCountChange(countEl);}npcSouls.forEach(function(s, si) {var sEl = lastPanel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul' + (si + 1) + '"]');if (sEl) sEl.value = s;});}closePicker();});});document.getElementById('pickerOverlay').classList.add('open');}

    /* ===== Picker/Dropdown系统（完整保留原版） ===== */
    function openFieldPicker(wrapEl){var selectEl=wrapEl.querySelector('select');if(!selectEl)return;var labelEl=wrapEl.closest('.wu-field').querySelector('.wu-field-label');var title=labelEl?labelEl.textContent.trim():'选择';var options=[];for(var i=0;i<selectEl.options.length;i++){var opt=selectEl.options[i];if(opt.value)options.push({value:opt.value,label:opt.textContent});}var curVal=selectEl.value;document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent=title;var h='';options.forEach(function(o){h+='<div class="picker-option'+(o.value===curVal?' active':'')+'\" data-val="'+escHtml(o.value)+'">'+escHtml(o.label)+'</div>';});document.getElementById('pickerBody').innerHTML=h;document.querySelectorAll('#pickerBody .picker-option').forEach(function(opt){opt.addEventListener('click',function(){selectEl.value=this.getAttribute('data-val');closePicker();});});document.getElementById('pickerOverlay').classList.add('open');}
    function openLocPicker(){if(!curLocCategory){alert('请先选择一个地点分类');return;}var locSource=(selectedEra==='斗三'?LOCATIONS_3:(selectedEra==='斗二'?LOCATIONS_2:LOCATIONS));var locs=locSource[curLocCategory]||[];if(curLocCategory==='自定义地点'){locs=[];_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era===selectedEra&&we.wtype==='location')locs.push(we.displayName);});}else if(curLocCategory==='自定义学院'){locs=[];_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era===selectedEra&&we.wtype==='academy')locs.push(we.displayName);});}var curVal=document.getElementById('locPickerBtn').getAttribute('data-value')||'';document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent=curLocCategory+' · 选择地点';var h='';locs.forEach(function(loc){h+='<div class="picker-option'+(loc===curVal?' active':'')+'\" data-val="'+escHtml(loc)+'">'+escHtml(loc)+'</div>';});document.getElementById('pickerBody').innerHTML=h;document.querySelectorAll('#pickerBody .picker-option').forEach(function(opt){opt.addEventListener('click',function(){var val=this.getAttribute('data-val');var btn=document.getElementById('locPickerBtn');btn.textContent=val;btn.setAttribute('data-value',val);btn.classList.remove('placeholder');closePicker();});});document.getElementById('pickerOverlay').classList.add('open');}
    function closePicker(){document.getElementById('pickerOverlay').classList.remove('open');}
    function toggleDropdown(id){var wrap=document.getElementById(id);var trigger=wrap.querySelector('.select-trigger');var dd=wrap.querySelector('.select-dropdown');var isOpen=dd.classList.contains('open');closeAllDropdowns();if(!isOpen){dd.classList.add('open');trigger.classList.add('open');}}
    function closeAllDropdowns(){document.querySelectorAll('.select-dropdown.open').forEach(function(d){d.classList.remove('open');d.closest('.custom-select').querySelector('.select-trigger').classList.remove('open');});}
    function selectOption(id,el){var wrap=document.getElementById(id);wrap.querySelectorAll('.select-option').forEach(function(o){o.classList.remove('active')});el.classList.add('active');var valSpan=wrap.querySelector('.select-value');valSpan.textContent=el.getAttribute('data-label')||el.textContent;valSpan.classList.remove('placeholder');wrap.setAttribute('data-value',el.getAttribute('data-value'));closeAllDropdowns();var cbName=wrap.getAttribute('data-onchange');if(cbName&&window[cbName])window[cbName]();}
    function getSelectVal(id){return document.getElementById(id).getAttribute('data-value')||'';}
    function updateCoinSystem(){var classic = document.getElementById('coinSystemClassic');var federal = document.getElementById('coinSystemFederal');if(classic) classic.style.display = (selectedEra === '斗三') ? 'none' : '';if(federal) federal.style.display = (selectedEra === '斗三') ? '' : 'none';}

    function rebuildTimeline(){var tl=(selectedEra==='斗三')?TL3:((selectedEra==='斗二')?TL2:TL);var dd=document.getElementById('dd-chapter');dd.innerHTML='';var keys=Object.keys(tl);keys.forEach(function(ch,idx){var opt=document.createElement('div');opt.className='select-option'+(idx===0?' active':'');opt.setAttribute('data-value',ch);opt.setAttribute('data-label',ch);opt.innerHTML='<span class="opt-time">'+idx+'</span>'+ch;opt.onclick=function(){selectOption('cs-chapter',this)};dd.appendChild(opt);});var firstKey=keys[0]||'';document.getElementById('cs-chapter').setAttribute('data-value',firstKey);document.querySelector('#cs-chapter .select-value').textContent=firstKey;document.querySelector('#cs-chapter .select-value').classList.remove('placeholder');updEvents();}
    function updEvents(){var tl=(selectedEra==='斗三')?TL3:((selectedEra==='斗二')?TL2:TL);var ch=getSelectVal('cs-chapter');var evts=tl[ch]||[];var dd=document.getElementById('dd-event');dd.innerHTML='';evts.forEach(function(ev,i){var opt=document.createElement('div');opt.className='select-option'+(i===0?' active':'');opt.setAttribute('data-value',ev);var timeMatch=ev.match(/^【([^】]+)】\s*/);var timeStr=timeMatch?timeMatch[1]:'';var eventStr=timeMatch?ev.replace(timeMatch[0],''):ev;opt.setAttribute('data-label',ev);var inner='';if(timeStr)inner+='<span class="opt-time">'+timeStr+'</span>';inner+='<span class="opt-event-text">'+eventStr+'</span>';opt.innerHTML=inner;opt.onclick=function(){selectOption('cs-event',this)};dd.appendChild(opt);});var firstEvt=evts[0]||'';document.getElementById('cs-event').setAttribute('data-value',firstEvt);var evValSpan=document.querySelector('#cs-event .select-value');evValSpan.textContent=firstEvt||'无事件';if(firstEvt)evValSpan.classList.remove('placeholder');}
    function rebuildLocations(){var locs=(selectedEra==='斗三')?LOCATIONS_3:((selectedEra==='斗二')?LOCATIONS_2:LOCATIONS);var area=document.getElementById('locCategory');var h='';Object.keys(locs).forEach(function(cat){h+='<button class="loc-cat-btn" data-cat="'+cat+'" onclick="selectLocCat(this)">'+cat+'</button>';});var hasCustomLoc=false;var hasCustomAcademy=false;_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era===selectedEra){if(we.wtype==='location')hasCustomLoc=true;if(we.wtype==='academy')hasCustomAcademy=true;}});if(hasCustomLoc){h+='<button class="loc-cat-btn" data-cat="自定义地点" onclick="selectLocCat(this)" style="border-color:var(--world);color:var(--world)">★ 自定义地点</button>';}if(hasCustomAcademy){h+='<button class="loc-cat-btn" data-cat="自定义学院" onclick="selectLocCat(this)" style="border-color:var(--academy);color:var(--academy)">★ 自定义学院</button>';}area.innerHTML=h;var btn=document.getElementById('locPickerBtn');btn.textContent='请先选择分类';btn.setAttribute('data-value','');btn.classList.add('placeholder');curLocCategory='';}
    function selectLocCat(btn){document.querySelectorAll('.loc-cat-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});btn.classList.add('active');curLocCategory=btn.getAttribute('data-cat');var locBtn=document.getElementById('locPickerBtn');locBtn.textContent='点击选择'+curLocCategory+'地点';locBtn.setAttribute('data-value','');locBtn.classList.add('placeholder');}
    function buildAffiliationOptions(){var canonFactions = (selectedEra==='斗三') ? CANON_FACTIONS_3 : ((selectedEra === '斗二') ? CANON_FACTIONS_2 : CANON_FACTIONS_1);var canonGroup = document.getElementById('affiliationCanonGroup');var customGroup = document.getElementById('affiliationCustomGroup');if(canonGroup){canonGroup.innerHTML = '';canonFactions.forEach(function(f){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = f; opt.textContent = f;canonGroup.appendChild(opt);});}if(customGroup){customGroup.innerHTML = '';_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era === selectedEra && (we.wtype === 'faction' || we.wtype === 'academy')){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = '[自定义]' + we.displayName;opt.textContent = '★ ' + we.displayName;customGroup.appendChild(opt);}});}var stCanon = document.getElementById('stationCanonGroup');var stCustom = document.getElementById('stationCustomGroup');var locs = (selectedEra==='斗三') ?     function buildWorldLocationOptions(){var era = worldElementEra;var locs = (era==='斗三') ? LOCATIONS_3 : ((era === '斗二') ? LOCATIONS_2 : LOCATIONS);var canonFactions = (era==='斗三') ? CANON_FACTIONS_3 : ((era === '斗二') ? CANON_FACTIONS_2 : CANON_FACTIONS_1);var wfCanon = document.getElementById('wfLocCanonGroup');var wfCustom = document.getElementById('wfLocCustomGroup');if(wfCanon){wfCanon.innerHTML = '';Object.keys(locs).forEach(function(cat){locs[cat].forEach(function(loc){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = loc; opt.textContent = loc;wfCanon.appendChild(opt);});});}if(wfCustom){wfCustom.innerHTML = '';_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era === era && we.wtype === 'location'){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = '[自定义]' + we.displayName;opt.textContent = '★ ' + we.displayName;wfCustom.appendChild(opt);}});}var waCanon = document.getElementById('waLocCanonGroup');var waCustom = document.getElementById('waLocCustomGroup');if(waCanon){waCanon.innerHTML = '';var cities = locs['城镇'] || locs['帝国与城镇'] || locs['联邦城市'] || [];cities.forEach(function(c){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = c; opt.textContent = c;waCanon.appendChild(opt);});}if(waCustom){waCustom.innerHTML = '';_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era === era && we.wtype === 'location'){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = '[自定义]' + we.displayName;opt.textContent = '★ ' + we.displayName;waCustom.appendChild(opt);}});}var wlFacCanon = document.getElementById('wlFacCanonGroup');var wlFacCustom = document.getElementById('wlFacCustomGroup');if(wlFacCanon){wlFacCanon.innerHTML = '';canonFactions.forEach(function(f){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = f; opt.textContent = f;wlFacCanon.appendChild(opt);});}if(wlFacCustom){wlFacCustom.innerHTML = '';_loadedWorldElements.forEach(function(we){if(we.era === era && we.wtype === 'faction'){var opt = document.createElement('option');opt.value = '[自定义]' + we.displayName;opt.textContent = '★ ' + we.displayName;wlFacCustom.appendChild(opt);}});}}
    function buildWorldNpcOptions(selectEl){if(!selectEl) return;var era = worldElementEra;var h = '<option value="">手动输入...</option>';_loadedNpcList.forEach(function(npc){if(npc.era === era){h += '<option value="' + escHtml(npc.displayName) + '">' + escHtml(npc.displayName) + '</option>';}});selectEl.innerHTML = h;}
    function addWfMember(){var container = document.getElementById('wfMemberList');if(!container) return;var h = '<div class="member-item">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.member-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">选择NPC</div><select class="wu-field-select" data-wfm="npc" onchange="onWfMemberSelect(this)"><option value="">手动输入...</option></select></div><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">或手动输入</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wfm="name" placeholder="成员名字"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">职位</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wfm="role" placeholder="如：长老、核心弟子"></div><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">简介</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wfm="desc" placeholder="一句话描述"></div></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);var lastItem = container.querySelector('.member-item:last-child');if(lastItem){var selectEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-wfm="npc"]');buildWorldNpcOptions(selectEl);}}
    function onWfMemberSelect(selectEl){var item = selectEl.closest('.member-item');var nameInput = item.querySelector('[data-wfm="name"]');if(selectEl.value){ nameInput.value = selectEl.value; nameInput.readOnly = true; }else{ nameInput.value = ''; nameInput.readOnly = false; }}
    function onWfLeaderSelect(selectEl){var nameInput = document.getElementById('f-wf-leader-name');if(selectEl.value){ nameInput.value = selectEl.value; nameInput.readOnly = true; }else{ nameInput.value = ''; nameInput.readOnly = false; }}
    function addWaTeacher(){var container = document.getElementById('waTeacherList');if(!container) return;var h = '<div class="member-item">';h += '<div class="mini-actions"><button class="mini-act-btn danger" title="删除" onclick="this.closest(\'.member-item\').remove()">' + SVG_TRASH + '</button></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">选择NPC</div><select class="wu-field-select" data-wat="npc" onchange="onWaTeacherSelect(this)"><option value="">手动输入...</option></select></div><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">或手动输入</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wat="name" placeholder="教师名字"></div></div>';h += '<div class="uni-row-2"><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">教授科目</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wat="subject" placeholder="如：格斗、魂导器"></div><div class="wu-field"><div class="wu-field-label">简介</div><input class="wu-field-input" data-wat="desc" placeholder="一句话描述"></div></div>';h += '</div>';container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', h);var lastItem = container.querySelector('.member-item:last-child');if(lastItem){var selectEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-wat="npc"]');buildWorldNpcOptions(selectEl);}}

    function startNewWorldElement(){createMode = 'world';worldElementType = '';worldElementEra = '斗一';maxStepReached = 1;refreshSteps();showPane('world-type');onPaneEnter('world-type');}

    function setWorldEra(btn){var parent = btn.closest('.world-card') || btn.closest('.card');if(parent){parent.querySelectorAll('.era-btn').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active');});}btn.classList.add('active');worldElementEra = btn.getAttribute('data-era');}
    function updateWorldPanelVisibility(){var t = worldElementType;var fb = document.getElementById('worldFactionBasic');var ab = document.getElementById('worldAcademyBasic');var lb = document.getElementById('worldLocationBasic');if(fb) fb.style.display = (t==='faction')?'':'none';if(ab) ab.style.display = (t==='academy')?'':'none';if(lb) lb.style.display = (t==='location')?'':'none';var fd = document.getElementById('worldFactionDetail');var ad = document.getElementById('worldAcademyDetail');var ld = document.getElementById('worldLocationDetail');if(fd) fd.style.display = (t==='faction')?'':'none';if(ad) ad.style.display = (t==='academy')?'':'none';if(ld) ld.style.display = (t==='location')?'':'none';var fr = document.getElementById('worldFactionRelations');var ar = document.getElementById('worldAcademyRelations');var lr = document.getElementById('worldLocationRelations');if(fr) fr.style.display = (t==='faction')?'':'none';if(ar) ar.style.display = (t==='academy')?'':'none';if(lr) lr.style.display = (t==='location')?'':'none';var typeNames = {'faction':'势力 / 宗门','academy':'学院','location':'地点 / 区域'};var tn = typeNames[t] || '世界观元素';var bt = document.getElementById('worldBasicTitle'); if(bt) bt.textContent = tn + ' · 基础信息';var dt = document.getElementById('worldDetailTitle'); if(dt) dt.textContent = tn + ' · 详细配置';var rt = document.getElementById('worldRelationsTitle'); if(rt) rt.textContent = tn + ' · 成员与关系';}

    /* ===== 第10楼：面板导航 + 表单收集/恢复/重置 + API + AI函数 ============== */
