    async function aiGenFullIntro(btnRef) {await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function() {var outline = document.getElementById('f-intro').value.trim();var direction = document.getElementById('f-intro-direction').value.trim();if (!outline && !direction) { throw new Error('请先填写开场白方向或大纲'); }var fd = collectFormData();var _dn = getDisplayName(fd);var introTones = collectToneTags('introToneTags');var pronoun = '他';if (fd.race === '人类') { pronoun = isFemaleGender(fd.gender) ? '她' : '他'; }else { pronoun = isFemaleBeastSex(fd.beastSex) ? '她' : '他'; }var prompt = '根据以下大纲和角色信息，生成一段500-800字的开场白叙事。\n\n';if(introTones.length > 0){prompt += '【铁律】基调风格：' + introTones.join('、') + '\n\n';}prompt += '要求：\n1. 第三人称视角，以「' + _dn + '」称呼主角，代词用「' + pronoun + '」\n2. 纯粹的叙事文字，有画面感\n3. 以一个悬念或转折收尾\n4. 禁止列出角色数据\n5. 不要使用模糊词和微表情\n6. 场景必须与降临章节、时间、地点吻合\n\n';prompt += '角色关键信息：时代'+fd.era+'，等级'+fd.level+'级'+rankName(parseFloat(fd.level));if(fd.soul1Name)prompt+='，武魂'+fd.soul1Name;if(fd.chapter)prompt+='，降临章节：'+fd.chapter;if(fd.location||fd.locCustom)prompt+='，地点：'+(fd.location||fd.locCustom||'');prompt+='\n\n';prompt += '大纲/方向：\n' + (outline || direction) + '\n';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt, 4000);if (result) {document.getElementById('f-full-intro').value = result.trim();}});}

    async function aiGenCoins(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var fd=collectFormData();var prompt='';if(selectedEra==='斗三'){prompt='根据角色身份和等级，生成合理的龙王传说时代初始资金。\n身份：'+fd.identity+'\n等级：'+fd.level+'级\n输出格式：\n联邦币：数字\n贡献点：数字\n银联币：数字';}else{prompt='根据角色身份和等级，生成合理的初始魂币数额。\n身份：'+fd.identity+'\n等级：'+fd.level+'级\n输出格式：\n金币：数字\n银币：数字\n铜币：数字';}var result=await callAI(SYS_GENERAL,prompt);if(result){if(selectedEra==='斗三'){var fcm=result.match(/联邦币[：:]\s*(\d+)/);var ctm=result.match(/贡献点[：:]\s*(\d+)/);var slcm=result.match(/银联币[：:]\s*(\d+)/);if(fcm)(document.getElementById('inv-fc')||{}).value=parseInt(fcm[1]);if(ctm)(document.getElementById('inv-contribution')||{}).value=parseInt(ctm[1]);if(slcm)(document.getElementById('inv-slc')||{}).value=parseInt(slcm[1]);}else{var gm=result.match(/金币[：:]\s*(\d+)/);var sm=result.match(/银币[：:]\s*(\d+)/);var cm=result.match(/铜币[：:]\s*(\d+)/);if(gm)document.getElementById('inv-gold').value=parseInt(gm[1]);if(sm)document.getElementById('inv-silver').value=parseInt(sm[1]);if(cm)document.getElementById('inv-copper').value=parseInt(cm[1]);}}});}

    async function aiGenInvBySelected(category, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var tagContainerMap={'outfits':'invOutfitSelectTags','soulDevices':'invSoulDevSelectTags','herbs':'invHerbSelectTags','food':'invFoodSelectTags','others':'invOtherSelectTags'};var countMap={'outfits':'aiCountOutfits','herbs':'aiCountHerbs','food':'aiCountFood','others':'aiCountOthers','soulDevices':'aiCountSoulDevices'};var catNames={'outfits':'服饰/穿着','herbs':'草药/天材地宝','food':'食物/消耗品','others':'杂物','soulDevices':'魂导器'};var selectedTags=getSelectedInvTags(tagContainerMap[category]||'');if(selectedTags.length===0){throw new Error('请先选择至少一个标签');}var count=getAiCount(countMap[category]||'aiCountOutfits');var fd=collectFormData();var prompt='为斗罗大陆的角色生成'+count+'个'+catNames[category]+'。\n角色等级：'+fd.level+'级\n时代：'+fd.era+'\n';prompt+='【铁律】必须为以下选中的类型生成物品：'+selectedTags.join('、')+'。\n';if(category==='soulDevices'){prompt+='每个魂导器格式：\n名称：（2-6字）\n等级：（1-10数字）\n外观：（30-60字）\n作用：（30-60字）\n';}else{prompt+='每个物品格式：\n名称：（2-6字）\n数量：（数字）\n描述：（30-60字）\n';}if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result=await callAI(SYS_GENERAL,prompt);if(result) parseAndAddInvItems(result, category);});}

    async function aiGenInvByCustom(category, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var customMap={'outfits':'invOutfitCustom','soulDevices':'invSoulDevCustom','spareBones':'invSpareBoneCustom','herbs':'invHerbCustom','food':'invFoodCustom','others':'invOtherCustom'};var customInput=document.getElementById(customMap[category]||'');var customDesc=(customInput?customInput.value:'').trim();if(!customDesc){throw new Error('请输入自定义描述');}var prompt='为斗罗大陆的角色生成1个物品。\n';prompt+='【铁律】必须按照以下用户描述生成：'+customDesc+'\n';if(category==='spareBones'){prompt+='格式：\n部位：\n名称：\n来源魂兽：\n年限：\n魂技壹：\n详情：';}else if(category==='soulDevices'){prompt+='格式：\n名称：\n等级：\n外观：\n作用：';}else{prompt+='格式：\n名称：\n数量：\n描述：';}var result=await callAI(SYS_GENERAL,prompt);if(result){if(category==='spareBones') parseAndAddSpareBone(result);else parseAndAddInvItems(result, category);if(customInput)customInput.value='';}});}

    async function aiGenInvRandom(category, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var countMap={'outfits':'aiCountOutfits','herbs':'aiCountHerbs','food':'aiCountFood','others':'aiCountOthers','soulDevices':'aiCountSoulDevices','spareBones':'aiCountSpareBone'};var catNames={'outfits':'服饰/穿着','herbs':'草药/天材地宝','food':'食物/消耗品','others':'杂物','soulDevices':'魂导器','spareBones':'备用魂骨'};var count=getAiCount(countMap[category]||'aiCountOutfits');var fd=collectFormData();var prompt='为斗罗大陆的角色随机生成'+count+'个'+catNames[category]+'。\n角色等级：'+fd.level+'级\n完全随机发挥。\n';if(category==='soulDevices'){prompt+='每个格式：\n名称：\n等级：\n外观：\n作用：\n';}else if(category==='spareBones'){prompt+='每个格式：\n部位：\n名称：\n来源魂兽：\n年限：\n魂技壹：\n详情：\n';}else{prompt+='每个格式：\n名称：\n数量：\n描述：\n';}if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result=await callAI(SYS_GENERAL,prompt);if(result){if(category==='spareBones'){var secs=splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec){parseAndAddSpareBone(sec);});}else{parseAndAddInvItems(result, category);}}});}

    async function aiGenSpareBone(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var count=getAiCount('aiCountSpareBone');var prompt='为斗罗大陆的角色生成'+count+'个备用魂骨。\n每个格式：\n部位：\n名称：\n来源魂兽：\n年限：\n魂技壹：\n详情：\n';if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result=await callAI(SYS_BONE,prompt);if(result){var secs=splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec){parseAndAddSpareBone(sec);});}});}

    async function aiGenCharDesc(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var fd=collectFormData();var pronoun='他';if(fd.race==='人类'){pronoun=isFemaleGender(fd.gender)?'她':'他';}else{pronoun=isFemaleBeastSex(fd.beastSex)?'她':'他';}var prompt='根据以下角色配置数据，生成一段400-600字的完整角色描述。\n\n';prompt+='要求：\n1. 第三人称，以角色姓名称呼\n2. 性别代词使用\"'+pronoun+'\"\n3. 覆盖：性格→爱好→外貌→穿着→身体数据(如有)\n4. 不要照抄标签，要扩充成具体文字\n5. NSFW数据正常写入\n6. 连贯自然\n\n角色数据：\n';if(fd.race==='人类'){prompt+='姓名：'+fd.name+'\n性别：'+fd.gender+'\n年龄：'+fd.age+'岁\n';if(fd.personality)prompt+='性格：'+fd.personality+'\n';}var result=await callAI(SYS_GENERAL,prompt,4000);if(result){document.getElementById('f-char-desc').value=result.trim();updateCharDescCount();}});}

    async function aiGenSpeechSamples(btnRef) {await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function() {var personality = document.getElementById('f-personality').value.trim();var tone = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('speechToneTags') || document.createElement('div'));var count = getAiCount('aiCountSpeech');if (!personality && tone.length === 0) { throw new Error('请先填写性格或选择语气标签'); }var prompt = '为以下角色生成' + count + '条示范对话。\n';if (personality) prompt += '性格：' + personality + '\n';if (tone.length > 0) prompt += '语气风格：' + tone.join('、') + '\n';prompt += '\n每条格式：\n场景：（5-15字）\n台词：（原话，15-40字，纯粹的对话）\n多条用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if (result) {var secs = splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec) {sec = sec.trim(); if (!sec) return;addSpeechSample();var container = document.getElementById('speechSampleList');var lastItem = container.querySelector('.custom-skill-item:last-child');if (lastItem) {var sceneMatch = sec.match(/场景[：:]\s*(.+)/);var lineMatch = sec.match(/台词[：:]\s*(.+)/);var scEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ss="scene"]');var lnEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-ss="line"]');if (scEl && sceneMatch) scEl.value = sceneMatch[1].trim();if (lnEl && lineMatch) lnEl.value = lineMatch[1].trim();}});}});}

    async function aiGenBeastSpeechSamples(btnRef) {await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function() {var species = (document.getElementById('f-beast-species') || {}).value || '';var prompt = '为以下化形魂兽角色生成3条示范对话。\n种族：' + species + '\n每条格式：\n场景：（5-15字）\n台词：（原话，15-40字）\n多条用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if (result) {var secs = splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec) {sec = sec.trim(); if (!sec) return;addBeastSpeechSample();var container = document.getElementById('beastSpeechSampleList');var lastItem = container.querySelector('.custom-skill-item:last-child');if (lastItem) {var sceneMatch = sec.match(/场景[：:]\s*(.+)/);var lineMatch = sec.match(/台词[：:]\s*(.+)/);var scEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-bss="scene"]');var lnEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-bss="line"]');if (scEl && sceneMatch) scEl.value = sceneMatch[1].trim();if (lnEl && lineMatch) lnEl.value = lineMatch[1].trim();}});}});}

    async function aiGenFusionForTarget(btnEl) {await withAiLoading(btnEl, async function() {var panel = btnEl.closest('.fusion-target-panel');var targetName = (panel.querySelector('[data-ft="name"]') || {}).value || '';if (!targetName.trim()) throw new Error('请先填写NPC姓名');var npcCount = parseInt((panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soulCount"]') || {}).value) || 1;var npcSouls = [];for (var i = 1; i <= npcCount; i++) {var sEl = panel.querySelector('[data-ft="soul' + i + '"]');if (sEl && sEl.value.trim()) npcSouls.push(sEl.value.trim());}if (npcSouls.length === 0) throw new Error('请至少填写一个NPC武魂名称');var myWuInfo = getMyWuInfo();if (myWuInfo.length === 0) throw new Error('请先在武魂信息中填写你的武魂');var prompt = '为以下两位魂师生成武魂融合技。\n\n我方武魂：\n';myWuInfo.forEach(function(w, idx) { prompt += '武魂' + (idx+1) + '：' + w.name + '\n'; });prompt += '\nNPC「' + targetName + '」的武魂：\n';npcSouls.forEach(function(s, idx) { prompt += '武魂' + (idx+1) + '：' + s + '\n'; });prompt += '\n每个融合技格式：\n我方武魂：\n对方武魂：\n融合技名称：（2-6字）\n融合技效果：（30-80字）\n多个用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if (result) {var resultsArea = panel.querySelector('[data-ft="results"]');if (resultsArea) resultsArea.innerHTML = '';var secs = splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec) {var mySoul = extractField(sec, '我方武魂');var targetSoul = extractField(sec, '对方武魂');var fusName = extractField(sec, '融合技名称');var fusEffect = extractField(sec, '融合技效果');if (fusName || fusEffect) {var rh = buildFusionResultItem(mySoul, targetSoul, fusName, fusEffect);if (resultsArea) resultsArea.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', rh);}});}});}

    async function aiGenSelfFusion(btnRef) {await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function() {var myWu = getMyWuList();if (myWu.length < 2) throw new Error('至少需要两个武魂');var prompt = '为以下角色生成自体武魂融合技。\n武魂：' + myWu.join('、') + '\n每个格式：\n武魂壹：\n武魂贰：\n融合技名称：（2-6字）\n融合技效果：（30-80字）\n多个用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if (result) {var combos = [];for (var ci = 0; ci < myWu.length; ci++) {for (var cj = ci + 1; cj < myWu.length; cj++) {combos.push(myWu[ci] + '×' + myWu[cj]);}}var secs = splitSections(result);var comboIdx = 0;secs.forEach(function(sec) {var fusName = extractField(sec, '融合技名称');var fusEffect = extractField(sec, '融合技效果');if (fusName && comboIdx < combos.length) {var comboEl = document.querySelector('.self-fusion-combo[data-sf-combo="' + combos[comboIdx] + '"]');if (comboEl) {var nameEl = comboEl.querySelector('[data-sf="name"]');var effectEl = comboEl.querySelector('[data-sf="effect"]');if (nameEl) nameEl.value = fusName;if (effectEl) effectEl.value = fusEffect || '';}comboIdx++;}});}});}

    async function aiGenWorldBasic(wtype, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var data = collectWorldData();var prompt = '请为以下斗罗大陆（'+data.era+'时代）的世界观元素补全未填的基础信息。已有信息保留不变。\n\n';if(wtype==='faction'){if(data.name) prompt += '势力名称已确定：'+data.name+'\n';prompt += '\n输出格式：\n势力名称：\n类型：\n总部位置：\n势力范围：（30-60字）\n历史由来：（50-100字）\n';}else if(wtype==='academy'){if(data.name) prompt += '学院名称已确定：'+data.name+'\n';prompt += '\n输出格式：\n学院名称：\n所在城市：\n等级：\n招生条件：\n学院简介：（50-100字）\n';}else if(wtype==='location'){if(data.name) prompt += '地点名称已确定：'+data.name+'\n';prompt += '\n输出格式：\n地点名称：\n地点类型：\n地理位置：\n环境描述：（50-80字）\n';}var result = await callAI(SYS_WORLD, prompt);if(result){var resultAreaId = {'faction':'wfBasicAiResult','academy':'waBasicAiResult','location':'wlBasicAiResult'};var area = document.getElementById(resultAreaId[wtype]);if(area) area.innerHTML = '<div class="ai-result-box">' + escHtml(result).replace(/\n/g,'<br>') + '</div>';if(wtype==='faction'){var fn = extractField(result,'势力名称'); if(fn && !data.name)(document.getElementById('f-wf-name')||{}).value=fn;var ft = extractField(result,'势力范围'); if(ft && !(document.getElementById('f-wf-territory')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wf-territory')||{}).value=ft;var fh = extractField(result,'历史由来'); if(fh && !(document.getElementById('f-wf-history')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wf-history')||{}).value=fh;}else if(wtype==='academy'){var an = extractField(result,'学院名称'); if(an && !data.name)(document.getElementById('f-wa-name')||{}).value=an;var aa = extractField(result,'招生条件'); if(aa && !(document.getElementById('f-wa-admission')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wa-admission')||{}).value=aa;var ai2 = extractField(result,'学院简介'); if(ai2 && !(document.getElementById('f-wa-intro')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wa-intro')||{}).value=ai2;}else if(wtype==='location'){var ln = extractField(result,'地点名称'); if(ln && !data.name)(document.getElementById('f-wl-name')||{}).value=ln;var lg = extractField(result,'地理位置'); if(lg && !(document.getElementById('f-wl-geo')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wl-geo')||{}).value=lg;var le = extractField(result,'环境描述'); if(le && !(document.getElementById('f-wl-env')||{}).value)(document.getElementById('f-wl-env')||{}).value=le;}}});}

    async function aiGenWorldDetail(wtype, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var data = collectWorldData();var prompt = '请为斗罗大陆（'+data.era+'时代）的「'+(data.name||'未知')+'」补全详细配置。\n\n';if(wtype==='faction'){prompt += '输出格式：\n特色武魂/修炼体系：（30-60字）\n独门魂技/秘术：（30-60字）\n经济来源：（20-40字）\n势力规矩：（30-60字）\n';}else if(wtype==='academy'){prompt += '输出格式：\n独门功法：（30-60字）\n特色课程：（30-60字）\n毕业要求：（20-40字）\n校园设施：（30-60字）\n';}else if(wtype==='location'){prompt += '输出格式：\n魂兽分布：（30-60字）\n天材地宝：（30-60字）\n特殊事件/传说：（30-60字）\n';}var result = await callAI(SYS_WORLD, prompt);if(result){var resultAreaId = {'faction':'wfDetailAiResult','academy':'waDetailAiResult','location':'wlDetailAiResult'};var area = document.getElementById(resultAreaId[wtype]);if(area) area.innerHTML = '<div class="ai-result-box">' + escHtml(result).replace(/\n/g,'<br>') + '</div>';}});}

    async function aiGenGodhood(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var lv = parseInt(document.getElementById('f-level').value) || 100;var godLvName = getGodLevelName(lv);var fd = collectFormData();var prompt = '为一位斗罗大陆'+fd.era+'时代的'+godLvName+'生成神位设定。\n';if(fd.soul1Name)prompt+='武魂：'+fd.soul1Name+'\n';prompt+='输出格式：\n神位名称：（2-4字）\n神位类型：（传承/自创/夺取）\n神位描述：（30-60字）\n神装外观：（50字左右描述外观）\n神技壹：（名称2-4字）\n效果壹：（30-60字）\n神技贰：（名称2-4字）\n效果贰：（30-60字）';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result){addGodPosition();var container = document.getElementById('godPositionList');var lastCard = container.querySelector('.godpos-card:last-child');if(lastCard){var uid = lastCard.getAttribute('data-gp-uid');var gpName = extractField(result,'神位名称');var gpType = extractField(result,'神位类型');var gpDesc = extractField(result,'神位描述');var gpArmor = extractField(result,'神装外观');var nameEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="name"]'); if(nameEl&&gpName)nameEl.value=gpName;if(gpType) setActiveSingleTag(uid+'_type',gpType);var descEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="desc"]'); if(descEl&&gpDesc)descEl.value=gpDesc;var armorEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-gp="armor"]'); if(armorEl&&gpArmor)armorEl.value=gpArmor;var sk1 = extractField(result,'神技壹');var ef1 = extractField(result,'效果壹');var sk2 = extractField(result,'神技贰');var ef2 = extractField(result,'效果贰');if(sk1){addGodSkill(uid);var skillContainer = document.getElementById('godSkills_'+uid);var ls = skillContainer.querySelector('.god-skill-item:last-child');if(ls){var snEl=ls.querySelector('[data-gs="name"]');if(snEl)snEl.value=sk1;var sdEl=ls.querySelector('[data-gs="desc"]');if(sdEl&&ef1)sdEl.value=ef1;}}if(sk2){addGodSkill(uid);var skillContainer2 = document.getElementById('godSkills_'+uid);var ls2 = skillContainer2.querySelector('.god-skill-item:last-child');if(ls2){var snEl2=ls2.querySelector('[data-gs="name"]');if(snEl2)snEl2.value=sk2;var sdEl2=ls2.querySelector('[data-gs="desc"]');if(sdEl2&&ef2)sdEl2.value=ef2;}}}document.getElementById('godAiResult').innerHTML = '<div class="ai-result-box">' + escHtml(result).replace(/\n/g,'<br>') + '</div>';}});}

    async function aiGenGodArmor(gpUid, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var card = document.querySelector('.godpos-card[data-gp-uid="'+gpUid+'"]');var gpName = card?(card.querySelector('[data-gp="name"]')||{}).value:'';var prompt = '为斗罗大陆的「'+(gpName||'未知')+'」神位生成神装外观描述。50字左右，描述外观、颜色、纹路特征。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result){var el = card?card.querySelector('[data-gp="armor"]'):null;if(el) el.value = result.trim();}});}

    async function aiGenGodSkills(gpUid, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var count = getAiCount('aiCountGS_'+gpUid);var card = document.querySelector('.godpos-card[data-gp-uid="'+gpUid+'"]');var gpName = card?(card.querySelector('[data-gp="name"]')||{}).value:'';var prompt = '为斗罗大陆的「'+(gpName||'未知')+'」神位生成'+count+'个神技。\n每个格式：\n神技名称：（2-4字）\n效果：（30-60字）\n';if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result){var secs = splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec){sec=sec.trim();if(!sec)return;addGodSkill(gpUid);var skillContainer = document.getElementById('godSkills_'+gpUid);var lastItem = skillContainer.querySelector('.god-skill-item:last-child');if(lastItem){var n = sec.match(/神技名称[：:]\s*(.+)/);var d = sec.match(/效果[：:]\s*(.+)/);var nEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-gs="name"]');if(nEl&&n)nEl.value=n[1].trim();var dEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-gs="desc"]');if(dEl&&d)dEl.value=d[1].trim();}});}});}

    async function aiGenMechaBySelected(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var selectedTags = getSelectedInvTags('mechaPreSelectTags');if(selectedTags.length===0) throw new Error('请先选择至少一个等级标签');var count = getAiCount('aiCountMecha');var prompt = '为龙王传说时代的角色生成'+count+'个机甲。\n等级必须是：'+selectedTags.join('、')+'\n每个格式：\n机甲名称：（2-6字）\n机甲等级：（白级/黄级/紫级/黑级/红级/神级）\n机甲描述：（30-60字描述外观和特点）\n';if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result) parseAndAddMechaItems(result);});}

    async function aiGenMechaByCustom(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var customInput = document.getElementById('mechaCustomDesc');var desc = (customInput?customInput.value:'').trim();if(!desc) throw new Error('请输入自定义描述');var prompt = '为龙王传说生成1个机甲。\n【铁律】按以下描述生成：'+desc+'\n格式：\n机甲名称：\n机甲等级：（白级/黄级/紫级/黑级/红级/神级）\n机甲描述：（30-60字）';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result){parseAndAddMechaItems(result);if(customInput)customInput.value='';}});}

    async function aiGenMechaRandom(btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var count = getAiCount('aiCountMecha');var prompt = '为龙王传说随机生成'+count+'个机甲。\n每个格式：\n机甲名称：\n机甲等级：\n机甲描述：\n';if(count>1)prompt+='多个用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result) parseAndAddMechaItems(result);});}

    function parseAndAddMechaItems(result){
        var secs = splitSections(result);
        secs.forEach(function(sec){
            sec = sec.trim(); if(!sec) return;
            addMechaItem();
            var container = document.getElementById('mechaList');
            var lastItem = container.querySelector('.mecha-item:last-child');
            if(lastItem){
                var uid = lastItem.getAttribute('data-mecha-uid') || '';
                var n = extractField(sec,'机甲名称');
                var lv = extractField(sec,'机甲等级');
                var d = extractField(sec,'机甲描述');
                var nEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-mecha="name"]');
                if(nEl&&n)nEl.value=n;
                if(lv){
                    document.querySelectorAll('#'+uid+'_level .mecha-level-chip').forEach(function(c){
                        c.classList.toggle('active', c.textContent.trim() === lv);
                    });
                }
                var dEl = lastItem.querySelector('[data-mecha="desc"]');
                if(dEl&&d)dEl.value=d;
            }
        });
    }

    async function aiGenSpiritsForWu(soulNum, btnRef){await withAiLoading(btnRef, async function(){var soulName = document.getElementById('f-soul' + soulNum + '-name').value.trim();if(!soulName) throw new Error('请先填写武魂名称');var count = getAiCount('aiCountSpirit'+soulNum);var prompt = '为龙王传说时代的角色生成'+count+'个魂灵。\n武魂：' + soulName + '\n\n每个魂灵格式：\n魂灵名称：（2-6字）\n魂灵类型：（人造魂灵 或 魂灵）\n来源种族：（什么魂兽制成的魂灵）\n魂灵年限：（如5000年）\n外观：（30-50字描述魂灵的外形）\n能力：（30-60字描述魂灵自带的战斗能力）\n\n';if(count>1)prompt+='多个魂灵用 --- 分隔。';var result = await callAI(SYS_GENERAL, prompt);if(result){var secs = splitSections(result);secs.forEach(function(sec){sec = sec.trim(); if(!sec) return;var soulNumStr = soulNum;addSoulSpirit(soulNumStr);var container = document.getElementById('spiritList' + soulNumStr);var lastCard = container.querySelector('.soul-spirit-card:last-child');if(!lastCard) return;var uid = lastCard.getAttribute('data-ss-uid');var sn = extractField(sec, '魂灵名称');var nameEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="name"]'); if(nameEl && sn) nameEl.value = sn;var st = extractField(sec, '魂灵类型');if(st) setActiveSingleTag(uid + '_type', st.indexOf('人造') >= 0 ? '人造魂灵' : '魂灵');var sy = extractField(sec, '魂灵年限');var yearsEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="years"]'); if(yearsEl && sy) yearsEl.value = sy;var sp = extractField(sec, '来源种族');var speciesEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="species"]'); if(speciesEl && sp) speciesEl.value = sp;var appearance = extractField(sec, '外观');var appEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="appearance"]'); if(appEl && appearance) appEl.value = appearance;var ability = extractField(sec, '能力');var abiEl = lastCard.querySelector('[data-ss="ability"]'); if(abiEl && ability) abiEl.value = ability;});updateRingStatusSummary();}});}
