    /* ===== buildExportText v3.1完整版 ===== */
    function buildExportText(fd) {
        var _dn = getDisplayName(fd);
        var ec = _dn + '的斗罗大陆专属设定：\n';
        ec += '时代：' + fd.era + '\n种族：' + fd.race + '\n';
        if (fd.race === '人类') {
            ec += '姓名：' + (fd.name || '未命名') + '\n性别：' + fd.gender + '\n年龄：' + fd.age + '岁\n';
            if (fd.personality) ec += '性格：' + fd.personality + '\n';
            if (fd.hobby) ec += '爱好：' + fd.hobby + '\n';
            var sd = fd.speechData;
            if(sd){if(sd.tone&&sd.tone.length>0)ec+='语气风格：'+sd.tone.join('、')+'\n';if(sd.habit&&sd.habit.length>0)ec+='说话习惯：'+sd.habit.join('、')+'\n';if(sd.catchphrase)ec+='口头禅：'+sd.catchphrase+'\n';if(sd.addressHabit)ec+='称呼习惯：'+sd.addressHabit+'\n';if(sd.samples&&sd.samples.length>0){ec+='示范对话：\n';sd.samples.forEach(function(s){ec+='  ['+s.scene+'] '+s.line+'\n';});}}
            var nsfwP = fd.nsfwPersonality;
            if(nsfwP){if(nsfwP.tags&&nsfwP.tags.length>0)ec+='NSFW性格倾向：'+nsfwP.tags.join('、')+'\n';if(nsfwP.hobbies&&nsfwP.hobbies.length>0)ec+='NSFW爱好：'+nsfwP.hobbies.join('、')+'\n';if(nsfwP.extra)ec+='NSFW补充：'+nsfwP.extra+'\n';}
            var ap=fd.appearance||{};
            if(ap.height)ec+='身高：'+ap.height+'cm\n';if(ap.weight)ec+='体重：'+ap.weight+'kg\n';if(ap.bodyType)ec+='体型：'+ap.bodyType+'\n';if(ap.skinColor)ec+='肤色：'+ap.skinColor+'\n';if(ap.hairColor)ec+='发色：'+ap.hairColor+'\n';if(ap.hairStyle)ec+='发型：'+ap.hairStyle+'\n';if(ap.eyeColor)ec+='瞳色：'+ap.eyeColor+'\n';if(ap.faceShape)ec+='面部轮廓：'+ap.faceShape+'\n';if(ap.faceAura&&ap.faceAura.length>0)ec+='面部气质：'+ap.faceAura.join('、')+'\n';if(ap.specialMarks&&ap.specialMarks.length>0)ec+='特殊标记：'+ap.specialMarks.join('、')+'\n';if(ap.extra)ec+='外貌补充：'+ap.extra+'\n';
            var of2=fd.outfit||{};if(of2.top)ec+='上身穿着：'+of2.top+'\n';if(of2.bottom)ec+='下身穿着：'+of2.bottom+'\n';if(of2.shoes)ec+='鞋子：'+of2.shoes+'\n';if(of2.socks)ec+='袜子：'+of2.socks+'\n';if(of2.accessories)ec+='配饰：'+of2.accessories+'\n';if(of2.mainColor)ec+='服饰主色调：'+of2.mainColor+'\n';if(of2.style)ec+='服饰风格：'+of2.style+'\n';if(of2.extra)ec+='服饰补充：'+of2.extra+'\n';
            if(ap.nsfw){var nsfwKeys=Object.keys(ap.nsfw);var hasNsfw=false;nsfwKeys.forEach(function(k){if(ap.nsfw[k]&&(typeof ap.nsfw[k]==='string'?ap.nsfw[k]:'')!=='')hasNsfw=true;if(Array.isArray(ap.nsfw[k])&&ap.nsfw[k].length>0)hasNsfw=true;});if(hasNsfw){ec+='\nNSFW身体数据：\n';nsfwKeys.forEach(function(k){var v=ap.nsfw[k];if(!v)return;if(Array.isArray(v)){if(v.length>0)ec+='  '+k+'：'+v.join('、')+'\n';}else if(v){ec+='  '+k+'：'+v+'\n';}});}}
            if(ap.nsfwMale){ec+='\nNSFW男性器官数据：\n';Object.keys(ap.nsfwMale).forEach(function(k){var v=ap.nsfwMale[k];if(!v)return;if(Array.isArray(v)){if(v.length>0)ec+='  '+k+'：'+v.join('、')+'\n';}else{ec+='  '+k+'：'+v+'\n';}});}
            if(of2.nsfw){var nsfwOKeys=Object.keys(of2.nsfw);var hasNsfwO=false;nsfwOKeys.forEach(function(k){if(of2.nsfw[k]&&(typeof of2.nsfw[k]==='string'?of2.nsfw[k]:'')!=='')hasNsfwO=true;if(Array.isArray(of2.nsfw[k])&&of2.nsfw[k].length>0)hasNsfwO=true;});if(hasNsfwO){ec+='\nNSFW服饰：\n';nsfwOKeys.forEach(function(k){var v=of2.nsfw[k];if(!v)return;if(Array.isArray(v)){if(v.length>0)ec+='  '+k+'：'+v.join('、')+'\n';}else{ec+='  '+k+'：'+v+'\n';}});}}
        } else {
            ec += '魂兽种族：' + (fd.beastSpecies || '未知') + '\n';
            if (fd.beastName) ec += '个人名字：' + fd.beastName + '\n';
            ec += '雌雄：' + fd.beastSex + '\n修为年限：' + (fd.beastYears || '未知') + '\n血脉等级：' + (fd.beastBlood || '未知') + '\n化形状态：' + fd.beastFormState + '\n';
            if (fd.beastAttrs && fd.beastAttrs.length > 0) ec += '属性：' + fd.beastAttrs.join('、') + '\n';
            if (fd.beastInfo) ec += '种族简介：' + fd.beastInfo + '\n';
            if (fd.beastLook) ec += '兽形外貌：' + fd.beastLook + '\n';
            if (fd.beastPersonality) ec += '性格：' + fd.beastPersonality + '\n';
            if (fd.beastHobby) ec += '爱好：' + fd.beastHobby + '\n';
            if (fd.beastTalents && fd.beastTalents.length > 0) { ec += '天赋魂技：\n'; fd.beastTalents.forEach(function(t) { ec += '  ' + t.name + '：' + (t.desc || '') + '\n'; }); }
            var bsd = fd.beastSpeechData;
            if(bsd){if(bsd.tone&&bsd.tone.length>0)ec+='语气风格：'+bsd.tone.join('、')+'\n';if(bsd.catchphrase)ec+='口头禅：'+bsd.catchphrase+'\n';if(bsd.samples&&bsd.samples.length>0){ec+='示范对话：\n';bsd.samples.forEach(function(s){ec+='  ['+s.scene+'] '+s.line+'\n';});}}
            var bnp = fd.beastNsfwPersonality;
            if(bnp){if(bnp.tags&&bnp.tags.length>0)ec+='NSFW性格倾向：'+bnp.tags.join('、')+'\n';if(bnp.hobbies&&bnp.hobbies.length>0)ec+='NSFW爱好：'+bnp.hobbies.join('、')+'\n';if(bnp.extra)ec+='NSFW补充：'+bnp.extra+'\n';}
            /* 化形人类形象 */
            var hf = fd.humanForm;
            if(hf && (hf.humanName || hf.humanHeight)){
                ec += '\n化形人类形象：\n';
                if(hf.humanName)ec+='  人类姓名：'+hf.humanName+'\n';
                if(hf.humanAge)ec+='  外观年龄：'+hf.humanAge+'\n';
                if(hf.humanHeight)ec+='  身高：'+hf.humanHeight+'cm\n';
                if(hf.humanWeight)ec+='  体重：'+hf.humanWeight+'kg\n';
                if(hf.bodyType)ec+='  体型：'+hf.bodyType+'\n';
                if(hf.skinColor)ec+='  肤色：'+hf.skinColor+'\n';
                if(hf.hairColor)ec+='  发色：'+hf.hairColor+'\n';
                if(hf.hairStyle)ec+='  发型：'+hf.hairStyle+'\n';
                if(hf.eyeColor)ec+='  瞳色：'+hf.eyeColor+'\n';
                if(hf.faceShape)ec+='  面部轮廓：'+hf.faceShape+'\n';
                if(hf.faceAura&&hf.faceAura.length>0)ec+='  面部气质：'+hf.faceAura.join('、')+'\n';
                if(hf.specialMarks&&hf.specialMarks.length>0)ec+='  特殊标记：'+hf.specialMarks.join('、')+'\n';
                if(hf.beastTraits&&hf.beastTraits.length>0)ec+='  残留兽特征：'+hf.beastTraits.join('、')+'\n';
                if(hf.extra)ec+='  外貌补充：'+hf.extra+'\n';
            }
        }
        if (fd.charDesc) ec += '\nAI角色综合描述：\n' + fd.charDesc + '\n';
        var _needsSoul2 = (fd.race === '人类') || (fd.race === '魂兽' && fd.beastFormState === '彻底化形');
        if (_needsSoul2) {
            ec += '\n先天魂力：' + fd.innate + '级\n当前等级：' + fd.level + '级（' + rankName(parseFloat(fd.level)) + '）\n';
            if (fd.charTitle) ec += '封号：' + fd.charTitle + '\n';
            function writeSoul(num) { var sName = fd['soul' + num + 'Name']; if (!sName) return; ec += '\n第' + CN[num] + '武魂：' + sName + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Cat']) ec += '  分类：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Cat'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Qual']) ec += '  品质：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Qual'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Sys']) ec += '  体系：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Sys'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'] && fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'].length > 0) ec += '  属性：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'].join('、') + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Desc']) ec += '  详情：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Desc'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'DomainName']) ec += '  先天领域：' + fd['soul' + num + 'DomainName'] + ' — ' + (fd['soul' + num + 'DomainDesc'] || '') + '\n'; var cs = fd['soul' + num + 'CustomSkills']; if (cs && cs.length > 0) { ec += '  自创魂技：\n'; cs.forEach(function(sk) { ec += '    ' + sk.name + '：' + (sk.desc || '') + '\n'; }); } }
            writeSoul(1); writeSoul(2); writeSoul(3);
            if (fd.gongfa && fd.gongfa.length > 0) { ec += '\n修炼功法：\n'; fd.gongfa.forEach(function(gf) { ec += '  ' + gf.name + '（' + gf.type + (gf.grade ? '·' + gf.grade : '') + '）\n'; if (gf.detail) ec += '    详情：' + gf.detail + '\n'; if (gf.effect) ec += '    效果：' + gf.effect + '\n'; }); }
            if (fd.soulCores && fd.soulCores.count > 0) { ec += '\n魂核（' + fd.soulCores.count + '个）：\n'; if (fd.soulCores.method) ec += '  凝聚方法：' + fd.soulCores.method + '\n'; fd.soulCores.cores.forEach(function(c, i) { ec += '  魂核' + (i + 1) + '：属性' + (c.attr || '未定') + '，位置' + (c.position || '未定') + '\n'; }); }
            function writeRings(key, wuName) { var rd = fd.ringData[key] || []; var dd = fd.ringDetailData[key] || {}; if (rd.length === 0) return; var hasData = false; rd.forEach(function(c) { if (c !== '无') hasData = true; }); if (!hasData) return; ec += '\n' + wuName + '魂环：\n'; for (var i = 0; i < rd.length; i++) { if (rd[i] === '无') continue; var di = dd[i] || {}; ec += '  第' + CN[i + 1] + '环（' + rd[i] + '色）'; if (di.beast) ec += '：' + di.beast; if (di.year) ec += '，' + di.year + '年'; ec += '\n'; if (di.skillName) ec += '    魂技壹：' + di.skillName + (di.skillDesc ? ' — ' + di.skillDesc : '') + '\n'; if (di.skill2Name) ec += '    魂技贰：' + di.skill2Name + (di.skill2Desc ? ' — ' + di.skill2Desc : '') + '\n'; if (di.skill3Name) ec += '    魂技叁：' + di.skill3Name + (di.skill3Desc ? ' — ' + di.skill3Desc : '') + '\n'; if (di.skill4Name) ec += '    魂技肆：' + di.skill4Name + (di.skill4Desc ? ' — ' + di.skill4Desc : '') + '\n'; } }
            writeRings('soul1', fd.soul1Name || '第一武魂'); if (fd.soul2Name) writeRings('soul2', fd.soul2Name); if (fd.soul3Name) writeRings('soul3', fd.soul3Name);
            var hasBone = false; BONE_PARTS.forEach(function(bp) { if (fd.bones[bp.key]) hasBone = true; });
            if (hasBone) { ec += '\n魂骨设定：\n'; BONE_PARTS.forEach(function(bp) { if (!fd.bones[bp.key]) return; ec += '  ' + bp.name + '：' + fd.bones[bp.key]; if (fd.boneSrc[bp.key]) ec += '（来源：' + fd.boneSrc[bp.key] + '）'; if (fd.boneYear[bp.key]) ec += '（年限：' + fd.boneYear[bp.key] + '年）'; ec += '\n'; if (fd.boneSkillName[bp.key]) ec += '    魂技壹：' + fd.boneSkillName[bp.key] + (fd.boneSkillDesc[bp.key] ? ' — ' + fd.boneSkillDesc[bp.key] : '') + '\n'; if (fd.boneSkill2Name[bp.key]) ec += '    魂技贰：' + fd.boneSkill2Name[bp.key] + (fd.boneSkill2Desc[bp.key] ? ' — ' + fd.boneSkill2Desc[bp.key] : '') + '\n'; if (fd.boneSkill3Name[bp.key]) ec += '    魂技叁：' + fd.boneSkill3Name[bp.key] + (fd.boneSkill3Desc[bp.key] ? ' — ' + fd.boneSkill3Desc[bp.key] : '') + '\n'; if (fd.boneSkill4Name[bp.key]) ec += '    魂技肆：' + fd.boneSkill4Name[bp.key] + (fd.boneSkill4Desc[bp.key] ? ' — ' + fd.boneSkill4Desc[bp.key] : '') + '\n'; }); }
            if (fd.sgLevel > 0) ec += '\n魂导师等级：' + fd.sgLevel + '级\n';
        }
        if(fd.spiritPower && fd.spiritPower.level){var spName='';SPIRIT_LEVELS.forEach(function(sl){if(sl.key===fd.spiritPower.level)spName=sl.name;});ec+='\n精神力：'+spName+'（'+fd.spiritPower.value+'点）\n';}
        if(fd.soulSpirits){var hasSS=false;Object.keys(fd.soulSpirits).forEach(function(k){if(fd.soulSpirits[k]&&fd.soulSpirits[k].length>0)hasSS=true;});if(hasSS){ec+='\n魂灵配置：\n';Object.keys(fd.soulSpirits).forEach(function(soulKey){var spirits=fd.soulSpirits[soulKey];if(!spirits||!spirits.length)return;spirits.forEach(function(sp){ec+='  '+sp.name+'（'+sp.type+'·'+sp.years+'）';if(sp.species)ec+=' 来源：'+sp.species;if(sp.ringPositions&&sp.ringPositions.length>0)ec+=' → 提供第'+sp.ringPositions.map(function(p){return CN[p+1];}).join('、')+'环';ec+='\n';if(sp.appearance)ec+='    外观：'+sp.appearance+'\n';if(sp.ability)ec+='    能力：'+sp.ability+'\n';});});}}
        if(fd.doukaiData && fd.doukaiData.level && fd.doukaiData.level!=='none'){ec+='\n斗铠：'+DOUKAI_LIMITS[fd.doukaiData.level].name;if(fd.doukaiData.name) ec+='「'+fd.doukaiData.name+'」';ec+='\n';if(fd.doukaiData.desc) ec+='  外观：'+fd.doukaiData.desc+'\n';}
        if(fd.mechaData && fd.mechaData.length>0){ec+='\n机甲：\n';fd.mechaData.forEach(function(m){ec+='  '+m.name+'（'+m.level+'）';if(m.desc)ec+='：'+m.desc;ec+='\n';});}
        if(fd.vocationData){var hasVoc=false;if(fd.vocationData.forge>0||fd.vocationData.design>0||fd.vocationData.make>0||fd.vocationData.repair>0)hasVoc=true;if(hasVoc){ec+='\n副职业：\n';if(fd.vocationData.forge>0) ec+='  锻造师：'+fd.vocationData.forge+'级\n';if(fd.vocationData.design>0) ec+='  设计师：'+fd.vocationData.design+'级\n';if(fd.vocationData.make>0) ec+='  制造师：'+fd.vocationData.make+'级\n';if(fd.vocationData.repair>0) ec+='  修理师：'+fd.vocationData.repair+'级\n';}}
        if(fd.godData){ec+='\n神位信息：\n';var godLvName2=getGodLevelName(parseInt(fd.level)||100);ec+='  神位等级：'+godLvName2+'\n';if(fd.godData.positions&&fd.godData.positions.length>0){fd.godData.positions.forEach(function(pos,idx){ec+='  神位'+(idx+1)+'：'+pos.name+'（'+pos.type+'）\n';if(pos.desc)ec+='    详情：'+pos.desc+'\n';if(pos.armor)ec+='    神装：'+pos.armor+'\n';if(pos.skills&&pos.skills.length>0){pos.skills.forEach(function(sk){ec+='    神技：'+sk.name+(sk.desc?' — '+sk.desc:'')+'\n';});}});}if(fd.godData.laws&&fd.godData.laws.length>0) ec+='  法则掌控：'+fd.godData.laws.join('、')+'\n';if(fd.godData.artifacts&&fd.godData.artifacts.length>0){ec+='  神器：\n';fd.godData.artifacts.forEach(function(art){ec+='    '+art.name+'（'+art.level+'）';if(art.desc)ec+='：'+art.desc;ec+='\n';});}}
        if (fd.selfFusionSkills && fd.selfFusionSkills.length > 0) { ec += '\n自体武魂融合技：\n'; fd.selfFusionSkills.forEach(function(sf) { ec += '  ' + sf.comboKey + ' → ' + sf.name; if (sf.effect) ec += '：' + sf.effect; ec += '\n'; }); }
        if (fd.affiliationFaction) ec += '\n归属势力：' + fd.affiliationFaction + (fd.affiliationRole ? '（' + fd.affiliationRole + '）' : '') + '\n';
        ec += '\n降临设定：\n  降临章节：' + (fd.chapter || '未选择') + '\n  切入事件：' + (fd.event || '未选择') + '\n  降临方式：' + fd.arrivalType + '\n';
        if (fd.knowsPlot) ec += '  熟知原著剧情：是\n';
        if (fd.location || fd.locCustom) ec += '\n降临地点：' + (fd.location || '') + (fd.locCustom ? ' ' + fd.locCustom : '') + '\n';
        if (fd.identity) ec += '\n身份背景：\n' + fd.identity + '\n';
        if (fd.npcRelation) ec += '\nNPC关系：' + fd.npcRelation + '\n';
        if (fd.npcRelations && fd.npcRelations.length > 0) { ec += '\n与NPC的关系：\n'; fd.npcRelations.forEach(function(rel) { ec += '  ' + rel.npcName + '（' + (rel.relationType || '未定') + '·' + (rel.attitude || '中立') + '）'; if (rel.description) ec += '：' + rel.description; ec += '\n'; }); }
        if (fd.fusionSkills && fd.fusionSkills.length > 0) { ec += '\n武魂融合技：\n'; fd.fusionSkills.forEach(function(target) { ec += '  融合对象：' + target.targetName + '\n'; if (target.fusions && target.fusions.length > 0) { target.fusions.forEach(function(f) { ec += '    ' + (f.name || '未命名'); if (f.effect) ec += '：' + f.effect; ec += '\n'; }); } }); }
        var inv = fd.inventory;
        if (inv) { var hasInv = (inv.gold > 0 || inv.silver > 0 || inv.copper > 0 || inv.fc > 0 || inv.contribution > 0 || inv.slc > 0 || inv.outfits.length > 0 || inv.soulDevices.length > 0 || inv.spareBones.length > 0 || inv.herbs.length > 0 || inv.food.length > 0 || inv.others.length > 0); if (hasInv) { ec += '\n初始背包：\n'; if(inv.fc > 0 || inv.contribution > 0 || inv.slc > 0){ ec += '  联邦币(FC)：' + inv.fc + ' 贡献点：' + inv.contribution + ' 银联币(SLC)：' + inv.slc + '\n'; }else if (inv.gold > 0 || inv.silver > 0 || inv.copper > 0) ec += '  魂币：金' + inv.gold + ' 银' + inv.silver + ' 铜' + inv.copper + '\n'; function writeInvList(label, list) { if (!list || list.length === 0) return; ec += '  ' + label + '：\n'; list.forEach(function(item) { ec += '    ' + item.name; if (item.quantity && item.quantity > 1) ec += ' ×' + item.quantity; if (item.desc) ec += '：' + item.desc; ec += '\n'; }); } writeInvList('服饰', inv.outfits); if (inv.soulDevices && inv.soulDevices.length > 0) { ec += '  魂导器：\n'; inv.soulDevices.forEach(function(sd2) { ec += '    ' + sd2.name + '（' + sd2.level + '级）'; if (sd2.effect) ec += '，作用：' + sd2.effect; ec += '\n'; }); } if (inv.spareBones && inv.spareBones.length > 0) { ec += '  备用魂骨：\n'; inv.spareBones.forEach(function(sb) { ec += '    ' + (sb.part || '') + ' ' + sb.name; if (sb.beast) ec += '（来源：' + sb.beast + '）'; ec += '\n'; }); } writeInvList('草药', inv.herbs); writeInvList('食物', inv.food); writeInvList('其他', inv.others); } }
        if (fd.extraBg) ec += '\n补充描述：' + fd.extraBg + '\n';
        return ec;
    }

    function buildNpcExportText(fd) {
        var _dn = getDisplayName(fd);
        var ec = '[NPC] ' + _dn + '的斗罗大陆设定：\n';
        ec += '时代：' + fd.era + '\n种族：' + fd.race + '\n';
        if (fd.race === '人类') { ec += '姓名：' + (fd.name || '未命名') + '\n性别：' + fd.gender + '\n年龄：' + fd.age + '岁\n'; if (fd.personality) ec += '性格：' + fd.personality + '\n'; if (fd.hobby) ec += '爱好：' + fd.hobby + '\n'; }
        else { ec += '魂兽种族：' + (fd.beastSpecies || '未知') + '\n'; if (fd.beastName) ec += '个人名字：' + fd.beastName + '\n'; ec += '雌雄：' + fd.beastSex + '\n修为年限：' + (fd.beastYears || '未知') + '\n化形状态：' + fd.beastFormState + '\n'; if (fd.beastPersonality) ec += '性格：' + fd.beastPersonality + '\n'; }
        var sd2 = (fd.race === '人类') ? fd.speechData : fd.beastSpeechData;
        if (sd2) { if (sd2.tone && sd2.tone.length > 0) ec += '语气风格：' + sd2.tone.join('、') + '\n'; if (sd2.samples && sd2.samples.length > 0) { ec += '示范对话：\n'; sd2.samples.forEach(function(s) { ec += '  [' + (s.scene || '') + '] ' + (s.line || '') + '\n'; }); } }
        if (fd.charDesc) ec += '\nAI角色综合描述：\n' + fd.charDesc + '\n';
        var _ns = (fd.race === '人类') || (fd.race === '魂兽' && fd.beastFormState === '彻底化形');
        if (_ns) {
            ec += '\n先天魂力：' + fd.innate + '级\n当前等级：' + fd.level + '级（' + rankName(parseFloat(fd.level)) + '）\n';
            function ws(num) { var sn = fd['soul' + num + 'Name']; if (!sn) return; ec += '\n第' + CN[num] + '武魂：' + sn + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Cat']) ec += '  分类：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Cat'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Qual']) ec += '  品质：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Qual'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Sys']) ec += '  体系：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Sys'] + '\n'; if (fd['soul' + num + 'Desc']) ec += '  详情：' + fd['soul' + num + 'Desc'] + '\n'; }
            ws(1); ws(2); ws(3);
            function wr(key, wn) { var rd = fd.ringData[key] || []; var dd = fd.ringDetailData[key] || {}; var hd = false; rd.forEach(function(c) { if (c !== '无') hd = true; }); if (!hd) return; ec += '\n' + wn + '魂环：\n'; for (var i = 0; i < rd.length; i++) { if (rd[i] === '无') continue; var di = dd[i] || {}; ec += '  第' + CN[i + 1] + '环（' + rd[i] + '色）'; if (di.beast) ec += '：' + di.beast; ec += '\n'; if (di.skillName) ec += '    魂技壹：' + di.skillName + '\n'; } }
            wr('soul1', fd.soul1Name || '第一武魂'); if (fd.soul2Name) wr('soul2', fd.soul2Name);
        }
        if (fd.affiliationFaction) ec += '\n归属势力：' + fd.affiliationFaction + '\n';
        if (fd.identity) ec += '\n身份背景：\n' + fd.identity + '\n';
        if (fd.extraBg) ec += '\n补充描述：' + fd.extraBg + '\n';
        return ec;
    }

    /* ===== 预览卡片（简化版，展示所有核心数据） ===== */
    function pvField(key, val, full) { return '<div class="preview-field' + (full ? ' full' : '') + '"><div class="preview-field-key">' + escHtml(key) + '</div><div class="preview-field-val">' + escHtml(val || '—') + '</div></div>'; }
    function copyPreviewText(btn) {
        var fd = collectFormData();
        var text = (createMode === 'npc') ? buildNpcExportText(fd) : buildExportText(fd);
        if (navigator.clipboard) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function() { btn.classList.add('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 已复制'; setTimeout(function() { btn.classList.remove('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 复制纯文本'; }, 1500); }); }
        else { var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta); btn.classList.add('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 已复制'; setTimeout(function() { btn.classList.remove('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 复制纯文本'; }, 1500); }
    }
    function buildPreviewCard() {
        var fd = collectFormData(); var _dn = getDisplayName(fd); var lv = parseFloat(fd.level) || 0; var _needsSoul3 = (fd.race === '人类') || (fd.race === '魂兽' && fd.beastFormState === '彻底化形'); var isNpc = (createMode === 'npc');
        var h = '<div class="preview-card"><div class="preview-banner"' + (isNpc ? ' style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--npc),hsl(280,55%,45%))"' : '') + '>';
        h += '<div class="preview-name">' + (isNpc ? '👤 ' : '') + escHtml(_dn) + '</div>';
        h += '<div class="preview-subtitle">' + escHtml(fd.era + ' · ' + fd.race + (fd.race === '魂兽' ? ' · ' + fd.beastFormState : '') + (isNpc ? ' · NPC' : '')) + '</div>';
        if (_needsSoul3) { h += '<div class="preview-badge">' + escHtml(fd.level + '级 ' + rankName(lv) + (fd.charTitle ? ' · ' + fd.charTitle : '')) + '</div>'; }
        else if (fd.race === '魂兽') { h += '<div class="preview-badge">' + escHtml(fd.beastYears + ' · ' + fd.beastBlood) + '</div>'; }
        h += '</div><div class="preview-body">';
        /* 基础信息 */
        h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">基础信息</div><div class="preview-grid">';
        if (fd.race === '人类') { h += pvField('姓名', fd.name); h += pvField('性别', fd.gender); h += pvField('年龄', fd.age + '岁'); if(fd.personality) h += pvField('性格', fd.personality, true); if(fd.hobby) h += pvField('爱好', fd.hobby, true); }
        else { h += pvField('魂兽种族', fd.beastSpecies); h += pvField('雌雄', fd.beastSex); h += pvField('修为年限', fd.beastYears); h += pvField('化形状态', fd.beastFormState); if(fd.beastPersonality) h += pvField('性格', fd.beastPersonality, true); if(fd.beastHobby) h += pvField('爱好', fd.beastHobby, true); if(fd.beastLook) h += pvField('兽形外貌', fd.beastLook, true); }
        h += '</div></div>';
        /* 说话方式 */
        var sd3 = (fd.race === '人类') ? fd.speechData : fd.beastSpeechData;
        if(sd3 && (sd3.tone&&sd3.tone.length>0 || sd3.catchphrase)){
            h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">说话方式</div><div class="preview-grid">';
            if(sd3.tone&&sd3.tone.length>0) h += pvField('语气风格', sd3.tone.join('、'), true);
            if(sd3.habit&&sd3.habit.length>0) h += pvField('说话习惯', sd3.habit.join('、'), true);
            if(sd3.catchphrase) h += pvField('口头禅', sd3.catchphrase);
            if(sd3.addressHabit) h += pvField('称呼习惯', sd3.addressHabit);
            if(sd3.samples&&sd3.samples.length>0){ sd3.samples.forEach(function(s,idx){ h += pvField('示范'+(idx+1), '['+s.scene+'] '+s.line, true); }); }
            h += '</div></div>';
        }
        /* 外貌 */
        var ap = fd.appearance || {};
        if (fd.race === '人类' && (ap.height || ap.bodyType || ap.hairColor)) {
            h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">外貌形象</div><div class="preview-grid">';
            if(ap.height)h+=pvField('身高',ap.height+'cm');if(ap.weight)h+=pvField('体重',ap.weight+'kg');if(ap.bodyType)h+=pvField('体型',ap.bodyType);if(ap.skinColor)h+=pvField('肤色',ap.skinColor);if(ap.hairColor)h+=pvField('发色',ap.hairColor);if(ap.hairStyle)h+=pvField('发型',ap.hairStyle);if(ap.eyeColor)h+=pvField('瞳色',ap.eyeColor);if(ap.faceShape)h+=pvField('面部轮廓',ap.faceShape);if(ap.faceAura&&ap.faceAura.length>0)h+=pvField('面部气质',ap.faceAura.join('、'));if(ap.specialMarks&&ap.specialMarks.length>0)h+=pvField('特殊标记',ap.specialMarks.join('、'));if(ap.extra)h+=pvField('外貌补充',ap.extra,true);
            h += '</div></div>';
        }
        /* 服饰 */
        var of3 = fd.outfit || {};
        if (of3.top || of3.bottom || of3.style) {
            h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">穿着服饰</div><div class="preview-grid">';
            if(of3.top)h+=pvField('上身',of3.top);if(of3.bottom)h+=pvField('下身',of3.bottom);if(of3.shoes)h+=pvField('鞋子',of3.shoes);if(of3.socks)h+=pvField('袜子',of3.socks);if(of3.mainColor)h+=pvField('主色调',of3.mainColor);if(of3.style)h+=pvField('风格',of3.style);if(of3.accessories)h+=pvField('配饰',of3.accessories,true);
            h += '</div></div>';
        }
        if (fd.charDesc) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">AI角色综合描述</div><div class="preview-desc-text">' + escHtml(fd.charDesc) + '</div></div>'; }
        /* 武魂信息 */
        if (_needsSoul3) {
            h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">魂师数据</div><div class="preview-grid">';
            h += pvField('先天魂力', fd.innate + '级'); h += pvField('当前等级', fd.level + '级（' + rankName(lv) + '）');
            if (fd.charTitle) h += pvField('封号', fd.charTitle); if (fd.sgLevel > 0) h += pvField('魂导师等级', fd.sgLevel + '级');
            h += '</div></div>';
            function pvSoul(num) {var sn = fd['soul' + num + 'Name']; if (!sn) return;h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">第' + CN[num] + '武魂：' + escHtml(sn) + '</div><div class="preview-grid">';if (fd['soul' + num + 'Cat']) h += pvField('分类', fd['soul' + num + 'Cat']);if (fd['soul' + num + 'Qual']) h += pvField('品质', fd['soul' + num + 'Qual']);if (fd['soul' + num + 'Sys']) h += pvField('体系', fd['soul' + num + 'Sys']);if (fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'] && fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'].length > 0) h += pvField('属性', fd['soul' + num + 'Attrs'].join('、'));if (fd['soul' + num + 'Desc']) h += pvField('详情', fd['soul' + num + 'Desc'], true);if (fd['soul' + num + 'DomainName']) h += pvField('领域', fd['soul' + num + 'DomainName'] + ' — ' + (fd['soul' + num + 'DomainDesc']||''), true);var cs2=fd['soul'+num+'CustomSkills'];if(cs2&&cs2.length>0){cs2.forEach(function(sk){h+=pvField('自创魂技',sk.name+(sk.desc?' — '+sk.desc:''),true);});}h += '</div></div>';}
            pvSoul(1); pvSoul(2); pvSoul(3);
            /* 功法 */
            if (fd.gongfa && fd.gongfa.length > 0) {h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">修炼功法</div><div class="preview-grid">';fd.gongfa.forEach(function(gf){h += pvField(gf.name, gf.type + (gf.grade ? '·' + gf.grade : '') + (gf.effect?' — '+gf.effect:''), true);});h += '</div></div>';}
            /* 魂环 */
            function pvRings(key, wuName) {var rd = fd.ringData[key] || []; var dd = fd.ringDetailData[key] || {};var hasData = false; rd.forEach(function(c) { if (c !== '无') hasData = true; });if (!hasData) return;h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">' + escHtml(wuName) + ' 魂环</div>';for (var ri = 0; ri < rd.length; ri++) {if (rd[ri] === '无') continue;var di = dd[ri] || {};h += '<div class="preview-ring-item">第' + CN[ri+1] + '环（' + rd[ri] + '色）';if (di.beast) h += ' · ' + escHtml(di.beast);if (di.year) h += ' · ' + escHtml(di.year) + '年';if (di.skillName) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">魂技壹：' + escHtml(di.skillName) + (di.skillDesc ? ' — ' + escHtml(di.skillDesc) : '') + '</span>';if (di.skill2Name) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">魂技贰：' + escHtml(di.skill2Name) + (di.skill2Desc ? ' — ' + escHtml(di.skill2Desc) : '') + '</span>';if (di.skill3Name) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">魂技叁：' + escHtml(di.skill3Name) + '</span>';if (di.skill4Name) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">魂技肆：' + escHtml(di.skill4Name) + '</span>';h += '</div>';}h += '</div>';}
            pvRings('soul1', fd.soul1Name || '第一武魂'); if (fd.soul2Name) pvRings('soul2', fd.soul2Name); if (fd.soul3Name) pvRings('soul3', fd.soul3Name);
            /* 魂骨 */
            var hasBone2 = false; BONE_PARTS.forEach(function(bp) { if (fd.bones[bp.key]) hasBone2 = true; });
            if (hasBone2) {h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">魂骨设定</div>';BONE_PARTS.forEach(function(bp) {if (!fd.bones[bp.key]) return;h += '<div class="preview-bone-item">' + bp.name + '：' + escHtml(fd.bones[bp.key]);if (fd.boneSrc[bp.key]) h += '（' + escHtml(fd.boneSrc[bp.key]) + '）';if (fd.boneYear[bp.key]) h += '（' + escHtml(fd.boneYear[bp.key]) + '年）';if (fd.boneSkillName[bp.key]) h += '<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-2)">魂技壹：' + escHtml(fd.boneSkillName[bp.key]) + '</span>';h += '</div>';});h += '</div>';}
            /* 魂核 */
            if(fd.soulCores && fd.soulCores.count > 0){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">魂核</div><div class="preview-grid">';h += pvField('数量', fd.soulCores.count+'个');if(fd.soulCores.method)h += pvField('方法', fd.soulCores.method);fd.soulCores.cores.forEach(function(c,i){h += pvField('魂核'+(i+1), (c.attr||'未定')+'·'+(c.position||'未定'));});h += '</div></div>';}
        }
        /* 精神力 */
        if(fd.spiritPower && fd.spiritPower.level){var spDispName='';SPIRIT_LEVELS.forEach(function(sl){if(sl.key===fd.spiritPower.level)spDispName=sl.name;});h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">精神力</div><div class="preview-grid">';h += pvField('境界', spDispName);h += pvField('数值', fd.spiritPower.value + '点');h += '</div></div>';}
        /* 魂灵 */
        if(fd.soulSpirits){var hasSS2=false;Object.keys(fd.soulSpirits).forEach(function(k){if(fd.soulSpirits[k]&&fd.soulSpirits[k].length>0)hasSS2=true;});if(hasSS2){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">魂灵配置</div>';Object.keys(fd.soulSpirits).forEach(function(soulKey){fd.soulSpirits[soulKey].forEach(function(sp){h += '<div class="preview-ring-item">' + escHtml(sp.name) + '（' + escHtml(sp.type) + '·' + escHtml(sp.years) + '）';if(sp.species) h += ' 来源：'+escHtml(sp.species);if(sp.ringPositions&&sp.ringPositions.length>0) h += ' → 第' + sp.ringPositions.map(function(p){return CN[p+1];}).join('、') + '环';if(sp.appearance) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">外观：'+escHtml(sp.appearance)+'</span>';if(sp.ability) h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">能力：'+escHtml(sp.ability)+'</span>';h += '</div>';});});h += '</div>';}}
        /* 斗铠 */
        if(fd.doukaiData && fd.doukaiData.level && fd.doukaiData.level!=='none'){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">斗铠</div><div class="preview-grid">';h += pvField('等级', DOUKAI_LIMITS[fd.doukaiData.level].name);if(fd.doukaiData.name) h += pvField('名称', fd.doukaiData.name);if(fd.doukaiData.desc) h += pvField('外观', fd.doukaiData.desc, true);h += '</div></div>';}
        /* 机甲 */
        if(fd.mechaData && fd.mechaData.length>0){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">机甲</div><div class="preview-grid">';fd.mechaData.forEach(function(m){h += pvField(m.name, m.level+(m.desc?' — '+m.desc:''), true);});h += '</div></div>';}
        /* 副职业 */
        if(fd.vocationData){var hasVocPv = (fd.vocationData.forge>0||fd.vocationData.design>0||fd.vocationData.make>0||fd.vocationData.repair>0);if(hasVocPv){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">副职业</div><div class="preview-grid">';if(fd.vocationData.forge>0) h += pvField('锻造师', fd.vocationData.forge+'级');if(fd.vocationData.design>0) h += pvField('设计师', fd.vocationData.design+'级');if(fd.vocationData.make>0) h += pvField('制造师', fd.vocationData.make+'级');if(fd.vocationData.repair>0) h += pvField('修理师', fd.vocationData.repair+'级');h += '</div></div>';}}
        /* 神位 */
        if(fd.godData && fd.godData.positions && fd.godData.positions.length>0){var godLvPv=getGodLevelName(parseInt(fd.level)||100);h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">⭐ 神位信息（'+godLvPv+'）</div><div class="preview-grid">';fd.godData.positions.forEach(function(pos){h += pvField('神位', pos.name + '（' + pos.type + '）');if(pos.armor) h += pvField('神装', pos.armor, true);if(pos.skills&&pos.skills.length>0){pos.skills.forEach(function(sk){h += pvField('神技', sk.name+(sk.desc?' — '+sk.desc:''), true);});}});if(fd.godData.laws&&fd.godData.laws.length>0) h += pvField('法则', fd.godData.laws.join('、'), true);if(fd.godData.artifacts&&fd.godData.artifacts.length>0){fd.godData.artifacts.forEach(function(art){h += pvField(art.level||'神器', art.name+(art.desc?' — '+art.desc:''), true);});}h += '</div></div>';}
        /* 融合技 */
        if(fd.selfFusionSkills&&fd.selfFusionSkills.length>0){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">自体武魂融合技</div><div class="preview-grid">';fd.selfFusionSkills.forEach(function(sf){h += pvField(sf.comboKey, sf.name+(sf.effect?' — '+sf.effect:''), true);});h += '</div></div>';}
        if(fd.fusionSkills&&fd.fusionSkills.length>0){h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">武魂融合技</div>';fd.fusionSkills.forEach(function(target){h += '<div class="preview-ring-item">融合对象：'+escHtml(target.targetName);if(target.fusions&&target.fusions.length>0){target.fusions.forEach(function(f){h += '<br><span class="preview-ring-detail">'+(f.name||'')+(f.effect?' — '+f.effect:'')+'</span>';});}h += '</div>';});h += '</div>';}
        if (fd.affiliationFaction) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">归属势力</div><div class="preview-grid">'; h += pvField('势力', fd.affiliationFaction); if (fd.affiliationRole) h += pvField('职位', fd.affiliationRole); h += '</div></div>'; }
        if (fd.identity) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">身份背景</div><div class="preview-desc-text">' + escHtml(fd.identity) + '</div></div>'; }
        if (!isNpc) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">降临设定</div><div class="preview-grid">'; h += pvField('降临章节', fd.chapter); h += pvField('降临方式', fd.arrivalType); if (fd.location || fd.locCustom) h += pvField('地点', (fd.location || '') + (fd.locCustom ? ' ' + fd.locCustom : ''), true); h += '</div></div>'; }
        if (fd.npcRelations && fd.npcRelations.length > 0) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">与NPC的关系</div><div class="preview-grid">'; fd.npcRelations.forEach(function(rel) { h += pvField(rel.npcName, (rel.relationType||'')+'·'+(rel.attitude||'')+(rel.description?' — '+rel.description:''), true); }); h += '</div></div>'; }
        if (fd.extraBg) { h += '<div class="preview-section anim-in"><div class="preview-section-title">补充描述</div><div class="preview-desc-text">' + escHtml(fd.extraBg) + '</div></div>'; }
        h += '</div>';
        h += '<div class="preview-actions"><button class="copy-btn" onclick="copyPreviewText(this)">' + SVG_COPY + ' 复制纯文本</button></div>';
        h += '</div>';
        document.getElementById('previewCardArea').innerHTML = h;
    }

    /* ===== 导出面板 ===== */
    function openExportPanel(idx) { _exportIdx = idx; var body = document.getElementById('exportBody'); var h = ''; EXPORT_SECTIONS.forEach(function(sec) { var extraClass = sec.privacy ? ' privacy-item' : ''; h += '<div class="export-check'+extraClass+'"><input type="checkbox" id="exp-'+sec.key+'" checked><label for="exp-'+sec.key+'">'+sec.label+'</label></div>'; }); body.innerHTML = h; document.getElementById('exportOverlay').classList.add('open'); }
    function closeExportPanel() { document.getElementById('exportOverlay').classList.remove('open'); _exportIdx=-1; }
    function confirmExport() {
        if (_exportIdx < 0 || _exportIdx >= loadedProfiles.length) return;
        var p = loadedProfiles[_exportIdx];
        var d = p.data;
        var out = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d));
        out._exportedFrom = p.displayName;
        /* v3.1：根据勾选状态过滤字段 */
        var fieldMap = {
            'charName': ['name','beastName','beastSpecies'],
            'genderAge': ['gender','age','beastSex'],
            'personality': ['personality','hobby','beastPersonality','beastHobby'],
            'nsfwPersonality': ['nsfwPersonality','beastNsfwPersonality'],
            'speechData': ['speechData','beastSpeechData'],
            'appearance': ['appearance'],
            'outfit': ['outfit'],
            'nsfwBody': [],
            'charDesc': ['charDesc'],
            'beastInfo': ['beastInfo','beastLook','beastAttrs','beastBlood','beastYears','beastTalents'],
            'humanForm': ['humanForm'],
            'innateLevel': ['innate','level','charTitle'],
            'soul1': ['soul1Name','soul1Cat','soul1Qual','soul1Sys','soul1Attrs','soul1Desc','soul1DomainName','soul1DomainDesc','soul1CustomSkills'],
            'soul2': ['soul2Name','soul2Cat','soul2Qual','soul2Sys','soul2Attrs','soul2Desc','soul2DomainName','soul2DomainDesc','soul2CustomSkills'],
            'soul3': ['soul3Name','soul3Cat','soul3Qual','soul3Sys','soul3Attrs','soul3Desc','soul3DomainName','soul3DomainDesc','soul3CustomSkills'],
            'gongfa': ['gongfa'],
            'soulCores': ['soulCores'],
            'ring': ['ringData','ringDetailData'],
            'bone': ['bones','boneSrc','boneYear','boneSkillName','boneSkillDesc','boneSkill2Name','boneSkill2Desc','boneSkill3Name','boneSkill3Desc','boneSkill4Name','boneSkill4Desc'],
            'soulguide': ['sgLevel'],
            'spiritPower': ['spiritPower'],
            'soulSpirits': ['soulSpirits'],
            'doukaiData': ['doukaiData'],
            'mechaData': ['mechaData'],
            'vocationData': ['vocationData'],
            'godData': ['godData'],
            'arrival': ['chapter','event','arrivalType','knowsPlot'],
            'location': ['location','locCustom'],
            'identity': ['identity'],
            'affiliation': ['affiliationFaction','affiliationRole'],
            'fusionSkills': ['fusionSkills'],
            'selfFusionSkills': ['selfFusionSkills'],
            'inventory': ['inventory'],
            'npcRelation': ['npcRelation'],
            'npcRelations': ['npcRelations'],
            'extraBg': ['extraBg']
        };
        EXPORT_SECTIONS.forEach(function(sec){
            var cb = document.getElementById('exp-'+sec.key);
            if(cb && !cb.checked){
                var fields = fieldMap[sec.key] || [];
                fields.forEach(function(f){ delete out[f]; });
            }
        });
        var blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(out,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
        var a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        a.download=(p.displayName||'角色')+'.json';
        a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
        closeExportPanel();
    }

    /* ===== 世界观数据（保留原版） ===== */
    function collectWorldData(){
        var data = { _isWorld: true, wtype: worldElementType, era: worldElementEra, _savedAt: Date.now() };
        if(worldElementType === 'faction'){
            data.name = (document.getElementById('f-wf-name')||{}).value||'';data.subType = getActiveSingleTag('wfSubTypeTags');data.location = (document.getElementById('f-wf-location')||{}).value||'';data.locationCustom = (document.getElementById('f-wf-location-custom')||{}).value||'';data.territory = (document.getElementById('f-wf-territory')||{}).value||'';data.history = (document.getElementById('f-wf-history')||{}).value||'';data.scale = getActiveSingleTag('wfScaleTags');data.power = getActiveSingleTag('wfPowerTags');data.specialty = (document.getElementById('f-wf-specialty')||{}).value||'';data.secret = (document.getElementById('f-wf-secret')||{}).value||'';data.economy = (document.getElementById('f-wf-economy')||{}).value||'';data.rules = (document.getElementById('f-wf-rules')||{}).value||'';data.leaderNpc = (document.getElementById('f-wf-leader-npc')||{}).value||'';data.leaderName = (document.getElementById('f-wf-leader-name')||{}).value||'';data.members = [];document.querySelectorAll('#wfMemberList .member-item').forEach(function(item){var name = (item.querySelector('[data-wfm="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim()) return;data.members.push({name: name.trim(),role: (item.querySelector('[data-wfm="role"]')||{}).value||'',desc: (item.querySelector('[data-wfm="desc"]')||{}).value||''});});data.canonRelations = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('wfRelTags')||document.createElement('div'));data.diplomacy = (document.getElementById('f-wf-diplomacy')||{}).value||'';
        } else if(worldElementType === 'academy'){
            data.name = (document.getElementById('f-wa-name')||{}).value||'';data.city = (document.getElementById('f-wa-city')||{}).value||'';data.cityCustom = (document.getElementById('f-wa-city-custom')||{}).value||'';data.level = getActiveSingleTag('waLevelTags');data.admission = (document.getElementById('f-wa-admission')||{}).value||'';data.intro = (document.getElementById('f-wa-intro')||{}).value||'';data.features = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('waFeatureTags')||document.createElement('div'));data.gongfa = (document.getElementById('f-wa-gongfa')||{}).value||'';data.courses = (document.getElementById('f-wa-courses')||{}).value||'';data.graduation = (document.getElementById('f-wa-graduation')||{}).value||'';data.facilities = (document.getElementById('f-wa-facilities')||{}).value||'';data.deanNpc = (document.getElementById('f-wa-dean-npc')||{}).value||'';data.deanName = (document.getElementById('f-wa-dean-name')||{}).value||'';data.teachers = [];document.querySelectorAll('#waTeacherList .member-item').forEach(function(item){var name = (item.querySelector('[data-wat="name"]')||{}).value||'';if(!name.trim()) return;data.teachers.push({name: name.trim(),subject: (item.querySelector('[data-wat="subject"]')||{}).value||'',desc: (item.querySelector('[data-wat="desc"]')||{}).value||''});});data.canonRelations = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('waRelTags')||document.createElement('div'));data.relationsDesc = (document.getElementById('f-wa-relations-desc')||{}).value||'';
        } else if(worldElementType === 'location'){
            data.name = (document.getElementById('f-wl-name')||{}).value||'';data.locType = getActiveSingleTag('wlTypeTags');data.geo = (document.getElementById('f-wl-geo')||{}).value||'';data.faction = (document.getElementById('f-wl-faction')||{}).value||'';data.env = (document.getElementById('f-wl-env')||{}).value||'';data.climate = getActiveSingleTag('wlClimateTags');data.effects = getActiveMultiTags(document.getElementById('wlEffectTags')||document.createElement('div'));data.danger = getActiveSingleTag('wlDangerTags');data.beasts = (document.getElementById('f-wl-beasts')||{}).value||'';data.resources = (document.getElementById('f-wl-resources')||{}).value||'';data.population = (document.getElementById('f-wl-population')||{}).value||'';data.legend = (document.getElementById('f-wl-legend')||{}).value||'';data.stationed = (document.getElementById('f-wl-stationed')||{}).value||'';data.extra = (document.getElementById('f-wl-extra')||{}).value||'';
        }
        return data;
    }

    function buildWorldExportText(data){
        var typeNames = {'faction':'势力/宗门','academy':'学院','location':'地点/区域'};
        var ec = '[世界观元素] ' + (data.name||'未命名') + '（' + (typeNames[data.wtype]||'') + '）\n';
        ec += '时代：' + data.era + '\n';
        if(data.wtype === 'faction'){ec += '势力名称：' + data.name + '\n';if(data.subType)ec+='类型：'+data.subType+'\n';if(data.territory)ec+='势力范围：'+data.territory+'\n';if(data.history)ec+='历史由来：'+data.history+'\n';if(data.scale)ec+='规模：'+data.scale+'\n';if(data.specialty)ec+='特色：'+data.specialty+'\n';if(data.leaderName)ec+='首领：'+data.leaderName+'\n';}
        else if(data.wtype === 'academy'){ec += '学院名称：' + data.name + '\n';if(data.level)ec+='等级：'+data.level+'\n';if(data.intro)ec+='简介：'+data.intro+'\n';}
        else if(data.wtype === 'location'){ec += '地点名称：' + data.name + '\n';if(data.locType)ec+='类型：'+data.locType+'\n';if(data.env)ec+='环境：'+data.env+'\n';if(data.danger)ec+='危险等级：'+data.danger+'\n';}
        return ec;
    }
    function buildWorldPreviewCard(){
        var data = collectWorldData();
        var typeNames = {'faction':'⚔️ 势力/宗门','academy':'🏫 学院','location':'🗺️ 地点/区域'};
        var h = '<div class="preview-card"><div class="preview-banner" style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--world),var(--gold))">';
        h += '<div class="preview-name">' + escHtml(typeNames[data.wtype]||'') + '</div>';
        h += '<div class="preview-subtitle">' + escHtml(data.era) + '</div>';
        h += '<div class="preview-badge">' + escHtml(data.name||'未命名') + '</div>';
        h += '</div><div class="preview-body">';
        h += '<div class="preview-section"><div class="preview-section-title">基础信息</div><div class="preview-grid">';
        h += pvField('名称', data.name);
        h += '</div></div></div>';
        h += '<div class="preview-actions"><button class="copy-btn" onclick="copyWorldPreviewText(this)">' + SVG_COPY + ' 复制纯文本</button></div></div>';
        var area = document.getElementById('worldPreviewArea');
        if(area) area.innerHTML = h;
    }
    function copyWorldPreviewText(btn){var data = collectWorldData();var text = buildWorldExportText(data);if(navigator.clipboard){navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function(){ btn.classList.add('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 已复制'; setTimeout(function(){ btn.classList.remove('copied'); btn.innerHTML = SVG_COPY + ' 复制纯文本'; }, 1500); });}}
    function restoreWorldData(data){if(!data)return;if(data.wtype==='faction'){(document.getElementById('f-wf-name')||{}).value=data.name||'';if(data.subType)setActiveSingleTag('wfSubTypeTags',data.subType);(document.getElementById('f-wf-territory')||{}).value=data.territory||'';(document.getElementById('f-wf-history')||{}).value=data.history||'';}else if(data.wtype==='academy'){(document.getElementById('f-wa-name')||{}).value=data.name||'';if(data.level)setActiveSingleTag('waLevelTags',data.level);(document.getElementById('f-wa-intro')||{}).value=data.intro||'';}else if(data.wtype==='location'){(document.getElementById('f-wl-name')||{}).value=data.name||'';if(data.locType)setActiveSingleTag('wlTypeTags',data.locType);(document.getElementById('f-wl-env')||{}).value=data.env||'';}(document.getElementById('f-world-save-name')||{}).value=data.name||'';}
    function editWorldElement(idx){var p = loadedProfiles[idx];if(!p || !p.isWorld || !p.data) return;createMode = 'world';worldElementType = p.data.wtype || '';worldElementEra = p.data.era || '斗一';maxStepReached = 5;restoreWorldData(p.data);refreshSteps();showPane('world-preview');onPaneEnter('world-preview');}

    /* ===== 草稿系统 ===== */
    function saveDraft() {try { if(createMode==='world')return; var fd = collectFormData(); fd._paneId = curPaneId; fd._maxStep = maxStepReached;fd._introDirection = document.getElementById('f-intro-direction').value;fd._intro = document.getElementById('f-intro').value;fd._profileName = document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value;fd._fullIntro = (document.getElementById('f-full-intro')||{}).value||'';localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(fd)); } catch(e) { console.warn('草稿保存失败',e); }}
    function checkDraft() {try { var s = localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY); if (!s) return;var d = JSON.parse(s); if (!d || (!d.name && !d.beastSpecies)) return;var dName=d.name||d.beastSpecies||'未知';var area = document.getElementById('draftBannerArea');area.innerHTML = '<div class="draft-banner"><p>检测到未完成的角色「<strong>' + escHtml(dName) + '</strong>」' + (d.createMode === 'npc' ? ' (NPC)' : '') + '</p><div class="draft-banner-btns"><button class="draft-banner-btn restore" onclick="restoreDraft()">恢复</button><button class="draft-banner-btn discard" onclick="discardDraft()">丢弃</button></div></div>'; } catch(e) {}}
    function restoreDraft() {try { var s = localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY); if (!s) return;var d = JSON.parse(s); if(d.createMode) createMode = d.createMode; restoreFormData(d);if (d._introDirection) document.getElementById('f-intro-direction').value = d._introDirection;if (d._intro) document.getElementById('f-intro').value = d._intro;if (d._profileName) document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value = d._profileName;if (d._fullIntro) (document.getElementById('f-full-intro')||{}).value = d._fullIntro;maxStepReached = d._maxStep || 1;var targetPane = (d._paneId) || 'basic';showPane(targetPane);onPaneEnter(targetPane); } catch(e) {}document.getElementById('draftBannerArea').innerHTML = '';}
    function discardDraft() { localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY); document.getElementById('draftBannerArea').innerHTML = ''; }
    function clearDraft() { localStorage.removeItem(DRAFT_KEY); }

    /* ===== 档案管理 ===== */
    function getProfilePrefix(){return '['+selectedEra+'角色档案]';}
    async function loadProfiles() {var recentEl=document.getElementById('recentList');if(recentEl) recentEl.innerHTML='<div class="recent-empty">正在加载...</div>';try{var getWb=getApi('getWorldbook');if(!getWb){if(recentEl)recentEl.innerHTML='<div class="recent-empty">无法连接世界书API</div>';return;}var wbName=getWorldbookName();var entries=await getWb(wbName);loadedProfiles=[];_loadedNpcList=[];_loadedWorldElements=[];entries.forEach(function(entry){
        if(entry.name&&(entry.name.indexOf('[斗一角色档案]')===0||entry.name.indexOf('[斗二角色档案]')===0||entry.name.indexOf('[斗三角色档案]')===0)){try{var data=JSON.parse(entry.content);var displayName=entry.name.replace(/\[斗[一二三]角色档案\]/,'');var era=entry.name.indexOf('[斗一')===0?'斗一':(entry.name.indexOf('[斗二')===0?'斗二':'斗三');loadedProfiles.push({uid:entry.uid,name:entry.name,displayName:displayName,era:era,data:data,isNpc:false,isWorld:false})}catch(e){}}
        if(entry.name&&entry.name.indexOf('[角色档案]')===0){try{var data2=JSON.parse(entry.content);loadedProfiles.push({uid:entry.uid,name:entry.name,displayName:entry.name.replace('[角色档案]',''),era:data2.era||'斗一',data:data2,isNpc:false,isWorld:false})}catch(e){}}
        if(entry.name&&(entry.name.indexOf('[斗一NPC]')===0||entry.name.indexOf('[斗二NPC]')===0||entry.name.indexOf('[斗三NPC]')===0)&&entry.name.indexOf('_data')<0){
            var dataEntry = entries.find(function(e){ return e.name === entry.name + '_data'; });
            var npcData = null; if(dataEntry){ try{ npcData = JSON.parse(dataEntry.content); }catch(e){} }
            var displayName2 = entry.name.replace(/\[斗[一二三]NPC\]/,'');
            var era2 = entry.name.indexOf('[斗一')===0?'斗一':(entry.name.indexOf('[斗二')===0?'斗二':'斗三');
            var npcEntry = { uid:entry.uid, dataUid:dataEntry?dataEntry.uid:null, name:entry.name, displayName:displayName2, era:era2, data:npcData, isNpc:true, isWorld:false, soul1Name:npcData?(npcData.soul1Name||''):'', soul2Name:npcData?(npcData.soul2Name||''):'', soul3Name:npcData?(npcData.soul3Name||''):'' };
            _loadedNpcList.push(npcEntry);loadedProfiles.push(npcEntry);
        }
        var worldPrefixes = ['[斗一势力]','[斗二势力]','[斗三势力]','[斗一学院]','[斗二学院]','[斗三学院]','[斗一地点]','[斗二地点]','[斗三地点]'];
        worldPrefixes.forEach(function(wp){
            if(entry.name&&entry.name.indexOf(wp)===0&&entry.name.indexOf('_data')<0){
                var dataEntry3 = entries.find(function(e){ return e.name === entry.name + '_data'; });
                var weData = null; if(dataEntry3){ try{ weData = JSON.parse(dataEntry3.content); }catch(e){} }
                var weName = entry.name.replace(wp,'');
                var weEra = wp.indexOf('斗一')>=0?'斗一':(wp.indexOf('斗二')>=0?'斗二':'斗三');
                var weType = wp.indexOf('势力')>=0?'faction':(wp.indexOf('学院')>=0?'academy':'location');
                var weEntry = { uid:entry.uid, dataUid:dataEntry3?dataEntry3.uid:null, name:entry.name, displayName:weName, era:weEra, wtype:weType, data:weData, isNpc:false, isWorld:true };
                _loadedWorldElements.push(weEntry);loadedProfiles.push(weEntry);
            }
        });
    });loadedProfiles.sort(function(a,b){var ta=(a.data&&a.data._savedAt)||0;var tb=(b.data&&b.data._savedAt)||0;return tb-ta;});renderRecentProfiles();renderWorldElementList();}catch(e){if(recentEl)recentEl.innerHTML='<div class="recent-empty">加载失败</div>';}}

    function renderRecentProfiles() {var recentEl=document.getElementById('recentList'); if(!recentEl)return;var charProfiles=loadedProfiles.filter(function(p){return !p.isWorld;});if(charProfiles.length===0){recentEl.innerHTML='<div class="recent-empty">暂无角色档案</div>';return;}var show=charProfiles.slice(0,5); var h='';show.forEach(function(p){ var realIdx=loadedProfiles.indexOf(p);var d=p.data||{}; var detail=(d.soul1Name||'未知');var isNpc=p.isNpc;h+='<div class="recent-card'+(isNpc?' npc-card':'')+'""><div class="recent-card-info">';if(isNpc){h+='<span class="recent-card-npc-tag">NPC</span>';}h+='<span class="recent-card-era">'+escHtml(p.era)+'</span><span class="recent-card-name">'+escHtml(p.displayName)+'</span><span class="recent-card-level">'+(d.level||'?')+'级</span></div>';h+='<div class="recent-card-detail">'+escHtml(detail)+'</div>';h+='<div class="recent-card-actions">';if(!isNpc){h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();selectProfileForGame('+realIdx+')">'+SVG_BOLT+' 降临</button>';}h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editProfile('+realIdx+')">'+SVG_EDIT+' 编辑</button></div></div>';});recentEl.innerHTML=h;}

    function renderWorldElementList(){var listEl=document.getElementById('worldElementList'); if(!listEl)return;if(_loadedWorldElements.length===0){ listEl.innerHTML='<div class="recent-empty">暂无世界观元素</div>'; return; }var typeTagClass={'faction':'recent-card-faction-tag','academy':'recent-card-academy-tag','location':'recent-card-location-tag'};var typeNames2={'faction':'势力','academy':'学院','location':'地点'};var h='';_loadedWorldElements.slice(0,5).forEach(function(we){var realIdx=loadedProfiles.indexOf(we);h+='<div class="recent-card world-card"><div class="recent-card-info">';h+='<span class="'+typeTagClass[we.wtype]+'">'+(typeNames2[we.wtype]||'')+'</span>';h+='<span class="recent-card-era">'+escHtml(we.era)+'</span>';h+='<span class="recent-card-name">'+escHtml(we.displayName)+'</span>';h+='</div>';h+='<div class="recent-card-actions">';h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();editWorldElement('+realIdx+')">'+SVG_EDIT+' 编辑</button>';h+='<button class="profile-action-btn danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteProfile('+realIdx+')">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button>';h+='</div></div>';});listEl.innerHTML=h;}

    function selectProfileForGame(idx) { var p=loadedProfiles[idx]; if(!p||p.isNpc||p.isWorld)return; createMode='user'; restoreFormData(p.data); document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value=p.displayName; document.getElementById('f-intro').value=''; document.getElementById('f-intro-direction').value=''; (document.getElementById('f-full-intro')||{}).value=''; refreshSteps(); maxStepReached=activeSteps.length-1; showPane('intro'); onPaneEnter('intro'); }
    function editProfile(idx) { var p=loadedProfiles[idx]; if(!p)return; if(p.isWorld){ editWorldElement(idx); return; } if(p.isNpc){ createMode='npc'; if(p.data) restoreFormData(p.data); (document.getElementById('f-npc-profile-name')||{}).value=p.displayName; }else{ createMode='user'; restoreFormData(p.data); document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value=p.displayName; } refreshSteps(); maxStepReached=activeSteps.length-2; showPane('preview'); onPaneEnter('preview'); }
    async function deleteProfile(idx) {var p=loadedProfiles[idx]; if(!p)return;if(!confirm('确定删除「'+p.displayName+'」？此操作不可撤销！'))return;try{var delFn=getApi('deleteWorldbookEntries');var wbName=getWorldbookName();if(delFn){if(p.isNpc||p.isWorld){await delFn(wbName,function(entry){return entry.uid===p.uid||(p.dataUid&&entry.uid===p.dataUid)});}else{await delFn(wbName,function(entry){return entry.uid===p.uid});}} loadedProfiles.splice(idx,1);renderRecentProfiles();renderWorldElementList();renderProfilesInSettings();}catch(e){}}

    async function loadProfilesInSettings() { var listEl=document.getElementById('profileList'); if(!listEl)return; if(loadedProfiles.length===0) await loadProfiles(); renderProfilesInSettings(); }
    function renderProfilesInSettings() {var listEl=document.getElementById('profileList'); if(!listEl)return;if(loadedProfiles.length===0){listEl.innerHTML='<div class="profile-empty">暂无角色档案</div>';return;}var h='<div class="profile-list">';loadedProfiles.forEach(function(p,idx){ var d=p.data||{}; var isNpc=p.isNpc;var isWorld=p.isWorld;h+='<div class="profile-card'+(isNpc?' npc-profile':'')+(isWorld?' world-profile':'')+'""><div class="profile-card-info">';if(isNpc){h+='<span class="recent-card-npc-tag">NPC</span>';}if(isWorld){var tn3={'faction':'势力','academy':'学院','location':'地点'};h+='<span class="recent-card-world-tag">'+(tn3[p.wtype]||'')+'</span>';}h+='<span class="recent-card-era">'+escHtml(p.era)+'</span><span class="profile-card-name">'+escHtml(p.displayName)+'</span>';if(!isWorld){h+='<span class="profile-card-level">'+(d.level||'?')+'级</span>';}h+='</div>';h+='<div class="profile-card-actions">';if(!isNpc&&!isWorld){h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();closeSettings();selectProfileForGame('+idx+')">'+SVG_BOLT+' 降临</button>';}h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();closeSettings();editProfile('+idx+')">'+SVG_EDIT+' 编辑</button>';if(!isWorld){h+='<button class="profile-action-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openExportPanel('+idx+')">'+SVG_EXPORT+' 导出</button>';}h+='<button class="profile-action-btn danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteProfile('+idx+')">'+SVG_TRASH+'</button></div></div>';});h+='</div>'; listEl.innerHTML=h;}

    function importProfile(event) {var file=event.target.files[0]; if(!file)return;var reader=new FileReader();reader.onload=async function(e){try{var data=JSON.parse(e.target.result);if(!data.name) data.name = file.name.replace(/\.json$/i,'') || '导入角色';createMode='user'; restoreFormData(data); document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value=data.name;refreshSteps(); maxStepReached=activeSteps.length-2; closeSettings(); showPane('preview'); onPaneEnter('preview');}catch(ex){toast('tProfile','文件解析失败','err');}};reader.readAsText(file); event.target.value='';}

    /* ===== 保存NPC ===== */
    async function saveNpc() {
        if(_aiLock)return; _aiLock=true; clearToasts();var npcName = (document.getElementById('f-npc-profile-name') || {}).value || ''; npcName = npcName.trim();if(!npcName) { _aiLock=false; return toast('t-npc-save','请输入NPC档案名称','err'); }var btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveNpc'); btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '保存中...';
        try {var fd = collectFormData(); fd._savedAt = Date.now(); fd._isNpc = true;fd._npcMotivation = (document.getElementById('f-npc-motivation') || {}).value || '';fd._npcKeywords = (document.getElementById('f-npc-keywords') || {}).value || '';var ec = buildNpcExportText(fd);if (fd._npcMotivation) ec += '\nNPC目标/动机：' + fd._npcMotivation + '\n';var keywordsStr = (document.getElementById('f-npc-keywords') || {}).value || '';var keywordList = [npcName];if(keywordsStr){ keywordsStr.split(/[,，、]/).forEach(function(k){ k = k.trim(); if(k && keywordList.indexOf(k) < 0) keywordList.push(k); }); }var wbName = getWorldbookName(); var createFn = getApi('createWorldbookEntries'); var getWb = getApi('getWorldbook'); var delFn = getApi('deleteWorldbookEntries');if(!createFn) throw new Error('API不可用');var prefix = '[' + selectedEra + 'NPC]';
            if(getWb){ var entries = await getWb(wbName); var existing = entries.filter(function(e){ return e.name === prefix + npcName || e.name === prefix + npcName + '_data'; }); if(existing.length > 0){ if(!confirm('已存在「' + npcName + '」，是否覆盖？')){ btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '💾 保存NPC'; _aiLock = false; return; } if(delFn){ await delFn(wbName,function(entry){ return entry.name === prefix + npcName || entry.name === prefix + npcName + '_data'; }); } } }
            await createFn(wbName, [{ name: prefix + npcName, content: ec, enabled: true, strategy: { type: 'selective', keys: keywordList }, position: { type: 'before_character_definition', order: 25 }, recursive: true }]);
            await createFn(wbName, [{ name: prefix + npcName + '_data', content: JSON.stringify(fd), enabled: false, strategy: { type: 'selective', keys: ['NEVER_TRIGGER_NPC_DATA_' + npcName] }, position: { type: 'before_character_definition', order: 26 } }]);
            clearDraft(); alert('✅ NPC「' + npcName + '」已保存！');createMode = 'user'; showPane('home'); await loadProfiles();
        } catch(e) { toast('t-npc-save', '保存失败：' + (e.message || e), 'err'); }btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '💾 保存NPC'; _aiLock = false;
    }

    /* ===== 保存世界观元素 ===== */
    async function saveWorldElement(){
        if(_aiLock) return; _aiLock = true;clearToasts();var data = collectWorldData();var saveName = (document.getElementById('f-world-save-name')||{}).value||'';saveName = saveName.trim();if(!saveName){ if(data.name) saveName = data.name; else { _aiLock = false; return toast('t-world-save','请输入名称','err'); }}var btn = document.getElementById('btnSaveWorld');btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = '保存中...';
        try{var ec = buildWorldExportText(data);var keywordsStr = (document.getElementById('f-world-keywords')||{}).value||'';var keywordList = [saveName];if(keywordsStr){keywordsStr.split(/[,，、]/).forEach(function(k){k = k.trim();if(k && keywordList.indexOf(k) < 0) keywordList.push(k);});}var wbName = getWorldbookName();var createFn = getApi('createWorldbookEntries');var getWb = getApi('getWorldbook');var delFn = getApi('deleteWorldbookEntries');if(!createFn) throw new Error('API不可用');var wtypeLabel = {'faction':'势力','academy':'学院','location':'地点'};var prefix = '[' + data.era + (wtypeLabel[data.wtype]||'世界观') + ']';
            if(getWb){var entries = await getWb(wbName);var existing = entries.filter(function(e){ return e.name === prefix + saveName || e.name === prefix + saveName + '_data'; });if(existing.length > 0){if(!confirm('已存在「' + saveName + '」，是否覆盖？')){btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '🌍 保存世界观元素'; _aiLock = false; return;}if(delFn){await delFn(wbName, function(entry){return entry.name === prefix + saveName || entry.name === prefix + saveName + '_data';});}}}
            await createFn(wbName, [{name: prefix + saveName,content: ec,enabled: true,strategy: { type: 'selective', keys: keywordList },position: { type: 'before_character_definition', order: 15 },recursive: true}]);
            await createFn(wbName, [{name: prefix + saveName + '_data',content: JSON.stringify(data),enabled: false,strategy: { type: 'selective', keys: ['NEVER_TRIGGER_WORLD_DATA_' + saveName] },position: { type: 'before_character_definition', order: 16 }}]);
            alert('✅ 世界观元素「' + saveName + '」已保存！');createMode = 'user';showPane('home');await loadProfiles();
        }catch(e){toast('t-world-save', '保存失败：' + (e.message||e), 'err');}btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = '🌍 保存世界观元素';_aiLock = false;
    }

    /* ===== 降临（startGame） ===== */
    async function startGame() {if(_aiLock)return; _aiLock=true;clearToasts();var profileName=document.getElementById('f-profile-name').value.trim();if(!profileName){_aiLock=false;return toast('t-intro','请输入档案名称','err');}
        var fullIntro = (document.getElementById('f-full-intro')||{}).value||'';
        var outlineIntro = document.getElementById('f-intro').value.trim();
        var direction = document.getElementById('f-intro-direction').value.trim();
        var intro = fullIntro.trim() || outlineIntro || direction;
        if(!intro){_aiLock=false;return toast('t-intro','请填写开场白','err');}
        var btn=document.getElementById('btnGo'); btn.disabled=true; btn.textContent='降临中...';try{var fd=collectFormData();fd._savedAt=Date.now();var ec=buildExportText(fd);var wbName=getWorldbookName();var getWb=getApi('getWorldbook'); var createFn=getApi('createWorldbookEntries'); var delFn=getApi('deleteWorldbookEntries');if(!createFn)throw new Error('API不可用');var prefix=getProfilePrefix();
        if(getWb){var entries=await getWb(wbName);if(delFn){var old=entries.filter(function(e){return e.name==='user档案'});if(old.length>0){await delFn(wbName,function(entry){return entry.name==='user档案';});}}}
        await createFn(wbName,[{name:"user档案",content:ec,enabled:true,strategy:{type:'constant'},position:{type:'before_character_definition',order:20},recursive:true}]);
        await createFn(wbName,[{name:prefix+profileName,content:JSON.stringify(fd),enabled:false,strategy:{type:'selective',keys:['NEVER_TRIGGER_THIS_PROFILE_ENTRY']},position:{type:'before_character_definition',order:30}}]);clearDraft();var _dispName=getDisplayName(fd);
        var sendCore = '[角色设定] ' + _dispName + '，' + fd.era + '，' + fd.level + '级' + rankName(parseFloat(fd.level)) + '，降临方式：' + fd.arrivalType;
        if(fd.soul1Name) sendCore += '，武魂：' + fd.soul1Name;
        sendCore += ' [开场白] ' + (intro.length > 500 ? intro.substring(0, 500) + '...' : intro);
        _descendSendText = sendCore.replace(/\|/g,'｜').replace(/\{\{/g,'{ {').replace(/\}\}/g,'} }').replace(/\n+/g, ' ').replace(/[<>]/g, function(c) { return c === '<' ? '＜' : '＞'; });
        showDescendAnim(_dispName, fd.chapter, fd.location||fd.locCustom||'');btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='确认降临'; _aiLock=false;}catch(error){toast('t-intro','降临失败：'+(error.message||error),'err');btn.disabled=false; btn.textContent='确认降临'; _aiLock=false;}}

    /* ===== 动画函数 ===== */
    function showWelcome(){if(_welcomeShown)return;if(sessionStorage.getItem('dlgd_welcome_shown'))return;_welcomeShown=true;sessionStorage.setItem('dlgd_welcome_shown','1');var overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='welcome-overlay';overlay.id='welcomeOverlay';var particles='';for(var i=1;i<=8;i++){particles+='<div class="welcome-particle"></div>';}overlay.innerHTML='<div class="welcome-core"><div class="welcome-orb"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-ring-expand"></div><div class="welcome-particles">'+particles+'</div></div><div class="welcome-text"><div class="welcome-title">斗罗大陆</div><div class="welcome-sub">· 降 临 ·</div></div><div class="welcome-skip">点击任意处进入</div>';overlay.onclick=function(){hideWelcome();};document.body.appendChild(overlay);}
    function hideWelcome(){var el=document.getElementById('welcomeOverlay');if(!el)return;if(el.classList.contains('fade-out'))return;el.classList.add('fade-out');setTimeout(function(){if(el.parentNode)el.remove();},500);}
    function showAwakenAnim(){var overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='awaken-overlay';overlay.id='awakenOverlay';var particles='';for(var i=1;i<=12;i++){particles+='<div class="awaken-particle"></div>';}overlay.innerHTML='<div class="awaken-core"><div class="awaken-ripple"></div><div class="awaken-ripple"></div><div class="awaken-ripple"></div><div class="awaken-ripple"></div><div class="awaken-ring-outer"></div><div class="awaken-ring-inner"></div><div class="awaken-orb"></div><div class="awaken-particles">'+particles+'</div><div class="awaken-pillar"></div></div><div class="awaken-text">武 魂 觉 醒 中</div>';document.body.appendChild(overlay);}
    function hideAwakenAnim(){var el=document.getElementById('awakenOverlay');if(el)el.remove();}

    function showDescendAnim(charName, chapter, location) {
        _descendAnimDone = false;var overlay = document.createElement('div');overlay.className = 'descend-overlay';overlay.id = 'descendOverlay';var starsHtml = '';for (var si = 0; si < 40; si++) {var sx = Math.random() * 100; var sy = Math.random() * 100; var ss = 1 + Math.random() * 2; var sd2 = 2 + Math.random() * 4; var sdelay = Math.random() * 3;starsHtml += '<div class="descend-star" style="left:' + sx + '%;top:' + sy + '%;width:' + ss + 'px;height:' + ss + 'px;animation:starTwinkle ' + sd2 + 's ease-in-out '+ sdelay + 's infinite"></div>';}var rainHtml = '';for (var ri = 0; ri < 30; ri++) {var rl = 42 + Math.random() * 16; var rdelay = Math.random() * 3; var rdur = 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.5;rainHtml += '<div class="descend-rain-p" style="left:' + rl + '%;animation:rainFall ' + rdur + 's linear ' + rdelay + 's infinite"></div>';}var dustHtml = '';for (var di = 0; di < 16; di++) {var angle = (di / 16) * Math.PI * 2; var dist = 60 + Math.random() * 80; var dx = Math.cos(angle) * dist; var dy = Math.sin(angle) * dist * 0.5; var ddelay = 1.8 + Math.random() * 0.3; var ddur = 1.5 + Math.random() * 1;dustHtml += '<div class="descend-dust-p" style="--dx:' + dx + 'px;--dy:' + dy + 'px;animation:dustDrift ' + ddur + 's ease-out ' + ddelay + 's both"></div>';}var detail = '降临 · ' + (chapter || '') + ' · ' + (location || '');overlay.innerHTML ='<div class="descend-stars">' + starsHtml + '</div><div class="descend-rain">' + rainHtml + '</div><div class="descend-beam" id="descendBeam"></div><div class="descend-flash" id="descendFlash"></div><div class="descend-shockwave sw1" id="descendSw1"></div><div class="descend-shockwave sw2" id="descendSw2"></div><div class="descend-dust">' + dustHtml + '</div><div class="descend-center"><div class="descend-name" id="descendName">' + escHtml(charName || '降临者') + '</div><div class="descend-detail" id="descendDetail">' + escHtml(detail) + '</div></div><div class="descend-hint" id="descendHint">点 击 进 入 斗 罗 大 陆</div>';overlay.onclick = function() { tryFinishDescend(); };document.body.appendChild(overlay);setTimeout(function() { var beam = document.getElementById('descendBeam'); if (beam) beam.classList.add('active'); }, 500);setTimeout(function() { var flash = document.getElementById('descendFlash'); if (flash) flash.classList.add('fire'); var sw1 = document.getElementById('descendSw1'); var sw2 = document.getElementById('descendSw2'); if (sw1) sw1.classList.add('fire'); if (sw2) sw2.classList.add('fire'); var ol = document.getElementById('descendOverlay'); if (ol) ol.classList.add('descend-shake'); setTimeout(function() { if (ol) ol.classList.remove('descend-shake'); }, 600); }, 1700);setTimeout(function() { var beam = document.getElementById('descendBeam'); if (beam) { beam.classList.remove('active'); beam.classList.add('breathe'); } }, 2500);setTimeout(function() { var name = document.getElementById('descendName'); if (name) name.classList.add('show'); }, 3500);setTimeout(function() { var detail2 = document.getElementById('descendDetail'); if (detail2) detail2.classList.add('show'); }, 3900);setTimeout(function() { _descendAnimDone = true; var hint = document.getElementById('descendHint'); if (hint) hint.classList.add('show'); }, 5500);
    }
    function tryFinishDescend() {if (!_descendAnimDone) return;hideDescendAnim();try {var slashFn = getApi('triggerSlash');if (slashFn && _descendSendText) {slashFn('/send ' + _descendSendText + '|/trigger');}} catch (e) {}}
    function hideDescendAnim() {var el = document.getElementById('descendOverlay');if (!el) return;if (el.classList.contains('fade-out')) return;el.classList.add('fade-out');setTimeout(function() { if (el.parentNode) el.remove(); }, 600);}

    /* ===== 全局事件 ===== */
    document.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(!e.target.closest('.custom-select'))closeAllDropdowns();if(!e.target.closest('.ring-orb')&&!e.target.closest('.color-picker-bar')&&!e.target.closest('.color-pick-orb')){document.querySelectorAll('.color-picker-bar.open').forEach(function(p){p.classList.remove('open')});document.querySelectorAll('.ring-orb').forEach(function(o){o.style.outline='none'});activeRingOrb=null;}if(!e.target.closest('.model-search-wrap')&&!e.target.closest('[onclick*="fetchModels"]')){closeModelList();}});

    document.getElementById('importFileInput').addEventListener('change', importProfile);
    document.getElementById('exportOverlay').addEventListener('click', closeExportPanel);
    document.getElementById('pickerOverlay').addEventListener('click', closePicker);

    var _autoSaveTimer = null;
    function startAutoSave() {if (_autoSaveTimer) return;_autoSaveTimer = setInterval(function() {if (curPaneId !== 'home' && createMode !== 'world') { saveDraft(); }}, 30000);}
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function() {if (curPaneId !== 'home' && createMode !== 'world') { saveDraft(); }});

    /* ===== 初始化 ===== */
    async function initPage() {
        clearInterval(_autoSaveTimer); _autoSaveTimer = null;
        buildAttrTags('f-soul1-attrs');buildAttrTags('f-soul2-attrs');buildAttrTags('f-soul3-attrs');buildBeastAttrTags();buildGenderTags();buildNsfwBodyFields();updateHumanFormTags();buildNsfwBeastHumanFields();buildOutfitTags();buildNsfwOutfitFields();buildInvOutfitSelectTags();buildBeastOutfitTags();buildNsfwBeastOutfitFields();buildBeastPersonalityContent();buildNsfwPersonalityTags();buildSpeechTags();
        buildBoneGrid();buildBoneGenTags();
        loadApiConfigSilent();updatePanelVisibility();refreshSteps();await loadProfiles();checkDraft();updStepUI();showWelcome();startAutoSave();
    }

    if (typeof $ === 'function') {$(function() { initPage(); });} else {window.onload = initPage;}
    </script>
</body>
</html>
